Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是：(1) 实证表明帧选择策略的相对有效性随语义类别和基准变化，为自适应证据获取提供动机；(2) 提出 AutoSkill，用 LLM 智能体自动发现可执行帧选择技能，并通过纯文本的语义分类体系完成源→目标的路由迁移；(3) 在五个长视频基准上验证其相较统一策略的增益。局限：方法依赖带标注的源池与 LLM 智能体的技能生成质量，路由映射基于文本诱导的类别估计，可能受域差距影响；提升幅度在部分模型上相对有限，且未涉及自监督或事件相机等更广义的时序感知场景。

Abstract: Long-Video Question Answering (LVQA) requires locating decisive evidence in hour-scale videos under a limited frame budget. Most training-free methods apply the same frame-selection strategy to all questions, despite substantial variation in the evidence required by different question types. Our analysis shows that the relative effectiveness of frame-selection strategies varies across semantic categories and benchmarks, motivating adaptive evidence acquisition. In this paper, we introduce AutoSkill, a source-supervised framework for automatically discovering and routing executable frame-selection skills. Starting from a small labelled source pool, LLM agents iteratively propose, implement, evaluate, and refine candidate skills. For a target benchmark, AutoSkill uses only unlabelled question and option text to induce a shared semantic taxonomy, rewrite labelled source examples into the target style, and estimate a category-to-skill mapping. Neither target videos nor target answers are used in this process. At inference time, each question is assigned one skill, which selects the frames used in a single inference of the frozen video MLLM. Across five long-video benchmark splits, AutoSkill improves Qwen2.5-VL-7B and Qwen3.5-4B by 2.4% and 1.2%, respectively, demonstrating the effectiveness of our AutoSkill.

</details>


### [29] [Aligned Radiometric RGB-Thermal Fusion for UAV Facade Anomaly Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12521)
*Yuan Yang,Shulei Li,Haobo Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一套面向无人机外立面巡检的传感器级RGB-热红外配准与编码流程，并将16位辐射热测量的"符号化局部对比度"编码为第四通道输入轻量单流检测器。作者同时发布M3T数据集（674对已配准的RGB与热辐射样本，涵盖8类构件与异常）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机外立面巡检结合RGB与热成像可同时筛查表层及亚表层异常，但存在两类核心障碍：(1) 两个传感器之间的几何错位以及热图像的渲染过程破坏空间对应关系；(2) 异常（如脱粘/分层）往往只表现为微弱的温度对比，直接使用原始热图难以被网络利用。因此需要从传感器层面解决对齐与热对比度表征问题，而非仅依赖网络端的多模态融合。

Method: 提出一条传感器级pipeline：逐传感器校正→RGB-to-thermal配准→公共支撑区域裁剪→对16位辐射测量做符号局部对比度编码（signed local contrast encoding），该编码保留"局部更热/更冷"的方向性区分，并作为紧凑单流（single-stream）检测器的第四输入通道（RGB三通道+该对比度通道）。配套构建M3T数据集，含5个外立面巡检项目、8类构件与异常类别、674对RGB与辐射热样本；并做受控位移分析以刻画局部对比度响应随位移的变化。

Result: 配准残差中位误差为3.384像素。按项目分组做四折评估，在IoU 0.5–0.95上得到mAP 0.168，单图计算量为28.50 GFLOPs。单划分消融显示：相比RGB-only及替代热输入方式，分层（delamination）检测有所改善，但整体精度未超过RGB单独输入。在RGBT-Tiny数据集上使用渲染热图进行评估，结果表现不一（mixed）。

Conclusion: 贡献在于：(1) 将配准、共支撑裁剪与符号局部对比度编码整合为可复现的传感器级流程；(2) 发布M3T配对RGB-辐射热数据集并提供配准精度与受控位移分析；(3) 以轻量单流检测器验证对齐后的辐射对比度对特定异常类别（如分层）的增益。局限：整体精度并未优于RGB-only，收益呈类别特定性；评估依赖小幅单划分消融而非大规模验证；跨数据集（渲染热图）泛化性不稳定；28.50 GFLOPs对无人机边缘部署仍属较高开销。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle facade inspection can combine red, green, and blue (RGB) imagery with thermal measurements to screen surface and subsurface anomalies. However, geometric discrepancies between the sensors and thermal image rendering can obscure spatial correspondence and weak temperature contrasts. This article presents a sensor-level pipeline comprising per-sensor correction, RGB-to-thermal registration, common-support cropping, and signed local contrast encoding of 16-bit radiometric measurements. The encoding preserves the distinction between locally hotter and colder regions and supplies the fourth input channel of a compact single-stream detector. We introduce M3T, a dataset of 674 paired RGB and radiometric thermal samples from five facade-inspection projects covering eight component and anomaly categories. The median residual registration error is 3.384 pixels, and a controlled-displacement analysis characterizes how the local contrast response changes under controlled displacement. Project-grouped four-fold evaluation yields mean average precision of 0.168 over intersection-over-union thresholds from 0.5 to 0.95, using 28.50 billion floating-point operations per image. A separate single-split ablation shows improved delamination detection over RGB-only and alternative thermal inputs, although aggregate accuracy does not improve over RGB alone. Evaluation on RGBT-Tiny shows mixed performance with rendered thermal imagery. These results characterize the category-specific benefits and limitations of aligned radiometric contrast for compact facade screening.

</details>


### [30] [Earth-Agent-Pro: Towards Real-World Full-Chain Earth Observation with Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12533)
*Zhutao Lv,Chenhao Dang,Yi Feng,Yanpei Gong,Xiaolei Wang,Junyan Ye,Conghui He,Weijia Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Earth-Agent-Pro，一个执行自适应（execution-adaptive）的 Plan-and-Execute 框架，利用专家编写的技能（skills）约束规划与运行时工具调用，并通过以工作流为中心的结构化记忆支持"后缀修复"，实现全链条开放世界地球观测（EO）任务的自主执行，同时发布 Earth-Bench-Pro 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的 EO 智能体需要把高层科学问题转化为可执行工作流：获取观测、准备数据、执行领域计算、并从运行时证据中得出结论。现有 EO 智能体通常从"已给定的观测"出发，而基准测试多提供准备好的输入或候选答案，导致"全链条开放世界 EO 执行"几乎未被检验。因此缺少能够评测"从高层请求到运行时数据获取、执行轨迹与开放答案"全流程的能力与配套基准，本文正是针对这一空白。

核心痛点是：EO 任务具有强环境依赖性（数据是否可得、格式是否匹配）和步骤间依赖关系，一旦某步运行时证据失效，传统链式 agent 往往整体重规划，成本高且易破坏已确认的证据。

Method: 提出 Earth-Agent-Pro，一个 execution-adaptive 的 Plan-and-Execute 框架，主要技术贡献包括：

1. **专家技能约束（expert-authored skills）**：由领域专家编写的技能库同时约束规划阶段的 workflow 组合与运行时工具使用，把开放动作空间限定在可执行的 EO 操作上。

2. **工作流中心结构化记忆（workflow-centered structured memory）**：记录计划步骤、已接受证据及其依赖关系。当运行时证据使某一步失效时，只对受影响的工作流"后缀（suffix）"进行修复，而非整体重规划——这是执行自适应性的关键机制。

3. **分离式 LLM 适配器（separate adapters）**，分别针对两个角色：
   - **Planner**：用序列级监督微调（sequence-level SFT）学习工作流组合；
   - **Executor**：用节点级 GRPO（group relative policy optimization）配合局部可验证奖励（locally verifiable rewards）实现工具参数落地（tool-argument grounding）。

框架同时覆盖 RGB 影像、光谱观测与遥感产品等多模态 EO 数据源。

Result: 发布 Earth-Bench-Pro：将 248 个专家精心构造的任务核心实例化为 744 个问题，覆盖三种匹配的评测机制（regimes）。其中 248 个 Open-World Execution 问题涵盖 RGB 影像、光谱观测与遥感产品，把高层请求与运行时数据需求、可执行轨迹、以及基于执行证据的开放式答案配对。

关键结果：
- 在共享 GPT-5 骨干下，Earth-Agent-Pro 取得 66.13% 的 LLM-as-Judge 准确率，比 ReAct 在该指标上高 20.95 个点，在 Tools-In-Order 指标上高 24.44 个点。
- 联合适配器微调（joint adapter tuning）将 Qwen3.5-9B 的 LLM-as-Judge 准确率从 38.31% 提升至 50.00%，相对未调优配置提升 11.69 个点。
- 消融/分解评测：仅规划（Planning-only）评测与使用参考工作流执行（execution with the reference workflow）分别证明适配器改善了工作流组合与参数落地能力。

代码与数据集将公开。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：(1) 提出执行自适应的 Plan-and-Execute EO 智能体框架，用专家技能约束规划与工具使用，并以结构化记忆实现依赖感知的工作流后缀修复；(2) 分离式 Planner/Executor 适配器，分别以序列级 SFT 与节点级 GRPO + 局部可验证奖励对齐；(3) 构建 Earth-Bench-Pro，首次系统评测全链条开放世界 EO 执行。

意义在于把 EO agent 评测从"给定观测+候选答案"推进到"高层请求→运行时数据获取→执行轨迹→开放答案"的完整链路，对遥感与地理空间自主智能体研究具有基准价值。

可能局限：依赖少量专家编写技能，技能库的覆盖度与可扩展性未充分讨论；执行过程依赖 LLM-as-Judge 与局部可验证奖励，评测的可靠性受评判模型影响；抽象中未给出推理成本、执行时延或工具调用失败率的定量分析，也未见对 744 题中不同模态（RGB/光谱/遥感产品）的分项性能报告。

Abstract: Real-world Earth observation (EO) agents must translate high-level scientific questions into executable workflows to acquire observations, prepare data, perform domain computations, and derive conclusions from runtime evidence. Existing EO agents typically start from supplied observations, while benchmarks typically provide prepared inputs or candidate answers, leaving full-chain open-world EO execution largely untested. We present Earth-Agent-Pro, an execution-adaptive Plan-and-Execute framework using expert-authored skills to constrain planning and runtime tool use. Workflow-centered structured memory records planned steps, accepted evidence, and their dependencies, enabling repair of only the affected workflow suffix when runtime evidence invalidates a step. Separate large language model adapters use sequence-level supervised fine-tuning for planner workflow composition and node-level group relative policy optimization with locally verifiable rewards for executor tool-argument grounding. Earth-Bench-Pro instantiates 248 expert-curated task cores as 744 questions under three matched regimes. Its 248 Open-World Execution questions span RGB imagery, spectral observations, and remote sensing products, pairing high-level requests with runtime data requirements, executable trajectories, and open-ended answers grounded in execution evidence. With a shared GPT-5 backbone, Earth-Agent-Pro achieves 66.13% LLM-as-Judge accuracy, exceeding ReAct by 20.95 points in this metric and 24.44 points in Tools-In-Order. Joint adapter tuning raises Qwen3.5-9B LLM-as-Judge accuracy from 38.31% to 50.00%, an 11.69-point gain over the untuned configuration. Planning-only evaluation and execution with the reference workflow show that the adapters improve workflow composition and argument grounding, respectively. Code and datasets will be released soon.

</details>


### [31] [RelateAnything: Real-Time Open-Vocabulary Relation Prediction From Any Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12552)
*Maëlic Neau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 RelateAnything，一个 53M 参数的开放词表场景图关系预测模型：输入图像与任意来源的区域，输出基于推理时以字符串形式提供的谓词词表所打分的 scored relations。模型 20 ms/帧运行，并配套构建了 RA-4M 关系语料与 OV-SGG-Bench 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词表检测与可提示分割已将类别体系从模型内部移到推理输入，但关系预测尚未如此。现有场景图模型仍被绑定在某一标注风格的 50/56 个谓词上，关系头以物体标签为条件，因而与特定检测器耦合。作者指出三大障碍（均非建模层面）：缺乏既自由文本又经过验证的关系语料；标签条件化架构无法接受未训练过的词表；标准指标奖励与训练语料的一致性，导致扩大词表反而被算作性能退化。

Method: 模型以图像与任意来源区域为输入，输出在推理时给定谓词词表上的评分关系；物体标签从不作为输入，因此区域来源可替换而无需重训练。词表实现为文本嵌入库（bank of text embeddings）而非学习到的分类器。针对 19,103 个谓词的训练采用正-未标注（positive-unlabeled）监督，并需要能区分反义词的文本编码器——作者指出对比式编码器会将反义词嵌入到余弦相似度约 0.95 的程度。为提供监督信号，构建 RA-4M 数据集：474k 图像、4.3M 关系、覆盖 10,102 个自由文本谓词，通过带编号的 box marker 生成并做几何验证。为评测构建 OV-SGG-Bench，包含六个评测轴，覆盖标准 recall 指标所偏好的先验无法满足的场景。

Result: 在三个跨数据集基准以及第四个零样本设定上，RelateAnything 的平均召回率是最强同规模开放词表方法的 2.3–3.5 倍；该优势在换用真实检测器后依然存在；在两项指标上均超过某 3B-VLM 场景图模型，而参数量不足其 2%。作者还发现域内测量会将迁移增益高估约 5 倍。作者指出标准 recall 指标偏向训练语料一致性，因此 Six-axis OV-SGG-Bench 被用来更全面评测。模型、语料与基准均已公开。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于将关系预测的类别体系开放化：标签无关、词表以推理时字符串/文本嵌入形式注入，配合 RA-4M 语料与 OV-SGG-Bench 基准，在跨数据集与零样本迁移上取得显著提升，且参数量与推理开销（20 ms/帧）很低，适合边缘/实时部署。局限方面：依赖能区分反义词的文本编码器（对比编码器不足）、训练需正-未标注监督、语料生成依赖几何验证流程，且域内评测会显著高估迁移性能。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary detection accepts any class list at inference, and promptable segmentation returns regions without class names: the taxonomy has left the model and become an input. Relation prediction has not. Scene-graph models are still trained and evaluated on the 50 or 56 predicates of one annotation style, their relation head conditioned on object labels and so tied to one detector. Three obstacles explain this, none primarily modelling: no relation corpus is both free-text and verified, a label-conditioned architecture cannot accept a vocabulary it was not trained on, and the standard metric rewards agreement with the training corpus, so a larger vocabulary scores as a regression. We present RelateAnything, a 53M-parameter model taking an image and regions from any source and returning scored relations over a predicate vocabulary supplied at inference as strings. Object labels are never an input, so the region source can change without retraining, and the vocabulary is a bank of text embeddings, not a learned classifier. It runs at 20 ms/frame. Training over 19,103 predicates requires positive-unlabeled supervision and a text encoder that separates antonyms, which contrastive encoders embed at cosine 0.95. To supply the supervision we build RA-4M, 474k images and 4.3M relations over 10,102 free-text predicates, generated against numbered box markers and geometrically verified. To measure it we build OV-SGG-Bench, six axes scored across datasets that the priors standard recall rewards cannot satisfy. On three cross-dataset benchmarks and a fourth zero-shot, RelateAnything has 2.3-3.5x the mean recall of the strongest open-vocabulary method of comparable scale, margins that survive a real detector, and leads a 3B-VLM scene-graph model on both metrics at under 2% of its parameters. In-domain measurement overstates transfer gains ~5x. Model, corpus and benchmark are public.

</details>


### [32] [DRS-VPT: Directly Relocalizing in a Scan with Vision Point Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12557)
*Lanke Frank Tarimo Fu,Maurice Fallon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DRS-VPT 是一个前馈式 Transformer 基础模型，用于图像到扫描（image-to-scan）的配准：输入查询图像与参考 3D 点云，在第一个相机坐标系下同时预测扫描位姿与点图（point map）、各相机位姿与点图，并输出由粗到细的逐点/逐像素特征金字塔，实现扫描到首帧图像的直接重投影对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机与 LiDAR 的跨模态配准（如自动驾驶中的相机-LiDAR 标定、室内相机-地图重定位）通常依赖分任务设计的专用流水线，难以统一处理，且室内重定位方法往往需要针对特定地图训练权重，泛化到未见环境能力有限。论文的动机是构建一个统一的基础模型式公式，把不同配准任务纳入同一"图像-扫描配准"框架，并借助大规模预训练范式获得强零样本迁移能力。

Method: DRS-VPT 采用前馈 Transformer 架构，将查询图像与参考 3D 点云作为输入，输出统一表达在首个相机坐标系下的：扫描位姿与点图、每台相机的位姿与点图。其关键创新在于预测一个由粗到细（coarse-to-fine）的金字塔式逐点（per-point）与逐像素（per-pixel）特征，用于扫描到第一帧图像的直接重投影对齐（reprojective alignment），从而在特征层面建立跨模态对应关系，而非依赖显式的迭代优化。该公式统一了相机-LiDAR 标定与室内相机-地图重定位等下游任务。论文仅给出摘要，未披露具体的 tokenization、几何编码或损失函数细节。

Result: 在自动驾驶的图像-to-LiDAR 配准任务上，单一 DRS-VPT 模型取得 state-of-the-art 性能；在室内重定位任务上取得有竞争力的结果，且无需针对特定地图训练权重；同时展现出对未见环境的强零样本迁移能力。定性分析表明模型学到了复杂的扫描-图像投影性质，例如背面点（back-facing points）的遮挡处理。摘要未给出具体数据集名称、评价指标与数值结果。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是提出统一的前馈图像-扫描配准基础模型 DRS-VPT，通过联合预测位姿、点图与由粗到细的对应特征金字塔，将相机-LiDAR 标定与室内重定位统一到一个框架，并在跨任务、跨环境（零样本）设置下验证了有效性，且定性揭示了模型对遮挡等几何投影性质的学习能力。局限在于：室内重定位仅达到"有竞争力"而非最优；该方向依赖大规模跨模态预训练数据；摘要未报告定量指标、计算开销或实时性，实际部署效率与泛化边界仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: We present DRS-VPT, a feed-forward transformer architecture for foundational image-to-scan registration. Given query images and a reference 3D point cloud, the model predicts the scan pose and point map alongside the poses and point maps of each camera, all expressed in the first camera's frame. It additionally predicts a coarse-to- fine pyramid of per-point and per-pixel features for direct reprojective alignment of the scan to the first image. This formulation unifies downstream tasks such as camera-LiDAR calibration in autonomous driving and indoor camera-to-map relocalization. A single DRS-VPT model achieves state-of-the-art performance for image-to-LiDAR registration in autonomous driving, competitive indoor relocalization without training map-specific weights, and strong zero-shot transfer to unseen environments. We also show qualitatively that the model learns complex scan-to-image projection properties such as occlusion of back-facing points.

</details>


### [33] [KAD-Net: Kinematics-Aware Decoupled Learning for Robust 3D Hand Pose Estimation from a Single Depth Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12559)
*Jun Lu,Zhenming Chen,Lin Chen,Kanlun Tan,Xiaoling Li,Qiao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KAD-Net 是一个面向单张深度图的 3D 手部姿态估计网络，通过"手指拓扑约束（FTC）"模块建模局部运动学关系以强化远端/遮挡关节的表示，并采用任务解耦的层级多任务框架将 2D 关节定位与深度回归分离，在 ICVL、NYU、MSRA 上取得 SOTA 精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单深度图 3D 手部姿态估计面临两大挑战：(1) 手部运动学结构复杂且存在自遮挡，现有方法难以充分建模关节间的拓扑依赖，尤其是远端关节（指尖等）常被遮挡而定位困难；(2) 传统层级多任务架构强制 2D 关节定位与深度回归共享同一特征空间，导致两个任务的梯度与特征相互干扰（负迁移），削弱了整体精度。该问题对人机交互、VR、手势控制等应用具有实际意义。

Method: 提出 Kinematics-Aware Decoupled Learning Network（KAD-Net），核心包含两部分：(1) Finger Topology Constraint（FTC）模块：利用连续三个手指关节构建局部运动学表示，对关节施加拓扑约束，从而补充远端关节的运动学特征；该模块借助可见关节提供的结构上下文来辅助定位被遮挡的远端关节，提升遮挡鲁棒性。(2) 任务解耦的层级多任务框架：将 2D 关节定位与深度估计分离，并针对深度回归设计专用的多任务学习策略，从而隔离 UV 平面特征与深度特征，缓解任务间的相互干扰与负迁移。

Result: 在 ICVL、NYU、MSRA 三个主流 3D 手部姿态基准数据集上进行大量实验，KAD-Net 的精度优于现有方法，取得 state-of-the-art 的 3D 手部姿态估计结果。论文摘要未给出具体误差指标（如平均关节误差 mm）数值，但明确指出在多个基准上均达到领先精度，验证了拓扑约束与任务解耦设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：提出 FTC 模块以运动学拓扑约束增强远端/遮挡关节表示，以及提出任务解耦层级多任务框架以隔离 2D 定位与深度回归特征、抑制负迁移，在标准基准上达到 SOTA。潜在应用包括人机交互、虚拟现实与手势控制系统。局限方面：摘要仅报告了在三个手部数据集上的精度，未说明模型参数量、推理速度或实时性，也未验证其向事件相机、机器人等更广泛感知场景的迁移能力；具体量化结果与消融细节仍有待正文补充。

Abstract: Due to the complexity of hand kinematics and self-occlusion, existing 3D hand pose estimation methods based on single depth images struggle to comprehensively model the topological dependencies among hand joints. Furthermore, traditional hierarchical multitask architectures enforce a shared feature space for both 2D joint localization and depth estimation, which can induce mutual interference. To address these challenges, we propose a Kinematics-Aware Decoupled Learning Network (KAD-Net) for robust 3D hand pose estimation. Specifically, we first design a Finger Topology Constraint (FTC) module to enhance the representation of distal joints. This module utilizes three consecutive finger joints to construct a local kinematic representation to impose topological constraints, which supplements the kinematic features of the distal joints. The FTC module leverages the structural context from visible joints to assist in locating occluded distal joints, thereby improving robustness to occlusion. Additionally, we propose a task-decoupled hierarchical multitask framework. This framework separates 2D joint localization from depth estimation and incorporates a dedicated multitask learning strategy for depth regression, effectively isolating the UV and depth features to mitigate mutual interference and negative transfer. Extensive experiments demonstrate that KAD-Net outperforms existing methods on several benchmark datasets (ICVL, NYU, and MSRA), achieving state-of-the-art accuracy in 3D hand pose estimation. Potential applications of KAD-Net include human-computer interaction, virtual reality and gesture-based control systems.

</details>


### [34] [An Ultra-Widefield Swept-Source OCTA Dataset and a Polar-Gated Mamba Network for Retinal Vessel Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12574)
*Yang Liu,Yibing Shen,Keming Zhao,Cenk Jiang,Zhenghang Qian,Zhicheng Du,Chen Xiong,Qidong Shao,Zijun Lin,Yunqi Hu,Jingjing Zhou,Lian Zhang,Peter E. Lobie,Peiwu Qin,Chengming Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个公开的超广角扫频源光学相干断层扫描血管成像(UWF SS-OCTA)血管分割数据集WOIVES，并提出PG-Mamba模型，通过引入两种互补的极坐标扫描顺序增强视觉状态空间模型，并结合动态FOV门控模块，实现大范围视网膜血管的精确分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超广角SS-OCTA可实现大面积视网膜血管成像，但在如此大视野下进行血管分割缺乏专用的公开基准数据集和针对定量血管分析的全面评估。同时，常规方向扫描范式在建模大范围血管的复杂拓扑与方向分布时存在局限，难以兼顾分割精度与下游血管量化指标的可靠性，因此亟需专用数据集与适配大视野血管结构的分割方法。

Method: 论文提出WOIVES数据集与PG-Mamba模型。WOIVES包含来自152名参与者的206只眼，视场达24×20 mm²，覆盖从正视到高度近视的屈光范围，并提供软概率血管标注。PG-Mamba基于视觉状态空间模型(Mamba)，在常规方向扫描的基础上引入两种互补的极坐标扫描顺序(polar-coordinate scan orders)，以更好适配大视野血管的radial方向结构；同时在bottleneck处加入辅助的动态视场门控模块(Dynamic FOV Gating)，执行空间调制，增强对不同视场区域特征的适应性。模型输出为软概率血管图，便于用于血管密度等定量分析。

Result: 在WOIVES数据集上采用交叉验证进行评估，PG-Mamba在多项分割指标上优于7种竞争方法。在血管定量分析中，PG-Mamba取得最低的中位绝对误差(median absolute errors)，涉及血管密度(vessel density)、分形维数(fractal dimension)和血管长度密度(vessel length density)三项指标，表明其分割结果在下游血管量化任务上更可靠。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献为：(1) 发布首个公开的UWF SS-OCTA血管分割数据集WOIVES，含软概率标注并覆盖多屈光状态，填补了大视野视网膜血管分割基准的空白；(2) 提出PG-Mamba，通过极坐标扫描顺序与动态FOV门控提升状态空间模型在大视野血管分割中的表现，并验证其在分割指标与血管定量指标上的一致性优势。潜在局限包括数据集规模相对有限(206只眼)、仅针对特定成像模态，以及下游定量评估主要围绕三类血管形态学指标，泛化性和临床适用性仍需更广泛验证。

Abstract: Ultra-widefield (UWF) swept-source optical coherence tomography angiography (SS-OCTA) enables large-area retinal vascular imaging, yet vessel segmentation at this scale lacks dedicated public benchmarks and comprehensive evaluation for quantitative vascular analysis. We introduce WOIVES, to our knowledge the first publicly available UWF SS-OCTA vessel-segmentation dataset, comprising 206 eyes from 152 participants with a 24x20mm^2 field of view. WOIVES spans emmetropia to high myopia and provides soft probability vessel annotations. We further propose PG-Mamba, a visual state space model that enhances conventional directional scans with two complementary polar-coordinate scan orders. An auxiliary Dynamic FOV Gating module performs spatial modulation at the bottleneck. PG-Mamba outperformed seven competitive approaches on broad segmentation metrics under cross-validation. It achieved the lowest median absolute errors for vessel density, fractal dimension, and vessel length density. WOIVES is publicly available on Zenodo (DOI: 10.5281/zenodo.21904672), and the PG-Mamba code is available at https://github.com/syb1234567/PG-Mamba.

</details>


### [35] [SCORE: SubDistribution-aware Collaborative Knowledge Reinforcing for Cloth-Hybrid Lifelong Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12577)
*Kunlun Xu,Liangyu Ma,Jiangmeng Li,Xin Tong,Xiaode Liu,Yufei Guo,Jiahuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 SCORE（SubDistribution-aware COllaborative Knowledge REinforcing）框架，用于 Cloth-Hybrid Lifelong Person Re-Identification（CH-LReID）场景，核心思想是显式建模"身份内多样性"（intra-identity diversity），以持续整合服装一致与服装变化的判别知识，缓解灾难性遗忘，取得 state-of-the-art 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lifelong Person Re-Identification（LReID）目标是在非平稳数据流上训练统一的行人检索模型。现有 LReID 方法大多假设每个行人的衣着保持一致，而更贴近现实、也更具挑战性的 Cloth-Hybrid LReID（CH-LReID）场景中，衣着一致的数据与衣着变化的数据交替出现。由于服装相关（clothing-relevant）知识与服装无关（clothing-irrelevant）知识之间存在冲突，模型在跨阶段学习时旧任务知识被严重破坏，导致众所周知的灾难性遗忘问题被显著放大。因此，如何在统一模型中同时保留并协调这两类相互冲突的判别知识，是该任务的关键难题。

Method: SCORE 的关键思路是显式建模身份内多样性，从而持续巩固互不相同的服装一致与服装变化知识。具体包含两个机制：
（1）Adaptive SubDistribution Modeling（自适应子分布建模）：为每个身份分配一组分布型"子原型"（distributional sub-prototypes），用以刻画同一身份在服装变化下的多模态特征分布，从而提升服装一致知识与服装变化知识之间的兼容性；
（2）Distributional Knowledge Reinforcement（分布型知识强化）：通过协作对齐机制（collaborative aligning mechanism），将旧分布子原型的知识保留到新的子原型中，抑制随任务序列推进而产生的遗忘。
整体上，该方法以"子分布"作为知识载体，用原型级对齐替代传统的单原型或参数级正则，实现新旧知识的协同巩固。

Result: 论文报告在 CH-LReID 这一 Cloth-Hybrid 终生长时场景下进行了大量实验，SCORE 取得了 state-of-the-art（SOTA）性能，说明其在缓解服装相关/无关知识冲突、抵抗灾难性遗忘方面有效。摘要未给出具体数据集名称、评价指标（如 mAP、Rank-1）与数值结果，也未报告消融实验规模与计算开销等细节；代码已开源于 https://github.com/zhoujiahuan1991/ECCV2026-SCORE（对应 ECCV 2026）。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：将 LReID 推进到更现实的 Cloth-Hybrid 场景，并提出用子分布建模刻画身份内多样性、以分布型知识强化实现跨阶段知识协同保留的统一框架 SCORE，在 CH-LReID 上达到 SOTA。其意义在于为"服装变化 + 终生长学习"这一冲突性持续学习问题提供了原型分布层面的解决思路。潜在局限包括：子原型数量与分配策略可能引入超参敏感性；随着任务与身份数量增长，子原型集合的存储与对齐开销可能上升；摘要未披露具体数据集、指标与效率数据，对真实大规模流式部署的可扩展性仍待验证。此外该方法聚焦于 LReID，与事件相机、时序/时空建模、小目标检测、UAV 与机器人感知、SNN 等方向无直接关联。

Abstract: Lifelong Person Re-Identification (LReID) aims to train a unified person retrieval model from a non-stationary data stream. Existing LReID methods mainly focus on scenarios where the clothing of each person is consistent. Recently, the Cloth-Hybrid LReID (CH-LReID) where cloth-consistent and cloth-changing data alternately occur, has emerged as a more practical and challenging scenario. Due to the conflict between clothing-relevant and clothing-irrelevant knowledge, the well-known catastrophic forgetting problem is significantly exacerbated in this task. To address this issue, we propose a SubDistribution-aware COllaborative Knowledge REinforcing (SCORE) framework, where our key idea is explicitly modeling the intra-identity diversity to continually consolidate distinct cloth-consistent and cloth-changing knowledge. Specifically, an Adaptive SubDistribution Modeling mechanism is developed, where a set of distributional subprototypes is assigned to each identity to capture the intra-identity diversity, improving the compatibility between cloth-consistent and cloth-changing knowledge. Then, a Distributional Knowledge Reinforcement scheme is introduced, where the knowledge of old distributional subprototypes is retained in the new ones by a collaborative aligning mechanism. Extensive experiments show that our SCORE achieves the state-of-the-art performance.
  Our code is available at https://github.com/zhoujiahuan1991/ECCV2026-SCORE

</details>


### [36] [RA-SOD: Reliability-Aware RGB-T Salient Object Detection under Modality Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12622)
*Hongbo Gao,Zhengyu Li,Xueru Nie,Dihao Zhu,Lijun Zhao,Yunke Wang,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RA-SOD 提出了一个可靠性感知（reliability-aware）的 RGB-热成像显著目标检测框架，显式建模各模态的可靠性并将其融入特征学习与跨模态融合，以应对真实场景中模态退化的不稳定问题，在四个基准上达到 SOTA 并对严重退化具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB-T 显著目标检测依赖可见光与热成像的互补信息以提升恶劣环境下的鲁棒性。然而真实场景中单模态可靠性高度不稳定：RGB 在低照度、运动模糊和噪声下退化，热成像则常出现对比度压缩与传感器伪影。这些退化会引入不可靠的感知证据，误导跨模态融合并显著降低检测性能。因此需要显式建模模态可靠性以抑制不可靠线索。

Method: RA-SOD 由三个核心组件构成：(1) 可靠性条件表示（reliability-conditioned representation）：自适应补偿退化模态的特征，同时保留结构线索；(2) 不确定性引导的双流细化策略（uncertainty-guided dual-stream refinement）：渐进式修正跨模态表示并抑制不可靠证据；(3) 逐像素模态竞争机制（pixel-wise modality competition）：依据空间可靠性动态选择模态线索，实现细粒度融合。三者协同将可靠性建模贯穿特征学习与融合全过程。

Result: 在四个公开基准（VT821、VT1000、VT5000、VT-IMAG）上进行实验，RA-SOD 取得 state-of-the-art 性能，并在严重模态退化条件下表现出较强鲁棒性。摘要未给出具体量化指标（如 S-measure、F-measure、MAE 数值），仅给出总体结论。代码与模型已开源。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出显式模态可靠性建模的 RGB-T 显著目标检测框架，通过可靠性条件表示、不确定性引导双流细化和逐像素模态竞争实现鲁棒融合，并在多基准上验证了有效性与退化鲁棒性。局限方面：摘要未提供具体定量结果、退化实验协议细节及计算开销信息，其对真实退化类型的泛化能力与轻量化/实时部署潜力尚待进一步验证。

Abstract: RGB-Thermal (RGB-T) salient object detection leverages complementary cues from visible and thermal modalities to improve robustness in challenging environments. However, in real-world scenarios, the reliability of each modality is inherently unstable: RGB images degrade under low illumination, motion blur, and noise, while thermal imagery often suffers from contrast compression and sensor artifacts. Such degradation introduces unreliable perceptual evidence that can mislead cross-modal fusion and significantly deteriorate detection performance. To address this challenge, we propose RA-SOD, a reliability-aware RGB-T salient object detection framework that explicitly models modality reliability and integrates it into feature learning and cross-modal fusion. First, we introduce a reliability-conditioned representation that adaptively compensates degraded modality features while preserving structural cues. Second, an uncertainty-guided dual-stream refinement strategy progressively corrects cross-modal representations while suppressing unreliable evidence. Finally, we propose a pixel-wise modality competition mechanism that dynamically selects modality cues according to spatial reliability for fine-grained fusion. Extensive experiments on four benchmarks (VT821, VT1000, VT5000, and VT-IMAG) demonstrate that RA-SOD achieves state-of-the-art performance and exhibits strong robustness under severe modality degradation. Code and models are available at https://github.com/zaoxienian/RA-SOD.

</details>


### [37] [Beyond Ambiguous Visual Cues: Studying Physiological Disruptions and Cross-Modal Inconsistencies in Deepfake Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12668)
*Chenxi Yang,Yassine Ouzar,Larbi Boubchir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种双向共注意力融合检测器（bidirectional co-attention fusion detector），将 rPPG（远程光电容积脉搏波）信号与面部行为特征联合建模，用于深度伪造检测，并构建了带生理真值的高保真伪造数据集（基于 COHFACE 与 UBFC-rPPG）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来有研究提示 rPPG 信号可作为真实性线索，但存在两个问题：（1）现有基准数据集缺乏生理真值（physiological ground truth），难以定量分析伪造对自然脉搏信号的破坏程度；（2）现有检测器多采用晚期融合（late fusion）或仅使用 rPPG 特征，未充分挖掘面部外观/运动特征与生理动态之间的跨层级（cross-level）依赖关系。作者因此构建高保真伪造样本，研究伪造如何同时扰乱脉搏信号与面部行为。

Method: 作者在真实 rPPG 数据集（COHFACE、UBFC-rPPG）上构造高保真深度伪造操作（face swapping 与 motion transfer），并据此分析伪造对生理信号和面部行为的影响。在此基础上提出双向共注意力融合检测器：将 rPPG 特征与面部行为特征表征为 token，通过双向共注意力机制显式建模脉搏动态与面部运动之间的跨层级依赖，从而学习鲁棒的联合真实性表征，而非简单的晚期融合或单一模态特征。

Result: 采用 subject-disjoint 5-fold 评估协议。在 face swapping 构造数据上取得 92.80% AUC；在 motion transfer 上取得 96.78% AUC，优于仅用 rPPG 的单模态基线和最佳特征级融合方法。此外，在固定两个特征提取器的条件下进行迁移学习，于 Celeb-DF-v2 上达到 91.20% 准确率和 86.08% AUC，显示在目标域适应场景下的适用性。

Conclusion: 主要贡献为：（1）在具有生理真值的真实 rPPG 数据上构建高保真深度伪造基准，用以同时分析生理与面部行为的伪造痕迹；（2）提出双向共注意力融合框架，有效利用 rPPG 与面部行为的跨层级依赖；（3）在自建数据和跨域迁移（Celeb-DF-v2）上验证有效性。潜在局限：数据集基于 COHFACE/UBFC-rPPG 构造，规模与场景多样性有限；跨域结果依赖固定特征提取器，泛化性仍需更广泛验证。

Abstract: Recent deepfake detection studies increasingly suggest remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) signals as an authenticity cue. However, existing benchmarks lack physiological ground truth, and current detectors underexplore the cross-level relationship between facial features and physiological dynamics, often relying on late fusion or rPPG features alone. In this paper, we construct high-fidelity deepfake manipulations on established real rPPG datasets (COHFACE and UBFC-rPPG) to investigate how forgeries disrupt natural physiological signals and facial behavior at the same time. Building on this analysis, we propose a bidirectional co-attention fusion detector that jointly models rPPG and facial behavior tokens. This mechanism explicitly captures the cross-level dependencies between pulse dynamics and facial motion to learn a robust, joint authenticity representation. Extensive experiments using a subject-disjoint 5-fold evaluation demonstrate the superiority of our approach. Achieving a 92.80\% AUC on constructed datasets using face swapping and 96.78\% AUC on motion transfer, our model outperforms both the rPPG-only single modality baseline and the best feature-level fusion methods. Furthermore, transfer-learning result of the fusion detector on Celeb-DF-v2 while keeping both feature extractors fixed achieves 91.20\% accuracy and 86.08\% AUC, which suggests applicability under target-domain adaptation.

</details>


### [38] [Detecting and Explaining Fake News Short Videos with Multimodal Content and Real-World Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12678)
*Yifeng Luo,Yupeng Li,Ming Tang,Jianxiong Guo,Liang Lan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 NVKE-CEI 统一系统用于假新闻视频检测与解释（FNVDE），包含基于视觉与 OCR 文本相似度时序变化的关键帧抽取方法 NVKE，以及融合内容与证据信息的 LLM 事实核查框架 CEI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短视频平台已成为公众重要新闻来源，同时助长假新闻视频传播，因此 FNVDE 任务具有现实意义。现有方法存在两大局限：(1) 常用帧采样策略可能遗漏与真实性相关的线索，或提供的时序上下文不足以理解新闻视频；(2) 已有方法忽视多模态理解或证据检索中的至少一方。这促使作者同时改进关键帧抽取与多模态+证据融合的检测解释流程。

Method: 提出统一系统 NVKE-CEI，含两个组件。NVKE（News Video Keyframes Extraction）：依据视觉相似度与 OCR 文本相似度的联合、按时间顺序的变化来选择关键帧，而非均匀或固定采样，从而保留与真实性判定相关的时序线索。CEI（Content and Evidence Information）：使用两个专门的基于 LLM 的事实核查器，分别进行基于内容的核查与基于证据的核查，其输出由一个轻量级 judge 模型进行融合，得到最终判定并生成以内容为依据的解释（content-grounded explanation）。

Result: 摘要中未给出具体数据集名称、评测指标数值或消融细节。作者称在大量实验（extensive experiments）中，NVKE-CEI 在检测性能上超越当前最优基线，同时能够生成高质量的、以内容为依据的解释。

Conclusion: 主要贡献为：(1) 将关键帧抽取与多模态、证据融合统一到 FNVDE 流程中；(2) 提出基于视觉+OCR 联合相似度时序变化的关键帧抽取方法 NVKE；(3) 提出基于双 LLM 事实核查器与轻量 judge 融合的 CEI 框架，兼顾检测与可解释性。局限在于：摘要未报告具体数据集与量化结果，关键帧选择依赖 OCR 与视觉相似度，对 OCR 质量及 LLM 核查器可靠性较为敏感，且证据检索来源与覆盖范围的影响未在摘要中说明。

Abstract: Short-video platforms have become a primary news source for the public, which has also enabled the widespread dissemination of fake news videos. We study the task of fake news video detection and explanation (FNVDE). Existing methods face two critical limitations. First, commonly used frame selection strategies may omit veracity-relevant cues or provide insufficient temporal context for understanding news videos. Second, prior methods neglect either multimodal understanding or evidence retrieval. To address these limitations, we propose NVKE-CEI, a unified system that integrates a news video keyframes extraction method (NVKE) and an FNVDE framework leveraging both content and evidence information (CEI). NVKE selects keyframes based on chronological changes in combined visual and OCR-text similarity. CEI employs two specialized LLM-based fact checkers (content-based and evidence-based) whose outputs are fused by a lightweight judge model. Extensive experiments show that NVKE-CEI outperforms state-of-the-art baselines while generating high-quality content-grounded explanations.

</details>


### [39] [NOVA-GS: Noise-Aware View-Consistent Gaussian Splatting for Low-Light Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12682)
*Shaurya Pavan A,Vemunuri Divya Madhuri,Yash Pradeep Gawande,Kaushik Mitra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NOVA-GS是一个面向真实低光场景的统一"噪声感知"3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）框架，将曝光增强、盲点自监督去噪与几何优化耦合到单一流程中；其核心创新在于用VGGT前馈估计替代SfM初始化，并引入噪声引导的球谐正则化，从而在无配对监督、无良好光照参考的条件下实现稳健的低光三维重建与新视角合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实低光条件下重建3D场景面临严重传感器噪声、极低信噪比以及退化后光度一致性被破坏的问题，这会直接导致几何估计不稳定、新视角合成质量下降。现有方法主要有两类局限：一是依赖良好光照的参考数据来完成可靠的SfM初始化，否则在退化输入下初始化失败；二是采用逐视角（per-view）增强，虽改善了单帧视觉效果，却引入跨视角不一致性，破坏了多视角几何约束。该问题对夜间/低光机器人视觉、移动端/UAV三维感知与数字内容生成等实际应用具有重要价值。

Method: NOVA-GS是一个统一框架，其关键设计包括：(1) 前端不依赖SfM，而是利用基于VGGT的前馈估计直接从退化低光输入中获取鲁棒的相机位姿与几何初始化；(2) 在此初始化之上集成三个相互耦合的模块——结构感知增强模块（structure-aware enhancement）用于曝光校正，自监督去噪模块（self-supervised denoising）采用盲点掩码（blind-spot masking）构造伪监督信号，以及一致性驱动的高斯泼溅优化（consistency-driven Gaussian Splatting optimization）以强制跨视角几何一致性；(3) 额外引入噪声引导的球谐（spherical harmonic, SH）正则化，用于抑制噪声区域中与视角相关的外观伪影。整体设计使增强、去噪与几何优化在单阶段内联合进行，而非分阶段串行处理。

Result: 论文在多个真实世界低光数据集上进行了大量实验，评价维度包括几何保真度（geometric fidelity）、颜色一致性（color consistency）以及对不同退化程度的鲁棒性。作者报告称，在不依赖配对监督（paired supervision）或良好光照参考的前提下，NOVA-GS相比现有方法取得了更好的几何精度、跨视角颜色一致性与整体鲁棒性。需要说明的是，摘要在所提供的文本范围内未给出具体数据集名称、定量指标数值（如PSNR/SSIM/Chamfer距离）与消融结果，这些细节需以原文与项目主页 https://shaurya2524.github.io/nova-gs/ 为准。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：提出了一个无需SfM初始化、无需配对监督或良好光照参考的统一低光3DGS框架，通过结构感知增强、盲点自监督去噪与一致性驱动优化三者的耦合，以及噪声引导的球谐正则化，缓解了低光下几何不稳与跨视角不一致的问题，并展示了在真实低光数据上的稳健重建能力。其意义在于降低了低光三维重建对高质量采集条件与外部参考数据的依赖，推动了低光/夜间场景下的三维感知可用性。潜在局限包括：摘要未披露具体数据集与定量结果，框架性能对VGGT前馈估计精度的依赖程度、计算开销与实时性、以及在极端噪声或高度动态场景下的泛化能力仍待验证。

Abstract: Reconstructing 3D scenes under real-world low-light conditions remains challenging due to severe sensor noise, low signal-to-noise ratios, and degraded photometric consistency, which destabilize geometry estimation and novel view synthesis. Existing approaches often rely on well-lit reference data for reliable Structure-from-Motion (SfM) initialization under degraded inputs or apply per-view enhancement methods that introduce cross-view inconsistencies. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{NOVA-GS}, a unified noise-aware framework for low-light 3D Gaussian Splatting that subsumes enhancement, denoising, and geometry optimization within a single process. Our method leverages VGGT-based feed-forward estimation to obtain robust camera poses and geometry directly from degraded inputs, eliminating the need for SfM. Building on this initialization, NOVA-GS integrates three coupled components: a structure-aware enhancement module for exposure correction, a self-supervised denoising module with blind-spot masking for pseudo-supervision, and a consistency-driven Gaussian Splatting optimization enforcing cross-view geometric coherence. We further introduce a noise-guided spherical harmonic regularization to suppress view-dependent artifacts in noisy regions. Extensive experiments on diverse real-world low-light datasets demonstrate improved geometric fidelity, color consistency, and robustness without requiring paired supervision or well-lit references. https://shaurya2524.github.io/nova-gs/

</details>


### [40] [Semantically Aligned Gradient-Driven Context-Preserving Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12691)
*Chiranjeev Chiranjeev,Muskan Dosi,Mayank Vatsa,Richa Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: IABEdit 提出一种模型无关的指令引导图像编辑训练框架，将可微分的语义验证嵌入到训练过程中。通过冻结的视觉语言模型（VLM）从真实编辑结果提取空间感知描述子，再由可训练对齐器从生成输出重建这些描述子，两者残差作为梯度信号，指导生成器"改什么"和"改哪里"，且不增加推理时 VLM 开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令引导图像编辑存在"训练时盲区"：生成编辑器从未被要求语义验证其输出是否真正满足指令，监督仅停留在重建损失和输入文本级条件上。这导致三类问题：编辑不完整、空间溢出（spatial spillover）以及定位不佳（poor localization）。该问题重要在于：即使指令对齐看似良好，生成结果在结构一致性和空间精确性上仍可能显著偏离用户意图，尤其在需要保留身份信息的遮挡场景下更为严重。

Method: 核心方法为"梯度对齐的 VLM 蒸馏"：（1）使用冻结 VLM 从 ground-truth 编辑图像中提取空间感知描述子（spatially-aware descriptors）；（2）训练一个可训练对齐器，从生成器输出中重建这些描述子；（3）两者之间的残差作为梯度回传，教导生成器同时学习编辑内容与编辑位置。该框架模型无关，兼容 U-Net（Stable Diffusion）与 MMDiT（FLUX）等不同骨干网络，且不改变其推理管线，无额外推理时 VLM 计算成本。相当于将语义验证从后处理/评估阶段前移至可微分的训练信号。

Result: 在 MagicBrush 数据集上，结构保真度较最佳扩散基线提升 +3.49 DINO-I，较最佳整体基线提升 +1.26，指令对齐保持竞争力。在 RealEdit 和 EMU Edit 基准上，基于嵌入的指标达到 SOTA 指令遵循性能。在 D-LORD 监控基准上，重度遮挡场景下（身份保持最难）超越专有 Gemini agent +5.13 DINO-P。人类评估与 GPT-4o 评估确认编辑感知精确、定位良好。

Conclusion: 贡献在于提出首个将可微分语义验证嵌入训练、实现梯度对齐 VLM 蒸馏的模型无关编辑框架，显著提升结构保真度、指令遵循与遮挡下身份保持，且无推理开销。意义在于证明该蒸馏方式在类真实监控与遮挡条件下依然有效。潜在局限：依赖冻结 VLM 描述子的质量与空间粒度；对齐器训练成本；监控/遮挡场景的提升可能依赖特定基准数据分布。摘要未报告推理速度与低资源场景下的表现。

Abstract: Instruction-guided image editing has a training-time blind spot. Generative editors are never required to semantically verify whether their outputs actually satisfy the instruction. Supervision stops at reconstruction and input textual-level conditioning. This produces incomplete edits, spatial spillover, and poor localization. We present IABEdit, a model-agnostic framework that embeds differentiable semantic verification into training. A frozen vision-language model extracts spatially-aware descriptors from the ground-truth edit. A trainable aligner then reproduces them from the generated output. The residual between the two becomes a gradient that teaches the generator both what to edit and where, with no inference-time VLM cost. IABEdit is compatible with diverse backbones, including U-Net (Stable Diffusion) and MMDiT (FLUX), without altering their inference pipelines. On MagicBrush, it improves structural fidelity by +3.49 DINO-I over the best diffusion baseline and +1.26 over the best overall baseline, while remaining competitive on instruction alignment. It also achieves state-of-the-art instruction adherence performance on RealEdit and EMU Edit benchmarks based on embedding-based metrics. Most consequentially, on the D-LORD surveillance benchmark, it surpasses the proprietary Gemini agent by +5.13 DINO-P under heavy occlusion, where preserving identity is hardest. This shows that gradient-aligned VLM distillation holds up under real-world-like surveillance and occlusion conditions. Human and GPT-4o evaluations confirm perceptually precise, well-localized edits.

</details>


### [41] [ExpertHTR: Unified Handwritten Text Recognition with Multi-Task Learning and Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12705)
*Dang Hoai Nam,Nguyen Duy Hieu,Quang Huu Hieu,Vo Nguyen Le Duy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ExpertHTR 是一个统一的视觉-语言（Vision-Language）框架，面向异构手写文本识别（HTR）数据集，通过"互补监督 + 条件化模型容量"实现跨来源的页面级联合训练。核心包括：基于 Page-Region-Line 统一结构的四任务互补监督，以及带共享分支的稀疏 Mixture-of-Experts（稀疏 MoE）架构（Sparsegen + 路由正则化）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手写文本识别资源普遍规模小、来源分散，不同数据集合在语言、文字系统（script）、文档结构和标注格式上差异显著，导致难以进行页面级（page-level）联合训练。因此需要一个能兼容异构标注、同时充分利用各数据集的互补信息、又能按需分配模型容量以应对异质分布的统一框架。

Method: 方法包含两个层面。（1）互补监督：将异构数据集的结构标注统一组织为通用的 Page-Region-Line 表示，并据此自动构造四个相关训练任务——完整转录（complete transcription）、物理行覆盖（physical-line coverage）、文本定位（text localization）、局部识别（localized recognition），无需额外人工标注。（2）条件化容量：在联合训练的稠密模型基础上引入稀疏 MoE 架构，包含一个始终激活的共享分支（always-active shared branch）与条件路由的全 MLP 专家（conditionally routed full-MLP experts）。其中 Sparsegen 机制允许激活的路由专家数量随隐藏表示动态变化，路由正则化（routing regularization）则缓解路由持续集中于少量专家的问题。

Result: 在七个异构手写文本识别基准上进行实验。结果表明：仅使用页面转录时，互补监督即可稳定提升训练效果；多源联合训练在多数数据集上带来进一步增益；稀疏专家模型在七个来源中的六个上优于稠密基线。最终统一模型在多数基准上显著超越所评估的通用 OCR 与视觉-语言系统，并在 IAM 段落级（paragraph-level）基准上取得 SOTA，但专用 HTR 系统在若干高难度集合上仍保持更强性能。

Conclusion: 主要贡献有三：其一，提出基于 Page-Region-Line 的通用结构表示与四任务互补监督，实现无需额外标注的异构多源联合训练；其二，提出带共享分支与 Sparsegen 动态专家激活的稀疏 MoE 架构及路由正则化，提升异质数据下的容量利用效率；其三，在七个基准上验证统一模型可媲美甚至超越通用 OCR/VLM，并在 IAM 段落级达 SOTA。局限在于：相较专用 HTR 系统，在部分困难数据集上仍有差距；论文未明确说明 Sparsegen 的动态专家数对推理效率与延迟的实际影响。

Abstract: Handwritten text recognition resources are often small and distributed across collections that differ in language, script, document structure, and annotation format, making joint page-level training difficult. We propose ExpertHTR, a unified vision-language framework that addresses this problem through complementary supervision and conditional model capacity. Structural annotations from heterogeneous datasets are first organized through a common Page-Region-Line representation and used to construct four related training tasks for complete transcription, physical-line coverage, text localization, and localized recognition, without requiring additional manual labels. Building on a jointly trained dense model, ExpertHTR introduces a sparse Mixture-of-Experts architecture with an always-active shared branch and conditionally routed full-MLP experts. Sparsegen allows the number of active routed experts to vary with the hidden representation, while routing regularization reduces persistent concentration on a small subset of experts. Experiments on seven heterogeneous handwriting benchmarks show that complementary supervision consistently improves training with page transcription alone, while joint multi-source training provides further gains on most datasets. The proposed sparse expert model further improves the dense baseline on six of the seven sources. The final unified model also substantially outperforms the evaluated general-purpose OCR and vision-language systems on most benchmarks and achieves state-of-the-art performance on the IAM paragraph-level benchmark, while specialized HTR systems remain stronger on several challenging collections.

</details>


### [42] [Multimodal Floorplan Encoding: Learning Dense Modality-Invariant Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12723)
*Xavier Anadón,Rémi Pautrat,Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出多模态平面图编码器（MMFE），将多种2D室内平面图表示（矢量CAD、栅格渲染、传感器密度图）映射到共享的稠密隐空间网格，通过冻结DINOv3骨干+可训练DPT头，并采用逐单元InfoNCE对齐训练，实现跨模态稠密匹配、几何对齐与检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面图存在多种异质形式（矢量CAD图、栅格渲染、传感器衍生密度图），模态间差异大，导致学习系统难以跨模态迁移，也难以支持以几何为中心的任务（如配准与检索）。该问题重要在于：统一的跨模态平面图表示可支撑几何一致性理解、跨模态检索与配准，是室内场景理解与机器人相关任务的基础。

Method: 提出MMFE：共享稠密隐空间网格表示。架构上，冻结的DINOv3骨干提取特征，接可训练的Dense Prediction Transformer（DPT）头进行稠密预测。训练目标为逐单元（per-cell）的InfoNCE对比损失，将跨模态空间对应区域拉近，同时将该单元之外的其它所有单元作为负样本。为增强对几何畸变的鲁棒性，引入受控相似变换（similarity transformations），并通过特征网格warping实现几何一致性约束。

Result: 在Structured3D上的held-out out-of-domain数据集评测显示：MMFE提升了跨模态稠密匹配性能；结合RANSAC可实现鲁棒的相似变换对齐；搭配学习到的聚合（aggregation）方法可获得较强的检索表现。

Conclusion: 主要贡献：提出面向多模态2D平面图的统一稠密表示与编码器MMFE，以逐单元InfoNCE与几何一致性约束实现跨模态对齐，支撑稠密匹配、配准与检索任务。意义：为异质平面图表示学习提供统一框架。可能的局限：摘要未说明具体数据规模与定量指标；对几何畸变的覆盖范围（如视角、尺度）与对非相似变换的鲁棒性尚不明确。

Abstract: Floorplans arise in many forms, from vector CAD drawings to raster renderings and sensor-derived density maps. This heterogeneity makes it difficult to build learning systems that transfer across modalities and support geometry-centric tasks such as alignment and retrieval. We introduce the Multimodal Floorplan Encoder (MMFE), which maps diverse 2D indoor representations into a shared dense latent grid. MMFE combines a frozen DINOv3 backbone with a trainable Dense Prediction Transformer (DPT) head, and is trained with a per-cell Information Noise-Contrastive Estimation (InfoNCE) objective that aligns spatially corresponding regions across modalities while using all other cells as negatives. To improve robustness to geometric distortions, we incorporate controlled similarity transformations and enforce geometric consistency through feature-grid warping. On Structured3D, a held-out out-of-domain dataset, MMFE improves cross-modal dense matching, enables robust similarity alignment with RANSAC, and yields strong retrieval when paired with learned aggregation.

</details>


### [43] [GRACE: Adaptive Concept Erasure with Geometry-Guided Retention in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12731)
*Qinghui Gong,Yihuai Liang,Yuanlun Xie,Deepak Kumar Jain,Vitomir Štruc,Zhengchun Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRACE：一个结构化概念擦除框架，通过语义加权敏感子空间估计与子空间约束适配器实现局部化、可选择的干预，并借助自动安全锚点与能量驱动动态门控，在文本到图像扩散模型中平衡概念擦除效果与生成保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像扩散模型从大规模预训练数据中不可避免地内化了敏感或不合规概念，因此需要后验概念擦除。但现有擦除方法存在两个问题：一是缺乏对参数更新的显式约束，导致过度干预与意外的语义漂移；二是依赖手工构造的反事实监督（如替代提示词），数据构建成本高，难以扩展到新概念。这些问题限制了擦除方法在安全合规场景下的可扩展性与生成质量保持能力。

Method: GRACE 提出结构化的概念擦除框架，核心是局部化与选择性干预。具体包括三个组件：(1) 语义加权敏感子空间估计，用于精确锁定干预方向，将擦除操作限制在敏感子空间内；(2) 轻量级子空间约束适配器，防止全局语义扰动；(3) 自动解耦的安全锚点机制，替代人工提示工程以消除对手工反事实监督的依赖；(4) 能量驱动的动态门控机制，在推理阶段自适应控制干预的时机与强度，缓解过度干预导致的语义漂移。整体通过子空间约束与动态门控实现擦除与保真之间的平衡。

Result: 论文进行了大量实验评估擦除效果与生成保真度的平衡。与五种最新（SOTA）概念擦除方法的平均性能相比，GRACE 将细粒度 NSFW 降低率提升了 17.86%，同时将宏平均目标 CLIP Score 降低了 4.75%、面向保真度的 Fréchet Inception Distance（FID）降低了 50.58%。这表明 GRACE 在更强的概念抑制能力下，显著更好地保持了原模型的生成效用。

Conclusion: GRACE 的主要贡献在于：通过语义加权敏感子空间估计与子空间约束适配器实现局部化概念擦除，用自动安全锚点机制消除手工提示工程依赖，并以能量驱动动态门控缓解语义漂移。方法在擦除有效性与生成保真度之间取得了优于五种 SOTA 方法的平衡。潜在局限包括：作为后验擦除方法仍依赖对敏感子空间的估计质量，未涉及概念擦除的鲁棒性（如对抗性绕过）与更广泛概念类别的泛化验证。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models inevitably internalize sensitive or non-compliant concepts from large-scale pretraining data, necessitating post-hoc concept erasure. However, existing erasure methods often lack explicit constraints on parameter updates, leading to over-intervention and unintended semantic drift. In addition, many methods rely on manually crafted counterfactual supervision, such as surrogate prompts, which incurs substantial data construction costs that limit scalability to new concepts. To address these limitations, we propose GRACE, a structured concept erasure framework designed to enable localized and selective intervention. Specifically, we introduce a semantically weighted sensitive subspace estimation to precisely lock intervention directions, and employ lightweight subspace-constrained adapters to prevent global semantic disturbance. To eliminate the dependency on manual prompt engineering, we design an automatically decoupled safe-anchor mechanism. To mitigate semantic drift induced by excessive intervention, we introduce an energy-driven dynamic gating mechanism that adaptively controls the timing and strength of intervention at inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves a superior balance between erasure effectiveness and generation fidelity. Compared with the average performance of five state-of-the-art (SOTA) concept erasure methods, our method improves the fine-grained NSFW reduction rate by $17.86\%$, while reducing the macro-averaged target CLIP Score and preservation-oriented Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) by $4.75\%$ and $50.58\%$, respectively, indicating stronger concept suppression with substantially improved preservation of the original model's generative utility.

</details>

*Pietro Di Stasio,Francesca Razzano,Elisa Liparulo,Gabriele Meoni,Nicolas Longépé,Deodato Tapete,Paolo Gamba,Gilda Schirinzi,Silvia Liberata Ullo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Timely monitoring of coastal water quality is critical for environmental protection, yet conventional satellite workflows rely on downlink and ground processing, introducing latency that can limit responsiveness to rapidly evolving turbidity events. To address this limitation, we propose AquaCubeAI, a lightweight machine-learning approach for onboard estimation of coastal water turbidity from Φsat-2 multispectral imagery. By shifting inference from the ground segment to the satellite, AquaCubeAI aims to enable lower-latency, more responsive, and more operationally useful turbidity monitoring under the strict compute and bandwidth constraints of spaceborne platforms. The model is trained on simulated Φsat-2 acquisitions spatially aligned with Copernicus Marine Service (CMEMS) High-Resolution Ocean Color (HR-OC) turbidity products over selected localized coastal sites spanning four European marine macro-regions. To provide a realistic evaluation of generalization in the presence of spatial correlation, we adopt a spatial block splitting protocol that mitigates data leakage between training and evaluation subsets. The main contributions of this work are: (i) a scalable dataset generation pipeline pairing simulated Φsat-2 multispectral patches with CMEMS HR-OC turbidity labels across selected localized European coastal sites; (ii) a compact Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP)-based turbidity regressor trained under a leakage-aware geospatial split and tailored to embedded constraints; and (iii) a reformulation for dense spatial prediction via parameter sharing, enabling turbidity mapping and simple threshold-based anomaly masks for onboard decision logic. Embedded deployment on an Intel Myriad Vision Processing Unit (VPU) further confirms the feasibility of low-power hardware and supports low-latency inference from multispectral inputs.

</details>


### [45] [Same Encoder, Different Winner: A Paired-View Framework for Cell Painting Encoder Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12761)
*Tim Treis,Nikita Moshkov,Johan Fredin Haslum,Shantanu Singh,Fabian J. Theis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 CP-BG-Bench，一个用于 Cell Painting（细胞涂色）视觉编码器的配对视图评测框架。该框架在四个匹配视图（原始裁剪 C、分割 S、密度增强变体 CD 与 SD）中固定中心细胞，将周围像素作为受控干预变量进行消融或增强，从而揭示不同评测协议对编码器排序的系统性分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 Cell Painting 视觉编码器通常依赖单一指标排序，最常用的是 replicate mean average precision（mAP）。但单一指标可能掩盖编码器在不同能力维度（如细胞与背景、形态与上下文、研究内与跨批次泛化）上的差异。作者希望验证：不同社区标准评测协议是否会系统性地改变编码器排名，并找出分歧背后的可解释维度。

Method: 核心方法是"配对视图"设计：对同一中心细胞构造四个匹配视图——原始裁剪 C、分割视图 S，以及密度增强变体 CD 和 SD，通过改变周围像素实现受控干预。在三个数据集（JUMP-CP、RxRx1、RxRx3-core）和三个编码器（DINOv3 ViT-B/16、OpenPhenom、SubCell）上实例化，并采用四种社区标准协议评测：replicate mAP、scIB 批次整合、CellProfiler 特征预测、跨批次扰动召回。分歧沿三个轴分解：细胞 vs 背景、形态 vs 上下文、研究内 vs 跨批次。密度增强用于量化恢复被分割移除的上下文信息的能力。

Result: 四种协议对同一批编码器给出系统性不同的排序。最大效应出现在 RxRx3-core：SubCell 使用分割输入时保留了 94% 的裁剪 replicate mAP，但仅保留 32% 的裁剪 R@10，说明分割保留的研究内信号大部分不可迁移；密度增强可恢复研究内 C-to-S 差距的 84%，但仅恢复跨批次差距的 8%。在三个数据集中的两个上，分割视图预测 CellProfiler 特征的性能等于或优于裁剪，反转了 replicate-mAP 排名。C-to-S 差距在不同数据集间相差一个数量级，但在不同编码器间保持相似，表明背景带来的增益由实验设计而非编码器决定。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是提出 CP-BG-Bench 配对视图评测框架，实证表明 Cell Painting 编码器的单指标排名对评测协议高度敏感，且协议分歧可解释为三个可解释轴的投影。该工作强调需要多协议、配对视图的评测方式，而非依赖单一 mAP。作者将发布配对视图数据集、重建流程、36 个训练检查点、聚合嵌入和完整评测套件。潜在局限包括当前仅覆盖三个数据集和三个编码器，且三个轴的划分仍为定性归因，需在更多模态与任务上验证其普适性；此外配对视图干预对真实生物信号的影响仍需谨慎解读。

Abstract: Vision encoders for Cell Painting are typically ranked by a single evaluation, commonly replicate mean average precision (mAP). We introduce CP-BG-Bench, a paired-view evaluation framework that holds the central cell fixed across four matched views (raw crop C, segmented S, and density-augmented variants CD and SD), ablating or augmenting surrounding pixels as a controlled intervention. Instantiating the framework on three datasets (JUMP-CP, RxRx1, RxRx3-core) and three encoders (DINOv3 ViT-B/16, OpenPhenom, SubCell) under four community-standard protocols (replicate mAP, scIB batch integration, CellProfiler feature prediction, cross-batch perturbation recall), we find that the four protocols rank the same encoders systematically differently, with disagreements decomposing along three axes: cell versus background, morphology versus context, and within-study versus across-batch. The largest effect: on RxRx3-core, SubCell with segmented inputs retains 94% of crop replicate mAP but only 32% of crop R@10, so the within-study signal preserved under segmentation is largely non-transferable; density augmentation recovers 84% of the within-study C-to-S gap but only 8% of the cross-batch gap. Segmented views predict CellProfiler features as well as or better than crops on two of three datasets, inverting the replicate-mAP ranking, and the C-to-S gap varies by an order of magnitude across datasets while remaining similar across encoders, indicating that background-driven gain is set by experimental design rather than by the encoder. Single-metric ranking of Cell Painting encoders is therefore sensitive to the protocol used, and protocol disagreements are interpretable as projections onto the three axes the paired-view design exposes. We will release the paired-view datasets, reconstruction pipelines, 36 trained checkpoints, aggregated embeddings, and the full evaluation suite.

</details>


### [46] [LG-PF: Lightweight Confidence-Guided Polarization Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12787)
*Zhuangfan Huang,Zhenyu Kuang,Gao Wang,Yang Liu,Haishu Tan,Xiaosong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 LG-PF，一个轻量级置信度引导的偏振图像融合框架，将偏振融合建模为"选择性残差迁移"过程。核心是用偏振置信度先验估计空间上可靠的 DoLP 响应，并通过掩膜引导的多尺度融合模块与轻量上下文感知有界校正头实现受控迁移，同时构建了多场景偏振融合数据集 MSP（1000 对像素对齐图像，17 类场景）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏振图像融合旨在将全强度图 S0 所携带的稳定亮度与结构信息，与线偏振度（DoLP）图对材质敏感的信息结合起来。然而 DoLP 的可靠性在空间上分布不均，盲目地迁移偏振信息会放大不稳定响应，并破坏由 S0 提供的结构外观。因此需要一个能区分"哪些偏振响应可信"并选择性融合的机制，避免无条件偏振迁移带来的伪影与结构失真。

Method: 将偏振融合重新表述为选择性残差迁移问题。主要模块包括：(1) Polarization Confidence Prior，用于估计空间上可靠的偏振响应分布；(2) Mask-guided Multi-scale Fusion，在三个特征尺度上以掩膜方式调控偏振信息向融合结果的迁移；(3) Lightweight Context-aware Bounded Correction Head，用于稳定局部光度与结构的过渡，通过有界校正抑制过渡区域的异常。置信度引导同时被嵌入优化目标中，以在保留可靠偏振细节的同时抑制缺乏支持的响应。整体参数仅 0.2936 M，属轻量化设计。

Result: 构建 MSP 数据集：包含来自 17 个室内外场景类别的 1000 对像素对齐图像。在 MSP 上，LG-PF 在全部六项评测指标上均取得最优结果；在 PIF 与 GAND 上进行子集评估时，无需微调即表现出良好的可迁移性。推理时间为每张图像 21.712 ms，参数规模 0.2936 M，在低计算成本下实现有竞争力的融合质量。源码、数据集与官方数据划分将在发表后公开。

Conclusion: 贡献在于提出置信度引导的选择性偏振残差迁移范式、配套的多尺度掩膜融合与有界校正轻量架构，并发布多场景偏振融合数据集 MSP，兼顾质量与效率。潜在限制包括：置信度先验在缺乏 DoLP 可靠性的极端场景（如低偏振或无偏振材质）下的泛化性未充分说明；跨数据集评估仅基于子集而非完整基准，可迁移性的结论范围有限；数据集规模（1000 对、17 类）相对中等，规模与场景多样性仍有扩展空间。

Abstract: Polarization image fusion combines the stable luminance and structural information of the total- intensity image S0 with the material-sensitive details of the degree of linear polarization (DoLP) image. However, the reliability of DoLP varies spatially, and indiscriminate polarization transfer may amplify unstable responses or disturb the structural appearance anchored by S0. We therefore propose LG-PF, a lightweight confidence-guided framework that formulates polarization fusion as a selective residual transfer process. A Polarization Confidence Prior estimates spatially reliable polarization responses, a Mask-guided Multi-scale Fusion module regulates their transfer across three feature scales, and a Lightweight Context-aware Bounded Correction Head stabilizes local photometric and structural transitions. Confidence guidance is also incorporated into the optimization objectives to preserve reliable polarization details while suppressing unsupported responses. We also construct MSP, a multi-scene polarization fusion dataset containing 1000 pixel-aligned image pairs from 17 indoor and outdoor scene categories. LG-PF achieves the best results across all six evaluated metrics on MSP, while subset-based evaluations on PIF and GAND show promising transferability without fine-tuning. With only 0.2936 M parameters and an inference time of 21.712 ms per image, LG-PF achieves competitive fusion quality with low computational cost. The source code, dataset, and official data splits will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [47] [LGFN: Lightweight Gated RGB-Polarization Fusion with Modality-Availability Conditioning for Camouflaged Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12798)
*Zhuangfan Huang,Xiaosong Li,Yang Liu,Tao Ye,Haishu Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LGFN 是一个轻量级的门控 RGB-偏振融合框架，用于伪装目标检测（COD）。它通过确定性的 Modality Router 支持"仅 RGB"与"偏振辅助"两种可独立优化的配置，在 PCOD_1200 数据集上取得优于现有 RGB 方法及多模态方法（PolarNet、IPNet）的性能，同时显著降低参数量、计算量与延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伪装目标检测是智能光学感知中的重要工程任务，但目标与背景高度相似时极具挑战。偏振成像可提供互补的物理线索（DoLP、AoP），然而现有方法通常假设固定的多模态输入配置，并将"偏振分支内部协调"与"RGB-偏振跨模态交互"纠缠在一起，导致模型难以适应偏振信号可能缺失或质量不稳定的实际场景。论文希望解耦这两类协调过程，并支持在偏振不可用时退化为纯 RGB 推理，从而提升部署灵活性与鲁棒性。

Method: LGFN 采用统一的 RGB-only / 偏振辅助双配置设计：(1) 确定性 Modality Router 依据偏振可用性选择配置；(2) 多模态配置下，可用性条件化的 Modality Gate 对可用的偏振分支进行校准；(3) Gated Polarization Hub 将学习得到的 DoLP 与 AoP 表示与显式偏振线索进行协调融合；(4) RGB-Polarization Cross Fusion 通过受控的残差交互将协调后的偏振表示注入 RGB 特征层级。推理阶段不依赖样本级统计量或手工设计的质量描述子，整体为轻量化门控架构。

Result: 在完整的 230 张图像 PCOD_1200 测试集上，RGB-only 配置取得 MAE 0.0090、Dice 0.8806、IoU 0.8144，在被评估的 RGB 类方法中六项指标均最优。在统一的局部重评估协议下，多模态配置在六项指标上均超过 PolarNet 与 IPNet。相较 IPNet，参数量减少 53.1%，FLOPs 减少 73.6%，延迟降低 63.0%。

Conclusion: 论文提出了一种可适应偏振可用性的轻量门控 RGB-偏振融合框架，通过解耦偏振内部协调与跨模态交互实现灵活配置，并在 COD 任务上同时提升精度与效率。其局限在于评测主要基于 PCOD_1200 这一较小规模数据集，且方法对偏振线索的依赖程度、在更大规模与真实复杂场景下的泛化能力尚待进一步验证。该工作与多模态物理感知、实时/边缘视觉系统具有一定相关性。

Abstract: Camouflaged object detection (COD) is an important engineering task in intelligent optical perception, but it remains challenging when targets closely resemble their surroundings. Polarization imaging provides complementary physical cues, whereas existing methods typically assume fixed multimodal input configurations and entangle intra-polarization coordination with interaction between red-green-blue (RGB) and polarization representations. We propose LGFN, a lightweight gated RGB-polarization fusion framework supporting separately optimized RGB-only and polarization-assisted configurations. A deterministic Modality Router selects the appropriate configuration according to polarization availability. In the multimodal configuration, an availability-conditioned Modality Gate calibrates the available polarization branches; the Gated Polarization Hub coordinates learned degree of linear polarization (DoLP) and angle of polarization (AoP) representations with explicit polarization cues; and RGB-Polarization Cross Fusion introduces the coordinated representation into the RGB hierarchy through controlled residual interaction. The multimodal configuration requires neither sample-dependent statistics nor handcrafted quality descriptors during inference. On the complete 230-image PCOD_1200 test set, the RGB-only configuration achieves a mean absolute error of 0.0090, a Dice score of 0.8806, and an intersection over union of 0.8144, obtaining the best results on all six metrics among the evaluated RGB-based methods. Under a common local reevaluation protocol, the multimodal configuration outperforms PolarNet and IPNet on all six metrics. Relative to IPNet, it reduces the parameter count, floating-point operations, and latency by 53.1%, 73.6%, and 63.0%, respectively.

</details>


### [48] [Online Video Agent Harness for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12818)
*Sen Yang,Boqiang Duan,Jing Yang,Weihao Bo,Jie Liu,Boyuan Tong,Ze Feng,Wenkang Zhang,Jingdong Wang,Hua Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoXAgent 是一个纯在线的长视频理解智能体框架（harness），从原始视频与用户查询出发，通过规划分解任务、按需调用异构专家工具、聚合多模态证据并解决冲突来生成答案，在长视频基准上以更小的上下文开销取得与前沿 LMM/视频智能体相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解类似"大海捞针"：与查询相关的证据在长时间跨度上稀疏分布。将密集帧塞入单一 VLM 上下文不仅面临"上下文腐化"（context rot）和极高成本，还难以覆盖全部内容。现有视频智能体多依赖与查询无关的离线预处理或临时拼凑的工具集，容易漏掉查询特定的细节并浪费算力。因此需要一个从视频文件与查询出发、在线按需寻找证据的框架。

Method: VideoXAgent 是一个纯在线（purely online）的智能体 harness：输入给定视频文件和用户查询，先进行任务规划与分解，再按需调用专用专家工具，最后聚合多模态证据、消解观察之间的冲突以形成答案。核心设计包括：（1）基于数据驱动的"原子能力"分类体系构建异构专家工具集，涵盖脚本（scripts）、VLM 及领域模型（如目标检测、OCR、ASR、人脸识别）；（2）在该工具集上支持按需调用；（3）harness 层面引入"客观证据提示"（objective evidence prompting）来抑制幻觉，并采用预算感知控制（budget-aware control）以避免不终止。整体上以渐进式的智能体证据搜寻替代将整段视频打包进单一上下文。

Result: 在 Video-MME-Long、LongVideoBench-Long、LVBench 和 MINERVA 四个长视频基准上评测。VideoXAgent 在更小的上下文占用下（每条样本约 50k tokens 的智能体上下文，即使对长达一小时的视频也如此）与前沿 LMM 和视频智能体表现相当。在复杂视频推理基准（如 MINERVA）上，其性能达到同类水平，但上下文用量仅为 1,024 帧密集打包基线的约 15%。此外，即使使用视觉能力较弱、甚至纯文本的编排器（orchestrator），该 harness 仍然有效。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出一个纯在线、工具驱动的长视频理解智能体框架，其"按需证据搜寻"范式在多个长视频基准上以显著更小的上下文开销达到前沿水平，并揭示了强长视频理解能力可来自渐进的智能体式证据搜索，而非将整段视频塞入单次上下文。潜在局限包括：性能依赖专家工具集与原子能力分类体系的覆盖度；按需调用与预算控制引入额外的编排开销与工程复杂度。

Abstract: Long video understanding often behaves like a visual needle-in-a-haystack problem: query-relevant evidence is sparsely distributed across long temporal spans, while packing dense frames into a single VLM context incurs \textit{context rot} and high cost. Existing video agents often rely on query-agnostic offline preprocessing or ad hoc tool sets, which can miss query-specific details and waste computation. In this work, we present VideoXAgent, a purely online video-agent harness for long video understanding that starts from the given video file and user query, plans and decomposes the task, invokes specialized expert tools on demand, and aggregates multimodal evidence to produce a final answer while resolving conflicts among observations. To support this on-demand invocation, we design a suite of heterogeneous expert tools guided by a data-driven taxonomy of atomic capabilities, spanning scripts, VLMs, and domain models (e.g., detection, OCR, ASR, face recognition). The harness further enforces objective evidence prompting and budget-aware control to curb hallucination and non-termination. Across Video-MME-Long, LongVideoBench-Long, LVBench, and MINERVA, VideoXAgent is competitive with frontier LMMs and video agents under a smaller context footprint---about 50k tokens of agent context per sample, even on hour-long videos. In particular, on complex video-reasoning benchmarks such as MINERVA, it matches this level while using only about 15\% of the context of a 1,024-frame dense-packing baseline. Notably, the harness remains effective with a visually weak or even text-only orchestrator, suggesting that strong long-video understanding can emerge from progressive agentic evidence seeking rather than from packing the full video into a single context. Project page: https://go-agent-x.github.io/video_agent_harness/

</details>


### [49] [SCDM: Spatial-Contextual Disentanglement Mamba via Differential Inference for Efficient Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12825)
*Mustafa Bora Çelik,Hayriye Aktaş Dinçer,Ayse Keles*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出空间-上下文差分 Mamba（SCDM），一种非对称双分支架构，通过正分支提取判别性病灶特征、负分支建模并抑制正常解剖上下文，并借助相似度驱动的排斥门与差分推理实现选择性表征解耦，在 RSNA 肺炎数据集上以 29.4M 参数、1.44G FLOPs 取得 0.858 AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医学图像分析中，状态空间模型（尤其是 VMamba）被视为建模长程依赖的高效方案。但病理特征往往与解剖背景视觉上高度相似，区分细微病变与正常组织仍是核心难题。现有 SSM 架构倾向于学习纠缠（entangled）表征，缺乏显式机制将疾病相关信号从正常解剖中分离，导致判别信息与背景干扰混杂。该问题在肺炎等病灶边界模糊、与周围组织同质的场景中尤为突出，因此亟需一种能主动建模并抑制正常解剖上下文、从而突出病变信号的表示学习机制。

Method: SCDM 采用非对称双分支设计以实现选择性表征解耦：正分支（Positive Branch）负责提取判别性病变特征，负分支（Negative Branch）主动建模正常解剖上下文并加以抑制。两条分支间通过相似度驱动的排斥门（similarity-driven repulsion gate）促进竞争性特征学习，使正分支远离负分支所表征的正常解剖模式；随后采用差分推理规则（differential inference rule）将两分支输出相减，从而抑制共有解剖成分、保留差异化病灶信号。该机制的关键优势在于无需额外分支标签，也不增加模型容量，即以共享/固定规模的参数量实现解耦学习。整体构建于 Mamba/SSM 框架之上，保留其对长程空间依赖的高效建模能力。

Result: 在 RSNA Pneumonia 数据集上进行评估。分类性能上达到 AUC = 0.858，与标准 VMamba 及视觉 Transformer 基线相比具有竞争力；效率上参数量仅 29.4M、FLOPs 为 1.44G，显著低于对比基线。此外，激活分析（activation analysis）表明差分机制带来高精度的病灶定位能力，能够通过抑制无关解剖干扰项有效隔离病变区域，验证了解耦机制在可解释性与定位层面的效果。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出 SCDM 这一面向医学图像的非对称双分支 SSM 架构，通过相似度驱动排斥门与差分推理实现病灶与正常解剖的表征解耦，在保持轻量化的同时获得竞争力分类性能与精确病灶定位，且无需额外监督标签。其意义在于为 SSM 在细粒度医学判别任务中的"干扰抑制"问题提供了显式建模范式。潜在局限包括：仅在单一 RSNA 肺炎数据集上验证，泛化性尚未在多种病变/模态上确认；负分支对"正常解剖"的建模依赖数据分布假设；排斥门与差分规则中超参数的影响未充分说明。

Abstract: State Space Models (SSMs), particularly VMamba, have emerged as efficient alternatives for modeling long-range dependencies in medical image analysis. However, distinguishing subtle pathological features from visually similar anatomical backgrounds remains a significant challenge. Existing SSM architectures often learn entangled representations, lacking explicit mechanisms to separate disease-specific signals from normal anatomy. To address this limitation, we propose Spatial-Contextual Differential Mamba (SCDM), an asymmetric dual-branch architecture designed for selective representational disentanglement. SCDM introduces a Positive Branch for extracting discriminative features and a Negative Branch that actively models and suppresses normal anatomical context. This separation is achieved through a similarity-driven repulsion gate and a differential inference rule, which promote competitive feature learning without requiring additional branch labels or increasing model capacity. Evaluated on the RSNA Pneumonia dataset, SCDM achieves competitive classification performance (AUC of 0.858) while requiring significantly fewer parameters (29.4M) and FLOPs (1.44G) compared to standard VMamba and vision transformer baselines. Furthermore, activation analyses demonstrate that our differential mechanism yields highly precise localization, effectively isolating lesions by inhibiting irrelevant anatomical distractors.

</details>


### [50] [CoralscapesV2: Panoptic and Fine-Grained Visual Scene Understanding in Coral Reefs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12826)
*Jonathan Sauder,Thomas Ruckli,Gabrielė Strodomskytė,Ibrahim Souleiman Abdallah,Rahma Hassan Abdi,Djama Goumaneh Awaleh,Mohamed Houssein Farah,Moustapha Nour,Osama Sharhubil Saad,Mustafa Mohammed Khalafallah Altaib,Maysoon Kteifan,Farah Alsoqi,Eyad Zgool,Jafar Al-Omari,Temesgen Gebremeskel Gebreluel,Zekaria Zekeria Abdulkerim,Meron Ghirmay,Teklehaimanot Beraki,Devis Tuia,Guilhem Banc-Prandi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 CoralscapesV2，是 Coralscapes 数据集的扩展版本，用于珊瑚礁通用视觉场景理解。该数据集将语义分割类别从 39 类扩展到 95 类细粒度视觉类别，提升了规模、覆盖范围与标注完整性，并首次提供 65k 条鱼体实例掩码标注，成为珊瑚礁领域首个全景分割（panoptic segmentation）数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为应对全球珊瑚礁衰退，生态监测需大幅扩展规模，计算机视觉成为处理海量 reef 影像的关键手段。然而数据采集范式正从高度标准化、受约束的调查图像转向规模化平台上的非受约束（in-the-wild）图像，这要求机器学习方法能够从通用目的的珊瑚礁影像中获得细粒度理解。现有标注方案（仅基于静态图像标注鱼类）被证明不足以捕捉完整的鱼类个体，因此亟需更完整、更泛化的数据集来支撑语义级与实例级理解。

Method: 论文构建了 CoralscapesV2 数据集，主要技术贡献体现在数据层面：(1) 在 Coralscapes 基础上扩大数据集规模与场景覆盖范围；(2) 提升语义分割标注的完整性与质量；(3) 将语义类别从 39 类扩展至 95 个细粒度视觉类别；(4) 提供 65k 条完整的鱼体实例掩码标注——利用视频信息进行标注以确保完备性，并指出仅用静态图像标注鱼类存在不足；(5) 首次为珊瑚礁提供全景分割标注，统一整合语义（stuff）与实例（thing）分割。数据集覆盖野外多种场景，构成具有挑战性的基准。

Result: CoralscapesV2 作为珊瑚礁全景分割的首个数据集，包含 95 类细粒度类别与 65k 鱼体实例掩码，覆盖野外多样化场景。论文将其定位为对当代语义分割与实例分割模型的挑战性基准（benchmark），但摘要中未给出具体的模型评测数值、指标或对比结果，主要贡献集中于数据集构建与标注方法论。

Conclusion: CoralscapesV2 是迈向珊瑚礁通用全景分割的重要一步，对扩大珊瑚礁监测具有实质意义。其应用前景广泛，包括从机器人或手持视频进行底栖覆盖（benthic cover）制图，以及设计自动化量化与理解鱼类行为、鱼-礁交互的方法。可能的局限在于：摘要未报告定量实验结果，且全景分割在高度杂乱、类别细粒度且存在遮挡的水下场景中仍具挑战性；此外，泛化到不同平台与采集条件的能力有待进一步验证。

Abstract: In order to design conservation and restoration strategies to counter the global decline of coral reefs, ecological monitoring of reefs needs to be scaled up dramatically. Computer vision methods are increasingly used to tackle the vast amount of data: as the paradigm of data collection in reefs shifts from highly standardized and constrained survey images to unconstrained imagery on scalable platforms, it is necessary to design machine learning methods that help to get a fine-grained understanding of reefs from general-purpose reef imagery. This paper provides CoralscapesV2, an extension of the Coralscapes dataset for general-purpose visual scene understanding in reefs. CoralscapesV2 increases the dataset size, scope, label completeness and quality for semantic segmentation, and extends the number of classes from 39 to 95 fine-grained visual categories. Furthermore, CoralscapesV2 provides 65k exhaustive fish instance mask annotations, meticulously annotated to completeness by using the video, revealing that annotation of fish based on only static images is insufficient. CoralscapesV2 is the first dataset for panoptic segmentation in coral reefs, capturing a wide range of scenarios in the wild, posing a challenging benchmark for contemporary semantic segmentation and instance segmentation models. CoralscapesV2 is an important step towards general-purpose panoptic segmentation in coral reefs, which has substantial implications for scaling up coral reef monitoring, as it can be employed in a wide range of applications from benthic cover mapping from robot or handheld videos to designing methods for automated quantification and understanding of fish behavior and fish-reef interactions.

</details>


### [51] [A Dual Cross-Attention Framework for Colposcopic CIN Grading and Swede Score Prediction Using a New Multi-Center Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12827)
*Dania Khan,Nuzhat Aisha Shaikh,Asfina Hassan Juicy,Raiyun Kabir,S M Shahida,Taufiq Hasan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双流交叉注意力深度学习框架，用于宫颈上皮内瘤变（CIN）分级与临床Swede评分预测，并发布多中心阴道镜数据集BUET Multi-Center Colposcopy Dataset。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宫颈癌在全球范围内是重大健康挑战，疾病负担不成比例地集中在低中收入国家（LMICs），原因在于缺乏训练有素的专科医生，以及基于阴道镜的筛查本身具有主观性。因此需要自动化的、基于风险的辅助筛查工具来支持资源受限环境下的分诊。论文的核心动机是构建一个能模拟专家阴道镜医师视觉推理过程的自动化CIN分级与Swede评分预测框架，同时填补多中心标注数据缺失的问题。

Method: 提出双流交叉注意力（dual-stream cross-attention）架构，通过显式融合成对的（paired）多模态宫颈图像（cervigrams）来评估组织的对比性反应，从而模仿专家阴道镜医师对醋酸/碘染前后组织变化的对比推理方式。针对Swede评分五个组成部分中严重的类别不平衡与评分不一致问题，设计了定制的复合损失函数（composite loss）。同时构建并标注了BUET多中心阴道镜数据集，用于Swede评分预测与CIN分级两个任务。

Result: 在三分类CIN分级任务上取得71.85%准确率与86.23% AUC-ROC，优于已有方法。在Swede评分各组成部分预测上，AUC-ROC范围为75.7%至88.4%，复合损失函数带来了F1-score的一致性提升。总Swede评分（0–10分）预测的平均绝对误差（MAE）为1.489。数据集与源码已公开（https://github.com/mHealthBuet/BUET-colposcopy）。

Conclusion: 主要贡献包括：面向CIN分级与Swede评分预测的双流交叉注意力框架、针对类别不平衡与评分不一致的复合损失函数，以及公开的多中心阴道镜数据集。结果表明该方法有望推动AI辅助阴道镜筛查工具的发展，支持资源受限医疗环境下的风险分诊。局限方面：摘要未报告外部验证、跨中心泛化性能与设备/标注者间差异的详细消融，MAE 1.489的总Swede评分预测精度仍有提升空间，且此类工作属医学影像领域，与事件相机、SNN、UAV感知等方向无直接关联。

Abstract: Cervical cancer is a major global health challenge, with disease burden falling disproportionately on low- and middle-income countries (LMICs) due to a shortage of trained specialists and the subjective nature of colposcopy-based screening. To address this challenge, we propose a novel deep learning framework for the automated grading of Cervical Intraepithelial Neoplasia (CIN) and the prediction of clinical Swede scores. We also introduce the BUET Multi-Center Colposcopy Dataset, a novel, multi-center cohort designed and annotated for Swede score prediction and CIN grading. Our proposed dual-stream cross-attention architecture mimics the visual reasoning of an expert colposcopist by explicitly fusing paired multimodal cervigrams to evaluate comparative tissue responses. Furthermore, we introduce a custom composite loss function to address severe class imbalances and scoring inconsistencies across the five Swede score components. The proposed framework achieved 71.85% accuracy and an 86.23% AUC-ROC for three-class CIN grading, outperforming existing methods. For Swede score component prediction, the architecture achieved AUC-ROC values ranging from 75.7% to 88.4%, with the composite loss function yielding consistent F1-score improvements. Finally, the total predicted Swede Score, which ranges between 0 and 10, shows a Mean Absolute Error (MAE) of 1.489. The results show that the proposed method can pave the way towards developing AI-assisted colposcopy screening tools to support risk-based triage in resource-limited healthcare settings. The dataset and source code are publicly available(url: https://github.com/mHealthBuet/BUET-colposcopy)

</details>


### [52] [Balancing Emotional Alignment and Semantic Consistency in Image Generation via Reinforcement Learning with Valence-Arousal Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12830)
*Jisheng Dang,Zhenxuan Wang,Bin Li,Ronghao Lin,Bin Hu,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于流匹配（Flow Matching）与组相对策略优化（GRPO）的连续情感控制图像生成框架，通过 valence-arousal（VA）连续条件、中性语义锚点（neutral semantic anchor）以及终端 VA 奖励，在提升情感对齐的同时抑制"情感-语义漂移"（emotion-semantic drift）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像的连续情感控制要求在增强情感对齐的同时，不改变提示词所描述的对象、布局或场景。现有监督式情感注入方法多在特征空间优化代理目标（proxy），容易导致情感-语义漂移：情感条件越强，内容被无意改变的程度也越大。因此需要一种既能精细控制连续情感、又能保持语义一致性的生成范式。

Method: 该方法建立在流匹配图像生成框架之上，包含三个关键组件：(1) 连续 VA 条件注入，实现情感强度的连续可调；(2) 将确定性的 probability-flow ODE 转换为保持边缘分布（marginal-preserving）的 SDE，从而获得非退化的转移密度，支持轨迹采样与策略比（policy-ratio）估计，使在线 RL 可行；(3) 基于 GRPO 的强化学习优化，终端奖励由冻结的 CLIP-based VA 回归器给出，度量预测 VA 与目标 VA 坐标之间的距离；同时以同一提示词在零 VA 条件下生成的图像作为特征空间参考，充当语义保持的锚点正则项。训练时使用缩短的去噪调度（reduced denoising schedule）以降低在线 RL 采样成本，推理时恢复原始调度。

Result: 在 3,300 个提示词-情感组合上进行实验。结果显示，相比 VA-conditioned 基线，所提方法在 valence 和 arousal 误差上显著降低；与 EmotiCrafter 相比，CLIPScore 有所提升。同时在无参考图像质量（reference-free image quality）指标上存在可测量的权衡（trade-off）。

Conclusion: 论文证明了 anchor-regularized Flow-GRPO 是平衡情感对齐与语义一致性的一种实用方案，其核心贡献在于将连续 VA 条件、marginal-preserving SDE 转换与 GRPO 相结合，并引入中性语义锚点来缓解情感-语义漂移。局限在于：无参考图像质量存在可感知的下降，且终端奖励依赖冻结的 CLIP-based VA 回归器，其精度可能构成性能上限。

Abstract: Continuous emotion control in text-to-image generation requires a model to improve affective alignment without changing the objects, layout, or scene described by the prompt. Existing supervised emotion-injection methods often optimize feature-space proxies and may therefore exhibit emotion-semantic drift, in which stronger emotional conditioning is accompanied by unintended content changes. We address this problem with a flow-matching image-generation framework that combines continuous valence-arousal (VA) conditioning, Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), and a neutral semantic anchor. The deterministic probability-flow ODE is converted into a marginal-preserving SDE, yielding non-degenerate transition densities for trajectory sampling and policy-ratio estimation. A frozen CLIP-based VA regressor supplies a terminal reward measuring the distance between the predicted and target VA coordinates, while an image generated from the same prompt under zero VA conditioning provides a feature-space reference for semantic preservation. A reduced denoising schedule is used for online RL sampling, whereas the original schedule is retained at inference. Experiments on 3,300 prompt-emotion combinations show substantially lower valence and arousal errors than the VA-conditioned baseline and an improved CLIPScore relative to EmotiCrafter, with a measurable trade-off in reference-free image quality. The results support anchor-regularized Flow-GRPO as a practical approach to balancing emotional alignment and semantic consistency in continuous-affect image synthesis.

</details>


### [53] [Self-supervised Pre-training Helps Retinal Disease Progression Modelling Most When Data Is Scarce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12834)
*Ifeoma Veronica Nwabufo,Julius Gervelmeyer,Sarah Müller,Philipp Berens*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了在纵向标注数据稀缺时，如何利用大规模横截面眼底影像进行自监督预训练以建模疾病进展。以年龄相关性黄斑变性（AMD）为例，作者在NAKO横截面队列上预训练编码器，并在AREDS纵向队列上预测进展至晚期AMD的时间。核心发现是：在编码器冻结且标注样本很少的场景下（纵向队列的典型情形），自监督预训练表示能用数百个标注样本达到临床可接受的判别性能，而这种优势在微调后消失；迁移效果主要取决于自监督目标本身，而非语料规模或领域匹配度。作者据此提出实用方案：冻结的自监督编码器加轻量生存头。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疾病进展建模本质上需要纵向影像队列（同一受试者的多次随访），但此类数据稀缺且样本量小；相反，横截面数据（每位受试者仅一张图像）却十分丰富。自监督预训练被认为可以弥合这一数据鸿沟，但此前并不清楚：何种预训练策略最有利于进展建模，以及这一结论如何随标注纵向数据量的变化而变化。该问题对AMD等致盲性眼病尤为重要——若能早期、准确地预测进展至晚期AMD的时间，将直接支持临床随访与干预决策。

Method: 研究以AMD为任务载体，采用"预训练—迁移—生存预测"的框架：在大型横截面NAKO队列上预训练编码器，在纵向AREDS数据集上预测进展至晚期AMD的时间（生存/时间到事件建模）。作者系统比较了多维度因素：(1) 模型来源——自研自监督编码器 vs 通用基础模型DINOv2 vs 领域专用基础模型RETFound；(2) 自监督目标——对比学习（contrastive）、掩码自编码（masked autoencoding）、自蒸馏（self-distillation）；(3) 使用协议——冻结编码器 vs 微调；(4) 标注量——从100到32,250个有标注样本的多个训练集规模。最终提出的实践方案是将冻结的自监督编码器与一个轻量级生存头（survival head）组合。

Result: 实验跨越对比、掩码自编码、自蒸馏三类目标，并在冻结与微调两种协议、100至32,250个标注样本的梯度上评估AMD进展预测。主要结果：(1) 最优模型取决于编码器的使用方式，而非单一固定赢家；(2) 在编码器冻结且标签很少的情形下（正是纵向队列的典型场景），预训练表示仅用数百个标注样本即可达到临床合理的判别性能，而从头训练的模型做不到；(3) 这一优势在微调后逐渐消失；(4) 迁移效果由自监督目标主导，而非语料规模或领域匹配度——即在一个规模适中的横截面队列上预训练的编码器，可匹敌甚至超过规模大得多的同领域基础模型。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献在于为"纵向数据稀缺下的疾病进展建模"提供了可操作的经验结论：采用冻结的自监督编码器搭配轻量生存头，并选择恰当的预训练目标，其效果可能优于依赖大规模领域基础模型或全量微调的方案。其意义在于挑战了"语料规模与领域匹配最重要"的直觉，指出自监督目标才是迁移性能的关键决定因素，这对临床小样本、纵向随访受限的场景具有直接指导价值。潜在局限包括：结论目前仅在AMD这一单一疾病与特定队列（NAKO、AREDS）上验证；冻结编码器+生存头的方案虽实用，但可能未充分挖掘微调或端到端进展建模的潜力，跨疾病、跨模态的泛化性仍有待检验。

Abstract: Modelling how a disease progresses over time requires longitudinal imaging cohorts, which are scarce and small, whereas cross-sectional data -- one image per participant -- is abundant. Self-supervised pre-training on such data offers a way to bridge this gap, but it is unclear which strategy best supports progression modelling, or how that answer depends on the amount of labelled longitudinal data. We study this for age-related macular degeneration (AMD), pre-training encoders on the large cross-sectional NAKO cohort and predicting time to late AMD on the longitudinal AREDS dataset. We compare in-house self-supervised encoders against a general-purpose (DINOv2) and a domain-specific (RETFound) foundation model, across contrastive, masked-autoencoding, and self-distillation objectives, under frozen and fine-tuned protocols, and across labelled training sets from 100 to 32,250 examples. Which model performs best depends on how the encoder is used. When the encoder is frozen and labels are few -- the regime typical of longitudinal cohorts -- pre-trained representations reach clinically reasonable discrimination from a few hundred labelled samples, while models trained from scratch do not; this advantage fades under fine-tuning. Transfer is governed by the self-supervision objective rather than corpus scale or domain match, so that an encoder pre-trained on a modest cross-sectional cohort matches or exceeds a far larger in-domain foundation model. Together, these results offer a practical recipe for building progression models where longitudinal data is scarce: a frozen self-supervised encoder with a lightweight survival head.

</details>


### [54] [HemaHier: Chain-Conditioned Ordinal Hierarchies for Lineage-Aware Bone-Marrow Cytology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12835)
*Afshin Bozorgpour,Peter Schüffler,Edgar Jost,Dorit Merhof*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HemaHier 是一个面向骨髓细胞学基础模型的序数-层次化预测头，通过"链条件化成熟度分数"在保持识别精度的同时，建模造血谱系结构与细胞成熟轨迹，减少生物学意义上的严重错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 骨髓细胞学数据本身具有内在结构：每个细胞隶属于某个造血谱系（lineage），且许多细胞类型位于有序的成熟轨迹（maturation trajectory）上。但标准的扁平分类器（flat classifier）忽略了这一结构，存在两个问题：(1) 将所有误分类等同视之，把同一谱系内的轻微混淆（如相邻成熟阶段）与跨谱系的严重错误（不同谱系混淆）赋予相同惩罚，而这在生物学与临床意义上有本质差别；(2) 只输出离散类别标签，无法给出谱系内的成熟度有序关系，缺乏对细胞成熟程度的连续刻画。因此需要一种能同时利用层次（谱系）与序数（成熟度）先验的预测框架。

Method: HemaHier 是附加在冻结或轻度微调的细胞学基础模型之上的预测头，核心组件为"链条件化成熟度分数"（chain-conditioned maturity score）：在每条谱系链（per-chain query）的条件下，模型读取一个单一的成熟度标量值，形成谱系内的有序表示。其监督策略具有选择性：仅在生物学上有效的健康链（healthy chains）上进行成熟度监督，而发育异常（dysplastic）与脱离链（off-chain）的细胞类型仍作为独立类别保留，但被排除在成熟度监督之外，以避免污染有序空间的语义。细粒度分类预测与谱系预测通过一个共享后验（shared posterior）耦合，从而保证层次一致性（hierarchical consistency，即细类预测与谱系预测不产生冲突）。训练采用分阶段目标（staged objective）：先稳定识别能力，再逐步加入谱系与成熟度监督。

Result: 在共享本体（shared ontology）下，于三个骨髓数据集上进行实验。HemaHier 取得了具有竞争力的识别性能，同时减少了生物学上严重的错误（跨谱系误判），并额外提供扁平分类器所不具备的谱系内成熟度排序能力。论文提供了开源代码（https://github.com/xmindflow/HemaHier）。摘要未给出具体指标数值，主要结论为识别精度与扁平基线相当，但错误类型分布更符合生物学合理性。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于将骨髓细胞分类从扁平离散标签问题重构为层次化 + 序数预测问题，通过链条件化成熟度分数、共享后验保证的层次一致性和分阶段训练目标，使模型输出不仅包含类别，还包含符合造血生物学规律的谱系-成熟度结构，并降低严重错误率。其意义在于把领域先验（谱系层级与成熟轨迹）显式注入预测头，可与现有细胞学基础模型兼容（冻结或轻度适配）。可能局限包括：成熟度监督依赖人工定义的"健康链"与本体结构，跨数据集的 ontology 对齐成本较高；对 dysplastic/off-chain 类型只做类别处理，未建模其异常结构；成熟度标量的临床可用性与标注一致性仍需进一步验证；摘要中未报告具体量化指标。

Abstract: Bone-marrow cytology is inherently structured: each cell belongs to a hematopoietic lineage, and many cell types lie on ordered maturation trajectories. Standard flat classifiers ignore this structure, treating a mild same-lineage confusion the same as a severe cross-lineage mistake and predicting only discrete labels. We propose HemaHier, an ordinal-hierarchical prediction head for a frozen or lightly adapted cytology foundation model. Its central component is a chain-conditioned maturity score that reads a single maturity value under a per-chain query, supervised only on biologically valid healthy chains, while dysplastic and off-chain cell types remain classes but are excluded from maturity supervision. Fine and lineage predictions are coupled through a shared posterior that guarantees hierarchical consistency, and a staged objective first stabilizes recognition, then adds lineage and maturity supervision. On three bone-marrow datasets under a shared ontology, HemaHier achieves competitive recognition while reducing biologically severe errors and adding a within-lineage maturity ordering that flat classifiers lack. Code is available at https://github.com/xmindflow/HemaHier.

</details>


### [55] [Pre-Trained Low-Rank Tensor Decomposition for Multi-Dimensional Image Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12843)
*Bing-Zhang Fu,Zhi-Long Han,Ting-Zhu Huang,Xi-Le Zhao,Deyu Meng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个预训练低秩张量分解框架 PLTD（Pre-trained Low-rank Tensor Decomposition），将预训练大视觉模型（DINOv3）与经典张量分解有机结合，在恢复保真度、可学习参数数量和碳足迹之间取得平衡，用于多维图像恢复任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统张量分解方法面向多维图像表示时为每张图像从头学习实例特定结构，忽略了不同图像之间共享的共性结构，导致三类问题：语义建模能力受限、计算成本高昂、可学习参数庞大。作者希望借助预训练大视觉模型所蕴含的通用视觉先验，弥补经典张量分解缺乏共性结构建模能力的缺陷。

Method: PLTD 将目标张量分解为一个隐张量（latent tensor）和一个可学习的变换，后者将隐张量映射回原始数据域。隐张量由两个互补项构成：（1）固定的预训练隐张量，从预训练大视觉模型 DINOv3 中蒸馏得到，用于捕获目标张量的共性结构；（2）可学习的低秩隐张量，用于刻画目标张量的实例特定结构。相比浅层且未经训练的深度张量分解，该设计同时具备共性先验与实例自适应能力。作者进一步构建了对应的多维图像恢复模型，从理论上论证该框架的优势，并讨论了 PLTD 与经典张量分解框架之间的联系。

Result: 在多维图像恢复任务上开展了大量实验，结果表明 PLTD 一致优于现有最先进（state-of-the-art）方法，同时实现了更高的恢复保真度、更少的可学习参数以及更小的碳足迹。文中通过理论分析论证了框架优势，但摘要未给出具体数据集、评价指标（如 PSNR/SSIM）与数值结果。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出首个预训练低秩张量分解框架 PLTD，把大视觉模型的通用先验引入经典张量分解，兼顾共性—实例双重结构建模，并在保真度、参数量与能耗之间取得平衡，同时给出了理论支撑及与经典框架的关联分析。潜在局限：方法依赖 DINOv3 等大视觉模型的预训练先验，其在事件流、非规则采样或稀疏时序传感等非图像域的可迁移性尚未验证，摘要亦未报告具体量化结果与推理效率细节。

Abstract: Recently, tensor decompositions are prevalent for multi-dimensional image representation, which learn the instance-specific structure of each image from scratch. However, tensor decompositions neglect the common structure across different images, leading to limited semantic modeling capability, high computational cost, and a large number of learnable parameters.
