To address this challenge, we suggest the first pre-trained low-rank tensor decomposition (PLTD) framework, which organically integrates the pre-trained large vision model into the classical tensor decomposition framework. Beyond the shallow and untrained deep tensor decomposition, the suggested PLTD achieves an unprecedented balance among higher recovery fidelity, fewer learnable parameters, and smaller carbon footprint. Specifically, PLTD factorizes the target tensor into a latent tensor and a learnable transform that maps the latent tensor back to the original data domain. The latent tensor consists of two indispensable and complementary terms, i.e., a fixed pre-trained latent tensor and a learnable low-rank latent tensor. The fixed pre-trained latent tensor is distilled from a pre-trained large vision model (i.e., DINOv3) to capture the common structure of the target tensor, while the learnable low-rank latent tensor characterizes the instance-specific structure of the target tensor. To examine the potential of PLTD, we develop the corresponding multi-dimensional image recovery model and theoretically justify the advantages of this framework. Additionally, we discuss the connections between PLTD and classical tensor decomposition frameworks. Extensive experiments on multi-dimensional image recovery demonstrate that PLTD consistently achieves superior performance compared with state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [56] [MGAvatar: Mesh-Bound Gaussians for Head Avatar Geometry and Appearance Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12850)
*Lei Shi,Sen Peng,Zhiyang Deng,Zhonggui Chen,Xiaohu Guo,Baorong Yang,Xiao Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MGAvatar提出一种高斯-网格混合表示，通过"顶点绑定高斯"与"面绑定高斯"两种绑定模式联合建模头部几何与外观，并引入视角条件神经颜色场和高斯偏移网络，实现高保真头部化身建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的头部建模需要稳定且富有表现力的几何表示。现有基于高斯（Gaussian）的头部化身方法通常依赖参数化模板（如FLAME）进行高斯初始化与形变，但这类模板缺乏个性化先验，且难以表示头发、衣物等模板之外的结构。因此需要一种能同时兼顾稳定几何与丰富外观的表示方式。

Method: 提出MGAvatar高斯-网格混合表示，核心是两种高斯-网格绑定模式的分阶段使用：1）几何阶段采用顶点绑定高斯（vertex-bound Gaussians），约束其可学习参数，通过渐进式网格形变表征复杂头部几何，并用姿态相关偏移模块（pose-dependent offset module）处理非刚性形变；2）几何稳定后切换为面绑定高斯（face-bound Gaussians）进行外观建模。为提升新视角/新姿态下外观一致性，引入视角条件神经颜色场（view-conditioned neural color field）以缓解独立优化高斯颜色带来的伪影；并设计高斯偏移网络（Gaussian offset network）在观测空间预测高斯偏移图，增强面绑定高斯捕获动态面部纹理的灵活性。

Result: 在多视角视频和单目视频上进行了大量实验，结果表明MGAvatar在渲染质量上优于现有方法，能够生成纹理细节丰富、高保真的头部化身。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出高斯-网格混合表示与双绑定模式（顶点绑定/面绑定）的分阶段建模策略，配合视角条件神经颜色场与高斯偏移网络，缓解了模板先验不足及独立优化高斯颜色导致的伪影问题，提升了头部化身的几何表达力与外观保真度。摘要未明确提及局限性，但双阶段切换流程及参数约束可能带来额外的训练复杂度与超参依赖。

Abstract: Accurate head modeling requires a stable yet expressive geometric representation. Existing Gaussian-based head avatars commonly rely on parametric templates (e.g., FLAME) for Gaussian initialization and deformation, but these templates lack personalized priors and struggle to represent structures such as hair and clothing. To address this issue, we propose MGAvatar, a Gaussian-mesh hybrid representation that jointly models geometry and appearance through two Gaussian-mesh binding modes. Specifically, we introduce vertex-bound Gaussians and constrain their learnable parameters, enabling progressive mesh deformation to represent complex head geometry, while a pose-dependent offset module accounts for non-rigid deformations. Once geometry is stabilized, MGAvatar switches to face-bound Gaussians for appearance modeling. To improve appearance consistency across novel poses and viewpoints, we introduce a view-conditioned neural color field that alleviates artifacts caused by independently optimized Gaussian colors. In addition, we design a Gaussian offset network to predict Gaussian offset maps in the observation space, providing greater flexibility for face-bound Gaussians to capture dynamic facial textures. Extensive experiments on multi-view and monocular videos show that MGAvatar outperforms existing methods in rendering quality, producing high-fidelity head avatars with rich texture details.

</details>


### [57] [3D CT-to-PET Translation via Latent Brownian Bridge Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12860)
*Sarita Mourya,Francesco Di Feola,Pierangelo Veltri,Paolo Soda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于潜空间布朗桥扩散模型（BBDM）的两阶段3D CT-to-PET翻译框架：先训练带对比学习的VAE实现CT-PET潜表示对齐，再在潜空间用BBDM将CT潜表示翻译为PET潜表示，解码拼接重建3D PET体，提升PET信号保真度并更好保留小病灶代谢激活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PET提供代谢功能信息，与CT的解剖信息互补，对肿瘤诊断与放疗计划至关重要。但PET受限于高辐射剂量、高成本与设备可及性差，难以普及；而CT更易获取、成本低、辐射相对可控。因此，从CT直接合成PET-like信息（CT-to-PET翻译）成为有吸引力的替代方案。然而CT与PET之间存在巨大的跨模态鸿沟（解剖形态 vs. 代谢功能），准确建模该映射尤为困难，现有深度学习方法在此问题上仍有不足。

Method: 提出两阶段3D CT-to-PET翻译框架，核心为潜空间布朗桥扩散模型（Latent Brownian Bridge Diffusion, BBDM）。第一阶段：在配对的CT-PET图像块（patches）上训练变分自编码器（VAE），并引入对比学习（contrastive learning）以增强解剖表示与代谢表示之间的潜空间对齐。第二阶段：在VAE潜空间中训练BBDM，学习从CT潜表示到对应PET潜表示的翻译映射。最后将翻译得到的PET潜表示解码并拼接（stitching），重建完整3D PET体数据。选择BBDM而非标准扩散模型，因其布朗桥过程可直接建模两个域之间的桥接转换，更契合配对数据的跨模态翻译任务；采用patch与潜空间建模则缓解3D体数据的高计算开销。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上进行评估。评价指标涵盖两类：图像保真度（image fidelity）指标与病灶级别的PET专用指标（lesion-level PET-specific metrics）。定量结果表明，所提方法相比竞争方法性能更优：PET信号保真度提升，临床相关摄取值模式（uptake patterns）保留更好，并在保留小病灶代谢激活方面表现更佳。

Conclusion: 主要贡献为：将布朗桥扩散模型引入潜空间以解决3D CT-to-PET跨模态翻译，结合对比学习实现解剖-代谢潜表示对齐，并在病灶级评价中验证了对小病灶代谢激活的保留能力，为虚拟成像（virtual imaging）应用提供路径。意义在于有望降低对实际PET扫描的依赖，减少辐射暴露与成本。潜在局限包括：依赖配对CT-PET数据训练、仅在两个公开数据集上验证、摘要未报告具体指标数值与计算开销、patch级拼接可能引入体数据边界一致性问题。

Abstract: Computed tomography (CT) and positron emission tomography (PET) provide complementary anatomical and functional information for cancer diagnosis and treatment planning. However, the widespread use of PET is limited by high radiation exposure, elevated costs, and restricted availability. To address these limitations, deep learning-based CT-to-PET translation has emerged as a promising approach for synthesizing PET-like information directly from CT images, although accurately modeling the large cross-modal gap remains challenging. In this work, we propose a 3D CT-to-PET translation framework based on latent Brownian Bridge Diffusion (BBDM). The method consists of two stages. First, a Variational Autoencoder (VAE) is trained on paired CT-PET patches, integrating contrastive learning to improve latent alignment between anatomical and metabolic representations. Second, a BBDM is trained in the latent space to translate CT latent representations into their corresponding PET counterparts. The translated PET latents are then decoded and stitched to reconstruct the final 3D PET volume. We evaluate the proposed approach on two publicly available datasets. Quantitative results based on image fidelity and lesion-level PET-specific metrics demonstrate improved performance compared with competing methods. In particular, the proposed approach improves PET signal fidelity, better preserves clinically relevant uptake patterns, and shows improved performance in preserving small-lesion metabolic activation, paving the way for virtual imaging applications.

</details>


### [58] [VideoTok4D: A 4D-Aware Video Tokenizer for Compact World Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12874)
*Xinyi Chen,Hanxin Zhu,Xijun Wang,Xingrui Wang,Sen Liang,Xin Li,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoTok4D 提出了一种面向 4D 世界建模的视频 tokenizer，通过将视频分解为静态与动态 token，并引入轨迹对齐的动态注意力机制，实现紧凑的 4D 场景表示，配合 Co4DGen 扩散先验支持高效 4D 场景生成，在保持 SOTA 性能的同时将存储需求降低最多 4 个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频 tokenizer 大多局限于 2D 视觉域，将视频视为图像序列而非对底层动态 3D 世界的观测，导致学习到的 token 继承了"观测中心"偏差，难以紧凑地表示真实的 4D 场景。这一问题在需要世界建模、跨视角一致性及高效生成的场景中尤为关键，因为稠密 4D 表示通常带来极高的存储与计算开销，限制了可扩展性。

Method: 方法包含三个核心设计：(1) 时空解耦策略，将视频分解为静态 token 与动态 token，以实现整体性的世界建模；(2) 轨迹感知的动态注意力机制，聚合轨迹对齐线索，促进跨视角的运动一致性；(3) Co4DGen，一种在 VideoTok4D token 空间上学习的扩散先验，用于高效的 4D 场景生成。整体框架将视频映射到紧凑的 4D 感知隐空间，使 token 携带世界级动态信息而非单纯的观测信息。

Result: 论文进行了大量实验，结果表明该方法达到 state-of-the-art 性能，同时相比稠密 4D 表示所需存储量最多减少 4 个数量级。此外，所构建的紧凑 token 空间显著缩短了扩散序列长度，从而支持高效的 4D 场景生成。摘要未给出具体数据集名称、评测指标数值与消融细节。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出 4D 感知的视频 tokenizer 范式，通过时空解耦、轨迹感知注意力与扩散先验，实现对真实 4D 场景的紧凑表示与高效生成，并大幅降低存储开销。其意义在于推动视频 tokenization 从 2D 观测中心转向 4D 世界建模。潜在局限在于摘要中未披露具体数据集、评估协议与定量结果，且跨视角运动一致性在稀疏或退化视角条件下的鲁棒性仍有待验证。

Abstract: Video tokenizers have emerged as a cornerstone of modern video modeling, underpinning progress in compression, reconstruction and generation by mapping high-dimensional visual signals into compact latent spaces. However, despite this progress, current tokenization paradigms largely remain within the 2D visual domain, treating videos as image sequences rather than observations of an underlying dynamic 3D world. Consequently, the learned tokens inherit this observation-centric bias, limiting their capacity to compactly represent real-world 4D scenes. To mitigate this issue, we propose VideoTok4D, a novel 4D-aware video tokenizer for compact world representation. Specifically, our approach comprises three key designs: 1) a spatiotemporal disentanglement strategy that factorizes videos into static and dynamic tokens for holistic world modeling; 2) a track-aware dynamic attention mechanism that aggregates trajectory-aligned cues to promote cross-view motion consistency; and 3) Co4DGen, a diffusion prior learned over the resulting VideoTok4D token space for efficient 4D scene generation. Extensive experiments have demonstrated that our proposed method achieves state-of-the-art performance while requiring up to 4 orders of magnitude less storage than dense 4D representations. Moreover, the compact token space substantially shortens diffusion sequences, enabling efficient generation.

</details>


### [59] [Learning Sign Language Recognition under Label Noise: A Study of Noise-Robust Losses for Isolated and Continuous Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12885)
*Akihisa Shitara,Yoichi Ochiai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文探讨在孤立手语识别(ISLR)与连续手语识别(CSLR)中，将图像分类中鲁棒的对称交叉熵(SCE)和广义交叉熵(GCE)应用于单标签分类器（而非CTC），以缓解标注风格差异和伪标签噪声问题。核心发现是：在ISLR上SCE/GCE在噪声下优于CE，但增益的稳定性与可归因性存疑；在CSLR上未观察到收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手语识别面临两类标签噪声：ISLR中单一标签被当作唯一真值，但手语存在风格差异（stylistic variation），使标注被模糊化；CSLR缺乏时间边界，只能依赖伪标签训练帧级辅助分类器（auxiliary classifier），伪标签本身带噪。论文动机是检验图像分类中抗噪的损失函数(SCE/GCE)能否迁移到这些单标签分类器上提升鲁棒性，并特别关注"增益是否真实"这一问题。

Method: 提出的方法不改变模型架构，而是替换损失函数：将SCE（对称交叉熵，结合CE的梯度与RCE的鲁棒性）和GCE（广义交叉熵，通过参数q调节鲁棒性）用于ISLR的单标签分类器，以及CSLR中帧级伪标签辅助分类器的前级单标签分类器（不是用于CTC）。动机上，SCE被期望比GCE更稳定、超参跨模型可迁移。研究设计包含噪声注入实验与对超参q、辅助损失权重lambda_aux、有效梯度的分析。

Result: 在ASL Citizen数据集上注入对称噪声，测试三个骨干（ST-GCN用三个随机种子）。结果：干净标签下鲁棒损失最多损失2.5个百分点；噪声率0.2时，六个条件下SCE/GCE均比CE高2.9-10.0个百分点（其中一个条件仅在dev上重选q后成立）。GCE增益更大，但其最优q无法跨骨干迁移，而某个SCE设置可在全部九个条件下使用；两者跨运行方差比CE大2-11倍。CSLR（PHOENIX-2014）未获收益，帧级目标存在系统性分配偏差，该部分仅为单配置诊断。在lambda_aux=25时，伪标签CE辅助使VAC、CorrNet、SlowFastSign的WER高于无辅助基线；GCE/SCE比CE改善1.7-3.2个百分点（六个条件中三个低于该基线）。但三种损失在相同lambda_aux下有效梯度相差一个数量级以上，匹配初始梯度后差距缩小到0.4-0.9个百分点，且单独降低CE权重即可超过基线，故退化与改进无法与权重效应分离。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是系统性地检验鲁棒损失在ISLR/CSLR中的效果，并揭示：（1）在ISLR噪声下SCE/GCE确有优势，且SCE的超参更具跨骨干可迁移性和稳定性；（2）CSLR中无收益，源于伪标签分配偏差；（3）辅助损失的"改进"很大程度上可由有效梯度/权重效应解释，无法独立归因。局限：仅使用对称噪声，多随机种子评估仅覆盖ST-GCN与VAC孤立设置，实验范围有限，结论不宜过度外推。

Abstract: In sign language recognition, the isolated (ISLR) classification loss treats a single label as ground truth, as does the frame-level auxiliary classifier over pseudo-labels we add to continuous (CSLR) methods, which lack one. Stylistic variation blurs ISLR annotation and the lack of temporal boundaries in CSLR forces pseudo-labels; both are noisy. We therefore apply symmetric and generalized cross entropy (SCE, GCE), robust alternatives to cross entropy (CE) from image classification, not to connectionist temporal classification but to the preceding single-label classifier. On ASL Citizen with injected symmetric noise on three backbones (three seeds for ST-GCN), robust losses cost at most 2.5 pt when labels are clean and beat CE by 2.9-10.0 pt in all six conditions at noise rate 0.2, one of which only after q was re-selected on dev. GCE gains more, but its optimal q does not transfer across backbones, whereas one SCE setting works in all nine conditions; both vary 2-11 times more than CE across runs, so a favorable point estimate does not establish stability. For CSLR (PHOENIX-2014) we report no gain; our frame-level targets carry a systematic assignment bias, making that study a diagnosis of a single configuration. At lambda_aux = 25 the pseudo-label CE auxiliary raises word error rate above the no-auxiliary baseline on VAC, CorrNet and SlowFastSign, and GCE/SCE improve on CE by 1.7-3.2 pt (three of six conditions return below that baseline). However, the three losses differ by more than an order of magnitude in effective gradient at a common lambda_aux: matching the initial gradient shrinks the gap to 0.4-0.9 pt, and lowering the CE weight alone already beats that baseline, so neither the degradation nor the improvement can be separated from the effect of the weight. We use only symmetric noise; multi-seed evaluation covers only ST-GCN and VAC isolated.

</details>


### [60] [UniPart: Towards Zero-shot Language-Grounded 3D Part Segmentation for Embodied Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12898)
*Xinqiang Yu,Zekun qi,Jiawei He,Wenyao Zhang,Xuchuan Chen,Guaocai Yao,Li Yi,Zhaoxiang Zhang,He Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniPart 是一个前馈式跨模态 3D Transformer，通过 CLIP 文本嵌入实现对点云的开放词汇、文本条件式部件（part）分割，并配套构建了大规模数据集 LangPart-1M（含 LangPart-4K 人工标注子集），在零样本部件分割基准上取得强结果并可迁移至真实机器人的语言条件抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度机器人操作需要理解物体的"部件"而非仅整体对象。现有 3D 基础模型存在两难：要么具备泛化能力但只做整体对象感知（object-aware），要么能感知部件但受限于固定闭集类别（closed-set taxonomies），导致零样本迁移能力弱。因此需要一个能由自由文本短语指定功能性部件、并具备开放词汇泛化能力的 3D 部件分割方案；该问题对语言驱动的抓取与操作任务尤为重要。此外，监督数据稀缺也是关键瓶颈。

Method: 提出 UniPart：一个前馈（feed-forward）跨模态 3D Transformer，以 CLIP 文本嵌入作为条件，对点云进行文本条件式部件分割。为扩充监督，构建 LangPart-1M 数据集，覆盖 16 万+ Objaverse 资产、约 800 万（text-to-part）对，采用多视角一致的部件生成（multi-view consistent part generation）流程来产生部件标注。并人工标注高质量子集 LangPart-4K，用于微调与评测。方法核心是跨模态条件化与开放词汇文本对齐。

Result: 在开放词汇部件（open-vocabulary part）基准上，UniPart 取得较强的零样本（zero-shot）结果。借助 LangPart-4K 进行微调与评测。此外，模型迁移到真实世界的语言条件部件抓取（language-conditioned part grasping），验证了从分割到机器人操作的实际可用性。

Conclusion: 主要贡献为：面向开放词汇部件分割的文本条件 3D Transformer（UniPart）、大规模部件-文本数据集 LangPart-1M 及人工标注子集 LangPart-4K，并验证了向真实机器人语言条件抓取的迁移。意义在于弥合 3D 基础模型在泛化性与部件级感知之间的鸿沟，支撑细粒度操作。局限性（基于摘要可推断）：依赖 CLIP 文本嵌入的表达上限、数据主要来自合成 Objaverse 资产，真实世界泛化仍受域差距影响。

Abstract: Fine-grained robotic manipulation depends on understanding parts, not only whole objects. Existing 3D foundation models tend to be either generalized but object-aware, or part-aware but limited to closed-set taxonomies, which weakens zero-shot transfer. We study text-conditioned 3D part segmentation, where a free-form phrase selects a functional part on point cloud. We introduce UniPart, a feed-forward cross-modal 3D Transformer that conditions CLIP text embedding. To scale supervision, we build LangPart-1M with 160K+ Objaverse assets and 8M text to part pairs using multi-view consistent part generation. We further manually label a high-quality subset, LangPart-4K, for fine-tuning and evaluation. UniPart achieves strong zero-shot results on open-vocabulary part benchmarks and transfers to language-conditioned part grasping in real world.

</details>


### [61] [Beyond Accuracy: Uncertainty-Guided Boundary Refinement for Reliable Biomedical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12892)
*Anima Kujur*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可靠性感知边界细化网络 RABR-Net，一个两阶段分割框架：先用 UNet++ EfficientNet-B4 生成初始预测，再将熵、TTA 方差、margin 不确定性、概率梯度与软边界线索融合为边界可靠性表示，驱动门控残差细化器选择性修正不确定的边界像素。在血液涂片显微镜图像上提升了 Dice、Boundary Dice 并降低了 HD95。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学图像分割不仅需要高的全局重叠度（如 Dice），还需要对临床有意义的边界进行可靠勾画。在血液涂片显微镜图像中，细胞质与细胞核轮廓是下游形态学分析的结构基础。然而，即使深度分割模型达到很高的 Dice，其在模糊边界区域仍可能表现出不确定或过度自信的预测，导致边界定位不可靠。该工作旨在针对这种"全局指标好看但边界不可信"的问题，提供一种面向可靠性与边界敏感的分割策略。

Method: 采用两阶段框架。第一阶段：以 UNet++ 搭配 EfficientNet-B4 作为基础分割器，输出初始类别概率与 logits。第二阶段：构建边界感知的可靠性表示，融合多种不确定性/边界线索——预测熵、测试时增强（TTA）方差、margin 不确定性、概率梯度以及软边界线索。该可靠性表示驱动一个门控残差细化器（gated residual refiner），通过门控机制选择性地修正不确定的边界像素，同时保留基础预测中置信度高的区域。整体设计强调可解释性，学习到的门控应集中在不确定的细胞质与细胞核边界。

Result: 在留出测试集上进行评估，覆盖多维度指标：重叠精度、逐类 Dice、Boundary Dice、HD95/ASSD、校准（calibration）、风险--覆盖率分析、图像扰动下的鲁棒性、定性修正图以及配对统计检验。相较缓存的基础预测，方法将 Dice 从 0.9602 提升至 0.9614，Boundary Dice 从 0.3448 提升至 0.3611，HD95 从 3.0354 降至 2.8274。统计检验确认 Dice、Boundary Dice 与 HD95 的改进具有显著性。定性结果显示学到的门控集中在不确定的细胞质和细胞核边界，修正图证实了局部化的边界细化。但校准指标在细化后并未自动改善。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出一种可靠性感知、边界敏感的两阶段分割框架，将多种不确定性度量整合为边界可靠性表示，并通过门控残差细化实现选择性边界修正，在血液涂片分割上显著改善 Dice、Boundary Dice 与 HD95。其价值在于提供可解释、聚焦可靠性的分割范式，边界收益明显（Boundary Dice 相对提升较大）。局限包括：整体 Dice 提升幅度较小（约 0.0012），HD95 绝对值仍偏高，且细化未带来校准改善，说明不确定性建模与置信度校准之间尚未统一。

Abstract: Accurate biomedical image segmentation requires not only high global overlap but also reliable delineation of clinically meaningful boundaries. In blood-smear microscopy, cytoplasm and nucleus contours provide the structural basis for downstream morphology analysis; however, deep segmentation models may remain uncertain or overconfident near ambiguous boundary regions even when achieving strong Dice scores. This work proposes a Reliability-Aware Boundary Refinement Network (RABR-Net), a two-stage framework for trustworthy image segmentation. A strong UNet++ EfficientNet-B4 base segmenter first produces initial class probabilities and logits. Predictive entropy, test-time augmentation variance, margin uncertainty, probability gradients, and soft boundary cues are then combined into a boundary-aware reliability representation. This representation guides a gated residual refiner that selectively corrects uncertain boundary pixels while preserving confident regions of the base prediction. The framework is evaluated using overlap accuracy, class-wise Dice, Boundary Dice, HD95/ASSD, calibration, risk--coverage analysis, robustness under image perturbations, qualitative correction maps, and paired statistical testing. On the held-out test set, the proposed method improves Dice from 0.9602 to 0.9614, Boundary Dice from 0.3448 to 0.3611, and HD95 from 3.0354 to 2.8274 compared with the cached base prediction. Statistical analysis confirms significant improvements in Dice, Boundary Dice, and HD95. Qualitative results show that the learned gate concentrates around uncertain cytoplasm and nucleus boundaries, and correction maps confirm localized boundary refinement. Although calibration does not automatically improve after refinement, the proposed framework provides an interpretable and reliability-focused strategy for boundary-sensitive biomedical image segmentation.

</details>


### [62] [Parallel Training Using a CNN-DNN Architecture for Accelerated Development of Diagnostic Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12902)
*Janine Weber-Hamacher,Astha Jaiswal,Philipp Fervers,Dorotya Móré,Athanasios Giannakis,Ricarda Fischbach,Andreas Michael Bucher,Rahil Shahzad,Jonathan Kottlors,Thorsten Persigehl,Axel Klawonn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于图像分解与定位的混合 CNN-DNN 网络模型，将 CT 图像切分为子域并行训练，从而在新冠疫情场景下实现大规模深度学习模型的高效训练与 COVID-19 诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在疫情等场景下，疾病数据规模巨大且动态增长，传统串行训练大型深度学习模型效率低下，难以快速响应。论文旨在解决大规模医学影像数据集上模型训练效率低与诊断性能难以兼顾的问题，通过并行化训练加速模型开发，服务于疫情应急准备。

Method: 研究采用混合 CNN-DNN 架构，核心思想是图像分解与定位：将 CT 图像划分为子域（如 4×4×1、4×4×2），在子域上并行训练卷积网络提取特征，再由 DNN 进行患者级聚合诊断。方法天然支持并行与高效训练，可捕捉不同层级特征。共训练 156 个模型，涵盖三种架构（包括 DenseNet121 与 3D CNN），最终组合成 12 个患者级 COVID-19 诊断模型。

Result: 数据来自德国三个中心的 300 例 COVID-19 与非 COVID-19 肺炎患者 CT 扫描。最佳性能：DenseNet121 配合 4×4×1 子域达到 88.78% 训练、76.67% 验证、76.03% 测试准确率；3D CNN 配合 4×4×1 子域达到 87.72% 训练、76.82% 验证、74.86% 测试准确率。并行训练时间最大缩短 31 倍（3D CNN 配合 4×4×2 子域）。评估指标包括诊断准确率与训练时间节省。

Conclusion: 论文证明了基于图像分解与定位的并行混合 CNN-DNN 训练方法能在提升效率的同时保持甚至改善诊断性能，有助于快速模型开发与疫情应急准备。局限在于数据集规模较小（300 例 CT）且为回顾性研究，泛化性与临床实用性仍需更大规模前瞻性验证，且并行加速效果与子域粒度及架构相关。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence has shown promise in assisting radiologists in imaging-based diagnosis across a wide range of diseases. Efficient training of large deep learning models is essential to cope with extremely large data sets or dynamically growing disease data, like in a pandemic like situation. In this retrospective study, we collected 300 CT scans from COVID-19 and non-COVID-19 pneumonia patients from three different centers in Germany. We investigated a hybrid CNN-DNN network model based on image decomposition and localization that naturally supports parallel and efficient training of deep learning models. In total, 156 models with three different architectures were trained to capture features at different levels resulting in 12 patient-level COVID-19 diagnosis models. Diagnostic performance as well as time saving were measured.
  The highest accuracy was obtained from DenseNet121 and 3D CNN models with a parallel CNN-DNN approach, resulting in $88.78\%$ training, $76.67\%$ validation and $76.03\%$ test accuracy for the DenseNet121 with $4\times4\times1$ subdomains and $87.72\%$ training, $76.82\%$ validation and $74.86\%$ test accuracy, respectively, for the 3D CNN with $4\times4\times1$ subdomains. The strongest reduction in parallel training time by a factor of $31$ was observed for the 3D CNN model and $4\times4\times2$ subdomains.
  Our parallel training approach improves efficiency as well as performance enabling rapid model development, among others crucial for pandemic preparedness.

</details>


### [63] [PA-CDM: Position-Aware Character Detection Matching for Evaluating Handwritten Mathematical Expression Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12917)
*Shiliang Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出位置感知的手写数学表达式识别（HMER）评测指标 PA-CDM，将渲染式字符检测匹配与位置森林编码及散度级加权结合，同时构建受控扰动基准 StructPerturb v2.0 和跨指标一致性协议，以解决现有指标对误差位置不敏感的盲区问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 HMER 评测依赖精确匹配率和字符串相似度指标，这类指标对错误出现的位置不敏感：两个 token 错误数相同的预测会得到相同分数，无论其是把下标放错位置还是交换了分数的分子分母。渲染式字符检测匹配（CDM）虽能稳健对齐字形，但依然是位置盲的——在受控的分数操作数交换测试中仅得 0.8595，而位置感知评分则给出 0.6253，说明其掩盖了结构性错误。树编辑类指标则存在互补盲区：解析器归一化覆盖范围之外的改写会被当作结构性错误惩罚（得 0.8552，而渲染式指标给出 1.0）。因此迫切需要一种既能对齐字形又能惩罚位置敏感错误的评测方法。

Method: 论文提出三项贡献。其一，PA-CDM（位置感知的字符检测匹配）：将字符检测匹配（CDM）与位置森林编码（position-forest encoding）相耦合，并引入散度级加权（divergence-level weighting），使指标在保持字形对齐能力的同时对错误发生的位置敏感。其二，StructPerturb v2.0：一个冻结的受控扰动基准，包含跨 15 个"类型—强度"单元（type–intensity cells）的 1,340 个扰动对，用于系统性探测指标对特定错误类型和严重程度的响应。其三，跨指标一致性协议：由敏感度矩阵（sensitivity matrix）、人类标注研究与 LLM 评判校准三部分组成的评测框架，用于评估自动指标的可信度。

Result: 在六名标注者参与的人类研究中，PA-CDM 在七个自动指标中取得与人类判断最高的一致性（Spearman rho=0.9535，n=990）。前沿 LLM 评判器的相关性略高（rho=0.9613），但代价高昂、具有非确定性且依赖 API。PA-CDM 以零边际成本逼近 LLM 评判器水平，同时具备确定性、可诊断的行为，并在受控分数操作数交换等位置敏感场景中显著区别于位置盲的位置基准指标。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献在于揭示了主流 HMER 评测指标在误差位置与结构改写上的盲区，并提出位置感知指标 PA-CDM、受控扰动基准 StructPerturb v2.0 以及跨指标一致性协议。PA-CDM 在人类一致性上优于其他自动指标，并以低成本、确定性方式接近 LLM 评判器性能，为 HMER 评测提供了更可靠、可诊断的方案。潜在局限包括：受控扰动基准依赖人工设计的错误类型与强度划分，可能无法覆盖真实场景中的全部错误模式；人类研究样本规模（六名标注者、n=990）相对有限；位置森林编码与散度级加权的有效性仍需在更广泛的识别器与数据集上进一步验证。

Abstract: Handwritten mathematical expression recognition (HMER) is conventionally scored by exact-match rates and string-similarity metrics that are blind to where an error occurs: two predictions with identical token-error counts receive identical scores whether they misplace a subscript or swap the operands of a fraction. Render-based character detection matching (CDM) aligns glyphs robustly but remains position-blind---on controlled fraction-operand swaps it scores 0.8595 where position-aware scoring yields 0.6253. Tree-edit metrics exhibit a complementary blind spot: rewrites outside the parser's normalization coverage are penalized as structural errors (0.8552 where render-based metrics score 1.0). We propose PA-CDM, a position-aware metric that couples character detection matching with position-forest encoding and divergence-level weighting; StructPerturb v2.0, a frozen benchmark of 1,340 controlled perturbation pairs across 15 type--intensity cells; and a cross-metric consistency protocol combining a sensitivity matrix, a human study, and LLM-judge calibration. In a six-annotator study, PA-CDM attains the highest correlation with human judgments among seven automatic metrics (Spearman rho=0.9535, n=990). A frontier LLM judge correlates slightly higher (rho=0.9613) but is costly, nondeterministic, and API-dependent; PA-CDM approaches it at zero marginal cost with deterministic, diagnosable behavior.

</details>


### [64] [Input Resolution Matters: Real-Time Object Detection Latency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12920)
*Qingyang Zhang,Fumio Machida,Laura Carnevali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种将目标检测端到端总延迟建模为预处理、推理、后处理三个阶段延迟分布卷积的轻量级概率框架，并将各阶段分布参数表示为源图像分辨率的函数。通过在 NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX 上运行 YOLOv11n 对 COCO2017 图像多分辨率实验，验证分辨率感知参数化相比固定参数基线能更好地逼近实测延迟分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实时目标检测与边缘部署（如无人机、机器人感知）中，端到端延迟不仅影响吞吐，还决定检测的时序一致性与最坏情况响应。传统工作多报告平均延迟或单点指标，忽略了延迟的随机性与分布特性，且很少刻画延迟随输入分辨率变化的规律。理解分辨率如何影响延迟分布，对系统设计、分辨率自适应调度与实时性保障具有重要意义，但现有方法缺乏显式且可解析的建模手段。

Method: 核心方法是将总延迟视为三个阶段（预处理、推理、后处理）延迟分布的卷积，基于阶段间独立性近似，使总延迟的概率密度为各阶段密度之卷积，其累积分布函数（CDF）即端到端检测时间的分布。每个阶段用参数化分布建模（如指数分布 Exponential、Erlang、正态 Normal、Gamma），并将分布参数显式表达为源图像分辨率的函数，从而实现分辨率感知的参数化。该建模保持理论可解释性与计算轻量性，直接作用于被测检测流水线，无需修改网络结构。

Result: 实验在 NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX 上以 YOLOv11n 检测器为对象，使用 COCO2017 图像在多种分辨率下采集延迟数据。评价采用 Kolmogorov-Smirnov、Anderson-Darling 与 Cramér-von Mises 三种拟合优度统计量，将所提分辨率感知参数化模型与固定参数基线进行对比。结果显示，分辨率感知参数化在实测环境下能改善分布逼近效果，尤其对灵活性更高的 Normal 与 Gamma 模型更明显；但拟合质量仍依赖具体分布，未出现对所有情形一致的绝对优势。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是提出一个有理论依据、轻量化的框架，用于研究目标检测流水线中依赖分辨率的延迟分布，将传统点估计延迟提升为分布层面的刻画。意义在于为实时与边缘部署系统提供分辨率自适应的延迟建模与风险评估工具。局限包括：阶段独立性为简化近似，可能不严格成立；参数化分布为经验选择，拟合质量分布依赖；实验仅覆盖单一检测器（YOLOv11n）与单一硬件平台（Jetson Orin NX）及 COCO2017 数据，泛化能力有待更广泛验证。

Abstract: We model total latency as the convolution of preprocessing, inference, and postprocessing distributions under a simplifying independence approximation, with selected stage parameters expressed as functions of source-image resolution. Under this assumption, the probability density of the total latency is the convolution of the stage-wise densities, and its cumulative distribution function (CDF) provides the distribution of end-to-end detection time. Each stage is modeled by a parametric distribution (e.g., Exponential, Erlang, Normal, Gamma), with parameters expressed as functions of the source-image resolution. Experiments with YOLOv11n on NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX using COCO2017 images across multiple resolutions assess the proposed models against fixed-parameter baselines using Kolmogorov Smirnov, Anderson Darling, and Cramér von Mises statistics. The results indicate that resolution-aware parameterization can improve distributional approximation in the measured setting, particularly for the more flexible Normal and Gamma models, while the quality of fit remains distribution dependent. Our contribution is a theoretically grounded and lightweight formulation for studying resolution-dependent latency distributions in a measured object detection pipeline.

</details>


### [65] [DementiaCare-Bench: A Modality-Validated Video Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12929)
*Afrouz Sheikholeslami,Yuankai Qi,Xuyun Zhang,Luping Zhou,Amin Beheshti,Quan Z. Sheng,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 DementiaCare-Bench，这是首个针对视频-语言模型（VLM）理解痴呆症行为与心理症状（BPSD）及其前置诱因能力的专业基准。基准包含 94 个专业护理培训视频片段、9 类 BPSD 与 2023 个多智能体生成的问题，并通过四种视觉条件下的"最小所需信息"标注，量化问题的真实视觉依赖程度；同时用轻量 LoRA 微调模型 DemCare-VLM 证明该基准暴露的缺陷可被修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球约 5700 万痴呆症患者，照护中最困难的并非记忆丧失，而是激越、游走、拒护、日落综合征等 BPSD。正确应对 BPSD 不仅需要识别行为本身，还需理解行为发生前的诱因——相同行为因触发情境不同应有不同回应。现有 VLM 或许能辅助照护者，但尚无基准评测这种"时序—诱因—应答"能力，导致该能力是否真实存在缺乏量化依据。

Method: 基准构建：56 个专业制作的照护培训视频切分为 94 个片段，覆盖 9 类 BPSD；由多智能体流水线生成 2023 个问题，每条临床主张均锚定到逐字转录文本片段（grounding）。视觉需求测量：每个问题在四种视觉条件（如无图像/乱序/有序片段等）下探测，并按"回答所需最小条件"标注，从而测量而非假设其视觉依赖。评测覆盖 12 个当前 VLM。修复方案：DemCare-VLM，通过轻量 LoRA 微调注入时序视频依赖能力。

Result: 主要指标为各视觉条件下的准确率与视频依赖增益（有序片段带来的提升）。作者设计时认为 77.7% 的问题需要有序帧，但实测仅 34.8% 确实需要。12 个 VLM 表现一致：最佳模型总体约 85%，但该均值主要由可凭临床知识回答的问题支撑；需要有序片段的问题上平均下降 17 分，某领先开源模型在判断"照护者应对是否恰当"上仅为随机水平。LoRA 微调后 DemCare-VLM 将视频依赖从 -3.3 分提升至 +4.5 分，表明该缺陷可修复。

Conclusion: 贡献在于：首个面向 BPSD 时序-诱因理解的视频-语言基准、以逐字转录锚定的临床问题生成流水线、以及按最小视觉条件衡量视觉需求的评测范式，并证明通过 LoRA 可恢复视频依赖。意义在于揭示当前 VLM 在真实照护推理中高度依赖语言先验、时序建模薄弱。局限在于基准规模与领域较窄（照护培训视频）、微调仅在单一基准验证，且涉及临床主张，泛化至真实照护场景与更广 VLM 仍需进一步评估。

Abstract: Dementia affects an estimated 57 million people worldwide, and for most families the hardest part of care is not memory loss but the behavioral and psychological symptoms of dementia (BPSD): agitation, wandering, resistance to care, sundowning. Understanding these symptoms requires more than recognizing the behavior itself; it also requires knowing what happened beforehand. The same behavior may call for a different response depending on its trigger. Video-language models (VLMs) could potentially support caregivers, yet no existing benchmark evaluates this capability. To fill this gap, we present DementiaCare-Bench: 56 professionally produced caregiver training videos segmented into 94 clips across nine BPSD categories, with 2023 questions generated by a multi-agent pipeline that grounds every clinical claim in a verbatim transcript span. Each question is then probed under four visual conditions and labelled by the least it requires, so its visual demand is measured rather than assumed. Measurement contradicts intent: we wrote 77.7% of the questions to require ordered frames, and 34.8% do. Across 12 current VLMs the pattern is uniform. The best reach 85% overall, but that average is carried by questions a language model can answer from clinical knowledge alone; accuracy falls by 17 points on average on questions that require the ordered clip, and a leading open model scores at chance on judging whether a caregiver's response was appropriate. A lightweight LoRA fine-tune, DemCare-VLM, moves video dependence from -3.3 to +4.5 points, so what the benchmark exposes can be repaired and not only measured.

</details>


### [66] [Fast and Faithful: Principled Conditional Flow Matching for Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12953)
*Shirin Shoushtari,Edward P. Chandler,Xiao Shi,Ulugbek S. Kamilov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种面向成像逆问题的 measurement-conditional 速度场参数化方法，将条件速度 v(x_t,t,y) 显式表示为后验均值 E[x_1|x_t,y] 的函数，并证明后验均值是带有显式数据一致性项的变分目标的唯一极小值点；由此把该目标拆解为含算子相关数据一致性更新的条件速度参数化，并在 flow matching 目标下端到端训练，推理时无需额外 guidance。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow matching 在求解成像逆问题（如超分、去噪、重建等）中已被广泛应用，但现有方法在引入测量信息 y 时存在明显缺陷：一类 conditioning-based 方法将测量信息作为网络输入（如拼接）直接送入模型，前向模型并未被显式约束在学到的条件速度场内部；另一类 inference-guided 方法则用无条件速度场叠加独立的数据一致性更新步，两阶段分离导致模型本身并不"理解"前向物理模型。两者共同的问题在于：学到的条件速度场没有显式强制满足前向测量模型，物理一致性依赖于外部启发式或推理期补偿，从而可能需要大量采样步数、且性能受 guidance 设计限制。该问题重要是因为精确、高效且物理一致的求解对 MRI、CT、稀疏视角成像等实际成像任务至关重要。

Method: 核心创新在于对条件速度场的"原理化参数化"。在线性插值路径下，作者将条件速度 v(x_t,t,y) 表达为后验均值 E[x_1|x_t,y] 的函数，并将该后验均值刻画为一个变分目标的唯一极小值点，该目标包含显式的数据一致性项，使前向算子（forward model）被显式纳入模型学习范围。进一步，作者证明该速度场定义了从源分布到 measurement-conditioned 后验分布的概率流（probability flow），为方法提供理论保证。通过对变分目标进行拆分，得到一种带有算子相关（operator-dependent）数据一致性更新的条件速度参数化形式，整个模型在 flow matching 目标下端到端训练，推理时无需任何额外 guidance（no additional guidance at inference）。此外，作者声称改变采样步数可在不重新训练的情况下，实现对 distortion-perception 权衡的 test-time 控制。

Result: 论文报告其方法取得了 state-of-the-art 的 PSNR，且所需函数评估次数（NFE）比最强的 flow baseline 少 50 倍，说明该方法在采样效率上显著优于现有 flow matching 逆问题求解器。通过调节采样步数，可在不重新训练的前提下实现对 distortion-perception 权衡的测试时控制。摘要中未给出具体数据集、任务名称或除 PSNR/NFE 之外的评价指标细节。在高效、实时的成像逆问题求解这一方向上，50× 的 NFE 缩减具有较明显的意义。

Conclusion: 主要贡献包括：(1) 提出 measurement-conditional 速度场的原理化参数化，将后验均值与显式数据一致性变分目标联系起来；(2) 证明条件速度场构成到测量条件后验的概率流；(3) 通过目标拆分实现 operator-dependent 数据一致性更新与 flow matching 的端到端训练，推理时无需额外 guidance；(4) 在 PSNR 与采样效率（50× 更少 NFE）上达到 state-of-the-art，并支持测试时 distortion-perception 权衡控制。局限性方面：理论分析建立在线性插值与前向算子（可能隐含线性假设）之上，摘要未说明其向非线性前向模型的推广能力；实验部分仅提及 PSNR 和 NFE，未给出感知指标、具体数据集及任务覆盖范围，实际适用性有待完整论文进一步验证。

Abstract: Flow matching approaches to imaging inverse problems commonly incorporate measurements in two ways. Conditioning-based approaches supply measurement-derived information as a network input, often through concatenation, while inference-guided approaches combine an unconditional velocity field with a separate data-consistency update. In these common formulations, the forward model is not explicitly enforced within the learned conditional velocity field. We propose a principled parametrization of the measurement-conditional velocity field to solve inverse problems. Under linear interpolation, we express the conditional velocity $v(x_t,t,y)$ in terms of the posterior mean $E[x_1 | x_t,y]$, and characterize that mean as the unique minimizer of a variational objective whose data-consistency term is explicit. We further prove that the velocity field defines a probability flow from the source distribution to the measurement-conditioned posterior. Splitting the variational objective yields a conditional velocity parameterization with operator-dependent data-consistency updates, which we train end-to-end under the flow-matching objective, with no additional guidance at inference. Our method achieves state-of-the-art PSNR with $50\times$ fewer function evaluations than the strongest flow baseline. Varying the sampling steps provides test-time control over the distortion-perception trade-off without retraining.

</details>


### [67] [Generative Retrieval for Unsupervised Text-Based Person Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12965)
*Mang Ye,Yucheng Ji,Yang Bai,Min Cao,Siyuan Chai,Bo Du,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 GTR+，一种面向无监督文本行人搜索（TBPS）的"先生成后检索"两阶段框架：生成阶段用三层递进的文本描述生成机制合成细粒度且风格多样的伪文本；检索阶段用基于高斯混合模型的置信度加权学习抑制噪声伪文本，并贡献了大规模细粒度 TBPS 数据集 LargeFine-Person。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本行人搜索旨在依据自然语言描述从大规模图像库中检索目标行人。现有方法大多依赖监督学习，需要大量人工标注的图像-文本配对，标注成本高昂且难以扩展。论文探索仅使用无标注图像的无监督 TBPS 设定，其核心难点在于：缺少真实文本监督信号，只能依赖自动生成的伪文本，而伪文本存在细粒度不足、风格单一以及噪声标签误导训练的问题。解决该问题有助于降低标注依赖，提升 TBPS 的可扩展性与泛化能力。

Method: 提出 GTR+，两阶段"生成-检索"框架。
（1）生成阶段：三层递进式描述生成框架（tiered description generation）。基础层（base tier）通过自动问答机制（automated question-and-answer）生成基础视觉属性描述；中间层（intermediate tier）利用样本间对比机制（inter-sample contrastive mechanism）增强细粒度细节；高级层（advanced tier）通过风格化扩展机制（stylized expansion）提升文本多样性。
（2）检索阶段：针对噪声伪文本，设计自适应置信度加权检索学习框架（adaptive confidence-weighted retrieval learning）。用高斯混合模型（GMM）将图文对建模为干净/噪声两类，并由两路信号进行校准：一是实时的图像-文本相似度，二是生成阶段得到的静态文本生成概率，从而在训练中产生自适应样本权重。
（3）数据集贡献：构建 LargeFine-Person，一个大规模 TBPS 数据集，具备高质量、细粒度、多样化的文本标注，为无监督设定下的 TBPS 预训练提供实用且可泛化的基准。

Result: 论文在多个 TBPS 基准数据集上进行实验，验证了 GTR+ 与 LargeFine-Person 的有效性和泛化能力（摘要未给出具体数据集名称、评价指标如 mAP/Rank-1 的数值结果）。代码已开源：https://github.com/Flame-Chasers/GTR。

Conclusion: 主要贡献包括：首次（或系统性地）探索仅用无标注图像的无监督 TBPS；提出三层递进式伪文本生成机制以提升描述的细粒度与多样性；提出基于 GMM 与双重校准的自适应置信度加权学习以缓解伪文本噪声；并发布大规模、细粒度、多样标注的 LargeFine-Person 数据集。其意义在于降低 TBPS 对人工标注的依赖，并为无监督 TBPS 预训练提供基准。潜在局限在于：伪文本质量仍受生成机制与底层视觉模型能力制约，GMM 对干净/噪声的二分假设可能过于简化，且摘要未提供定量结果以充分支撑方法的性能上限。

Abstract: Text-based person search (TBPS) aims to retrieve images of a target person from a large image gallery based on a given natural language description. Most existing methods rely on supervised learning with manually annotated image-text pairs. In this paper, we explore unsupervised TBPS, with only unlabeled images. We propose GTR+, a two-stage generation-then-retrieval framework. In the generation stage, we introduce a tiered description generation framework designed to produce fine-grained and stylistically diverse textual descriptions through a three-tier sequential process. The base tier leverages an automated question-and-answer mechanism to generate basic visual attribute descriptions; the intermediate tier enhances fine-grained detail using an inter-sample contrastive mechanism; the advanced tier further enriches textual diversity via a stylized expansion mechanism. In the retrieval stage, to mitigate the impact of noisy pseudo texts, we develop an adaptive confidence-weighted retrieval learning framework. We model image-text pairs as clean or noisy using a Gaussian Mixture Model, calibrated by real-time image-text similarity and static text generation probability from the prior stage, yielding adaptive sample weights during training. Beyond that, we also contribute LargeFine-Person, a large-scale TBPS dataset with high-quality, fine-grained, and diverse textual annotations, enabling a practical and generalizable TBPS pre-training benchmark under unsupervised setting. Experiments on multiple TBPS benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness and generalization of both GTR+ and LargeFine-Person. Code is available at: https://github.com/Flame-Chasers/GTR.

</details>


### [68] [Investigating Temporal Motion Features for Pose-to-Text Indian Sign Language Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12993)
*Manav Dhamecha,Praveen Kumar Chandaliya,Pruthwik Mishra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究预训练T5模型规模与显式运动特征对姿态到文本的印度手语翻译（SLT）的影响，面向WSLP 2026 Shared Task。核心发现：在空间姿态特征基础上显式加入帧间姿态差分（运动特征）能带来最大幅度的BLEU提升，使T5-small + Motion成为整体最强模型；系统在官方测试榜排名第5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手语翻译（SLT）旨在将手语视频/姿态序列自动转写为文本，对无障碍交流具有重要意义。手语的关键信息不仅包含静态手型与空间构型（spatial），还包含运动的轨迹与动态变化（motion），而许多基于姿态的方法仅建模单帧姿态的空间结构，忽略了显式的时间运动信息。同时，预训练语言模型规模（small/base/large）对下游微调效果的影响尚不明确。作者因此在WSLP 2026共享任务设定下系统考察这两个因素：模型规模与显式运动特征，以确定在有限姿态数据下更有效的建模范式。

Method: 方法采用"姿态编码器 + 预训练T5"的编码器-解码器范式：
- 姿态序列通过一个轻量级姿态编码器投影到T5的嵌入空间，随后对完整模型进行端到端微调以生成英文文本。
- 对比三种预训练规模：T5-small、T5-base、T5-large，仅改变解码器/语言模型容量。
- 提出运动增强变体 T5-small + Motion：在输入表示中显式加入帧间姿态差分（frame-to-frame pose differences），即将相邻帧姿态的差作为运动特征与原始空间姿态拼接/融合，从而向模型注入显式的时间动态信息，而非仅依赖编码器隐式建模时序。
- 该运动特征作为一种直接的、低成本的时序表示增强，属于姿态级（pose-based）SLT中显式运动建模的轻量化设计。

Result: 数据来自WSLP 2026共享任务，验证集5,257例，测试集5,334例。评估指标包括BLEU、ROUGE和chrF。主要结果：
- 在仅使用空间特征的模型中，T5-small取得最佳BLEU与ROUGE，而T5-large取得最高chrF，说明模型规模增大并不在所有指标上单调获益。
- 在T5-small上加入运动特征（T5-small + Motion）带来本研究中最显著的单点提升，BLEU相对空间基线大幅提高，使其成为BLEU指标上整体最强的模型。
- 提交系统在官方WSLP 2026 SLT测试排行榜位列第5。源码与训练模型已在GitHub与HuggingFace公开。

Conclusion: 主要贡献：(1) 在WSLP 2026共享任务上系统比较了T5规模对姿态到文本印度手语翻译的影响；(2) 提出并验证了显式运动特征（帧间姿态差分）的有效性，证明其对BLEU的提升超过单纯扩大模型规模，表明在姿态SLT中显式的时序/运动表示比模型容量更重要。意义在于为手语翻译及类似姿态序列到文本任务提供了低成本、有效的运动增强方案，并提供了可复现的公开代码与模型。局限：仅针对印度手语与WSLP 2026数据集，泛化性有待验证；规模结果未体现一致的单调趋势，需进一步分析；运动特征仅为简单帧间差分，未采用更复杂的时间建模（如光流式编码、时序注意力）；最终排名第5，与最优系统仍有差距，未报告与其它竞赛系统的详细对比或消融分析范围。

Abstract: We investigate the effect of pretrained T5 model scale and explicit motion features on pose-to-text Indian Sign Language Translation (SLT) for the WSLP 2026 Shared Task. Pose sequences are projected into the embedding space of T5 through a lightweight pose encoder, with the complete model fine-tuned to generate English text. The shared task data used for this work consists of a test set with 5,334 examples and a validation set with 5,257 examples. We compare T5-small, T5-base, and T5-large, and additionally introduce a motion-augmented variant, T5-small + Motion, that adds explicit frame-to-frame pose differences to the input representation. T5-small achieves the best BLEU and ROUGE scores among the spatial-only models, while T5-large obtains the highest chrF score. Augmenting T5-small with motion features yields the largest single improvement observed in our study, substantially improving BLEU over the spatial-only baseline and making it the strongest model overall on this metric. Our submitted system ranked 5th on the official WSLP 2026 SLT testing leaderboard. The source code and trained models are publicly available on GitHub and HuggingFace.

</details>


### [69] [SV-Cine: Diagnosis-Conditioned Segmentation of Single Ventricle Physiology via Generative Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12997)
*Lila Cunge,Yuehong Liu,Hang Xu,Thomas Coudert,Pierangelo Renella,J Paul Finn,William Hsu,Kim-Lien Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出面向单心室生理（SVP）心脏MRI的分割框架：一方面用SDF4CHD合成3D心脏网格并结合生成模型生成合成MRI做数据增强，另一方面提出SV-Cine——在心脏基础模型CineMA中通过FiLM层注入患者级诊断信息，实现诊断条件化的特征自适应，从而提升SVP这一罕见畸形下的心室与心肌分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SVP是先天性心脏病中的罕见亚型，仅有一个功能性心室，解剖构型高度非典型，使常规心脏图像分割方法失效。同时临床数据稀缺、各SVP亚型形态差异大，导致训练稳健的分割模型非常困难。因此需要能在小样本、强形态异质的条件下，仍可准确分割心室腔与心肌的方法；这对心功能评估（如射血分数）具有临床意义。

Method: 方法包含两部分。第一是数据增强流水线：基于SDF4CHD生成合成的3D心脏网格，并借助生成式建模将这些结构转化为对应的合成心脏MRI，以缓解真实临床数据稀缺问题。第二是SV-Cine模型：以心脏基础模型CineMA为骨干，将其改造为“诊断条件化”的适配形式，通过Feature-wise Linear Modulation（FiLM）层引入患者级诊断信息，在分割过程中进行诊断感知的特征调制，从而利用预训练阶段从大规模MRI数据中学到的解剖先验，并针对SVP特定亚型调整特征表示。

Result: 在包含多种SVP亚型的内部队列上评估。SV-Cine在左心室获得中位Dice 0.89（IQR 0.80–0.91），右心室0.72（IQR 0.54–0.84）；在右心室分割上比最强基线nnU-Net高出0.39 Dice。对优势心室的射血分数估计中位误差为5.55个百分点（IQR 3.41–7.69）。与内部队列相比，外部队列上左心室与心肌分割性能下降，而右心室Dice在两者间相当。结果表明，预训练基础模型可通过诊断先验适配到高度专用的下游任务，同时保留预训练获得的解剖知识。

Conclusion: 贡献在于将合成数据增强与诊断条件化的基础模型适配结合，用于数据稀缺、形态高度异质的SVP心脏MRI分割，并在内部队列上显著优于nnU-Net（尤其右心室）。意义在于验证了“大规模预训练 + 诊断先验调制”对小样本专业临床任务的可行性。局限包括：依赖单一内部队列与有限外部验证，跨中心泛化性（尤其LV与心肌）有限；方法基于特定基础模型与诊断标签，结论的普适性及对更广泛SVP亚型的稳健性仍需进一步验证。该工作与通用视觉感知任务关联较弱，主要价值在医学图像分割与大模型适配范式。

Abstract: Single Ventricle Physiology (SVP) is a rare subtype of congenital heart disease characterized by the presence of a single functional cardiac ventricle with atypical anatomic configurations that challenge conventional image segmentation approaches. The scarcity of clinical data and the morphological diversity across SVP subtypes make the development of robust segmentation methods particularly difficult. To address these limitations, we propose a cardiac MRI segmentation framework focused on ventricular chambers and myocardium segmentation tailored for SVP. First, we introduce a data augmentation pipeline that generates synthetic 3D cardiac meshes using SDF4CHD and corresponding synthetic cardiac MRI through generative modeling. Second, we introduce SV-Cine, a diagnosis-conditioned adaptation of the foundation model CineMA that incorporates patient-level diagnostic information through Feature-wise Linear Modulation layers, enabling diagnosis-aware feature adaptation during segmentation. We evaluated the framework on an internal cohort with varying SVP subtypes. SV-Cine achieved median Dice scores of 0.89 (IQR: 0.80--0.91) for the left ventricle and 0.72 (IQR: 0.54--0.84) for the right ventricle, outperforming the strongest baseline, nnU-Net, by 0.39 Dice points on right ventricle segmentation. It also yields a median ejection fraction error of 5.55 percentage points (IQR: 3.41--7.69) for the dominant ventricle. Compared with the internal cohort, LV and myocardium segmentation performance was lower for the external cohort; whereas RV Dice scores were comparable for both cohorts. Our findings suggest that a pretrained foundation model can be adapted for highly specialized downstream tasks through usage of diagnosis priors while leveraging anatomic knowledge learned from large-scale MRI datasets during pretraining.

</details>


### [70] [Physics-Aware Video Generation via Agentic Planning and Graph-Guided Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13006)
*Minh-Loi Nguyen,Xuan-Vu Le,Thanh-Toan Do,Tam V. Nguyen,Minh-Triet Tran,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysPlan 是一个无需训练（training-free）的视频扩散模型物理引导框架，将范式从"随机视觉插值"转变为"智能体物理模拟"。它借助 VLM 作为迭代式认知模拟器，生成视觉思维链（Chain-of-Visual-Thought）来构造运动学轨迹与 3D 深度几何的多模态表征，并通过以对象为中心的测试时优化引导视频生成，从而提升生成视频的物理合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型（VDMs）虽能合成高保真、照片级真实的视频内容，但本质上缺乏对物理规律的内在理解，常产生视觉上吸引人却在因果上不合理的序列，表现为结构性幻觉与物理上不可行的动力学。现有的无需训练的测试时优化方法虽有潜力注入物理感知，但依赖全局梯度更新和僵化的调度启发式规则：前者会无意中破坏被动背景，后者无法建模复杂的动态状态变化。因此，如何在保持背景稳定与物理动态精准的前提下有效引导 VDM 成为关键问题。

Method: PhysPlan 提出三项核心创新：(1) 以 VLM 作为迭代式认知模拟器，将多模态输入分解为 Chain-of-Visual-Thought，生成包含运动学轨迹与 3D 深度几何的多模态表征；(2) 提出 Object-Centric Gradient Routing（以对象为中心的梯度路由），将这些物理信号导向以对象为单位的测试时优化，隔离运动学修改并完全锁定被动环境，避免全局梯度对背景的污染；(3) 提出 Kinetic Intensity Profiling（动力学强度剖析），根据物理形变的严重程度动态参数化框架超参数，取代此前僵化的调度启发式规则。

Result: 论文在 PhyGenBench 与 Physics-IQ 两个物理合理性基准上进行了大量实验评估。结果表明，PhysPlan 显著优于基础（foundational）VDM 基线以及可控（controllable）VDM 基线，验证了其在提升视频生成物理理解能力方面的有效性。

Conclusion: PhysPlan 的主要贡献在于将物理感知的视频生成重新定义为"智能体物理模拟"而非随机视觉插值，并通过 VLM 认知模拟、对象中心梯度路由与动力学强度剖析三项机制，在无需训练的前提下显式提升生成视频的物理合理性。其意义在于为改善视频生成模型的物理理解提供了新范式，同时保持背景完整性与动态自适应性。潜在局限包括：依赖 VLM 推理带来的计算开销、对 VLM 认知模拟器准确性的敏感性，以及评估主要限于物理合理性基准而未涉及更广泛生成质量维度。

Abstract: Video diffusion models (VDMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in synthesizing high-fidelity, photorealistic video content. However, they fundamentally lack an intrinsic understanding of physical laws and frequently produce visually appealing but causally illogical sequences characterized by structural hallucinations and physically implausible dynamics. Injecting physical awareness via training-free test-time optimization is a promising alternative, yet existing methods rely on global gradient updates and rigid scheduling heuristics that inadvertently corrupt passive backgrounds and fail to model complex dynamic state changes. To address this, we propose PhysPlan, a novel training-free guidance framework that shifts the paradigm from stochastic visual interpolation to agentic physics simulation. First, a VLM operates as an iterative cognitive simulator, decomposing multimodal inputs into a Chain-of-Visual-Thought to create a multimodal representation of kinematic trajectories and 3D depth geometries. Second, these signals drives an object-centric test-time optimization. Unlike prior training-free methods that rely on global gradients and rigid scheduling heuristics, PhysPlan introduces Object-Centric Gradient Routing to isolate kinematic modifications and completely lock the passive environment. Furthermore, our Kinetic Intensity Profiling dynamically parameterizes framework hyperparameters to accommodate the varying severity of physical deformations. Extensive evaluations on the PhyGenBench and Physics-IQ benchmarks demonstrate that PhysPlan significantly outperforms both foundational and controllable VDM baselines, offering a promising approach for improving the physical understanding of video generation.

</details>


### [71] [Pixel Decodability Is Not a Compression Signal: Causally Evaluating Importance Proxies for Visual KV-Cache Eviction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13012)
*Chenyu Zhou,Qiliang Jiang,Shuning Wu,Xu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究视觉语言模型（VLM）视觉 Key-Value（KV）缓存中"可像素解码保留"这一性质。作者用学习到的像素反演解码器度量缓存单元保留的视觉信息，并用单超图块（super-patch）KV 消融度量因果利用度，发现二者在本实验设定下解耦：保留量并不能正向预测该单元是否被因果依赖。因此可像素解码保留是一个与功能正交的信息轴，且作为 KV 压缩信号并不具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 的视觉 KV 缓存保留了相当多可被反演重建的像素级视觉内容，这引出一个自然假设：保留越多视觉信息的缓存单元越重要，因而可作为 KV 逐出（eviction）或压缩的度量。然而"保留的信息量"与"回答问题时计算实际因果依赖的单元"是否为同一件事，此前缺乏严格检验。明确这一问题对 KV 缓存压缩、推理效率与可解释性都至关重要，否则可能基于错误代理做出剪枝决策。

Method: 方法上构建两个独立度量：(1) 保留度（retention）——用一个学习得到的像素反演解码器（pixel-inversion decoder）从视觉 KV 缓存单元重建像素内容，衡量其可解码信息量；(2) 因果利用度（causal utilization）——通过单超图块 KV 消融，测量教师强制（teacher-forced）下 gold-answer 对数概率的下降量，作为该单元对正确答案计算的因果贡献。在预注册（preregistered）、符号校准（sign-calibrated）、留出（held-out）的实验设计下，在同一图像内部比较这两个量。实验还包含正对照（positive control），并对比两组架构（一个无编码器模型 encoder-free 与一个基于编码器模型 encoder-based）的保留差异；同时在不同粒度（super-patch 与 token）和不同缓存预算下评估保留度作为 KV 逐出排序信号的表现。

Result: 主要发现：可像素解码的保留度与因果利用度解耦，且在统计功效充分的零假设检验中表现为无正向关系；保留度也与注意力解耦，而注意力虽弱但显著地追踪因果利用度，是设计中的正对照，也是唯一发现的此类信号。保留量随架构而异——无编码器模型比基于编码器的模型多保留 2.7 倍的可解码内容。工程层面的结论是一个受控的负结果：在 super-patch 粒度上，去混淆后的可像素解码保留度对 KV 逐出的排序能力不优于随机；在 token 粒度上仅在较大预算时获得微弱的逆重要性信号，且在每一预算档位上都被注意力幅值（attention magnitude）所支配。因此可重建性在本实验设定下、在所有测试粒度上都不是有竞争力的 KV 压缩信号。

Conclusion: 贡献在于将视觉 KV 缓存中的"可像素解码保留"刻画为一条与功能（因果利用）正交的信息轴，并通过预注册的严格实验证明其不能正向预测因果重要性，也无法作为有效的 KV 逐出信号。意义在于对"保留信息多=重要"的直觉给出受控反例，提示 KV 压缩应依赖注意力等因果相关代理。局限在于结论限于作者所用的模型对与测试设定（预注册范围、特定消融粒度与预算），作者亦明确以"in our setting"限定，未声称对所有 VLM 或所有粒度普遍成立。

Abstract: Vision-language models retain a substantial amount of pixel-decodable visual content in their visual key-value cache. We show, in our setting, that this retention is task-inert: across our preregistered tests, how much a unit retains never positively tracks whether the computation that answers the question causally relies on it. We measure retention with a learned pixel-inversion decoder and causal use with single-super-patch KV ablation, the teacher-forced drop in gold-answer log-probability, and relate the two within images under a preregistered, sign-calibrated, held-out design. Retention is decoupled from attention and, in a well-powered null, from causal utilization. Utilization is not inert to every proxy: attention weakly but significantly tracks it, the only signal we find that does and the design's positive control. We characterize pixel-decodable retention as an informational axis of the visual KV cache, orthogonal to the functional one. How much task-inert content a cache holds differs by architecture in our model pair: the encoder-free model retains 2.7 times more than the encoder-based one. The engineering consequence is a controlled negative result. At super-patch granularity, deconfounded pixel-decodable retention ranks KV eviction no better than random; at token granularity it acquires only a weak inverse-importance signal at larger budgets, dominated at every budget by attention magnitude. In our setting, pixel-decodable reconstructability is not a competitive KV-compression signal at any granularity we test.

</details>


### [72] [TileNet: Tile-Based CNN-SVM Architecture for Autonomous Unmanned Aerial Systems Inspection of Flat Roofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13013)
*Samuel Dunthorne,Hashim A. Hashim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于无人机（UAS）的实时屋顶缺陷检测框架，采用双高度多分辨率飞行策略采集影像，并结合轻量级 CNN-SVM 分类器与分块（tile-based）架构，在嵌入式无人机硬件上实现低延迟在线推理，测试精度达 94.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平屋顶是建筑围护结构中最关键的部分之一，直接影响结构性能与热效率，进而关联家庭能耗、碳排放与长期环境可持续性。及时检测屋顶缺陷有助于减少供暖/制冷损失、防止潮湿引发的霉变等退化，并支持国家气候减排目标。然而传统人工屋顶巡检存在安全风险、成本高、效率低等问题，因此需要一种能够自主、实时、安全地检测屋顶缺陷（尤其是细小尺度的缺陷）的方案，这对无人机嵌入式平台的计算与功耗约束提出了挑战。

Method: 论文提出一个端到端的 UAS 深度学习框架：（1）采用双高度（dual-altitude）多分辨率飞行策略，在一次任务中进行高空与低空图像采集，以同时捕捉小而精细的缺陷与较大的结构性问题；（2）为应对嵌入式无人机的算力与功耗限制，提出 tile-based 分块架构，将大幅航拍影像切分为小块处理；（3）设计轻量级 CNN-SVM 分类器用于低延迟机载推理，模型包含 5 个卷积层与 4 个全连接层，最后一层为线性 SVM 分类头，兼顾特征提取能力与嵌入式部署效率。

Result: 在按照片级别（photo-level）划分的数据集上进行评估，使用分块并增强后的图像，训练集 43,383 张、验证集 3,869 张、测试集 2,540 张，三个随机种子下平均测试精度为 94.4%（95% 置信区间 ±0.4%）。相比之下，GoogLeNet 为 89.2%，AlexNet 为 79.8%，所提模型在精度上均优于这两个基线。实验使用现场采集的真实 UAS 影像，采集平台为 DJI Matrice 350 RTK 无人机，验证了系统能够支持快速、可重复且安全的屋顶巡检，降低人员风险与运营成本，促进可持续建筑维护。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于将多分辨率双高度飞行策略与轻量级 CNN-SVM 分块分类框架相结合，为嵌入式无人机平台提供了一套实时、低功耗的屋顶缺陷检测方案，并在真实 UAS 数据上取得较高精度，具备降低人工风险与运维成本的实际价值。潜在局限可能包括：缺陷类型与数据分布依赖特定数据集和现场条件，泛化能力有待更多场景验证；SVM 分类头与分块策略对细粒度定位与像素级分割能力有限；论文侧重分类精度，未充分体现对实时性指标（如帧率、延迟）与功耗的定量分析。

Abstract: Flat roofs are among the most influential components of the building envelope, governing both structural performance and thermal efficiency, and thereby contributing directly to household energy consumption, carbon emissions, and long-term environmental sustainability. Timely detection of roof defects is essential for reducing heating and cooling losses, preventing moisture-driven degradation such as mold growth, and supporting national climate-change mitigation goals. This paper presents a real-time, Unmanned Aerial System (UAS)-based deep learning framework that autonomously detects defects using live imagery captured during dual-altitude aerial passes. The multi-resolution flight strategy is designed to aid the identification of both small, fine-scale defects and larger structural issues, enabling more comprehensive assessments. To meet the strict computational and power constraints of embedded UAS hardware, the proposed framework integrates a tile-based architecture with a lightweight Convolution Neural Network-Support Vector Machine (CNN-SVM) classifier designed for low-latency onboard inference. The final model-comprising five convolutional layers and four dense layers, the last a linear SVM head, achieved a mean test accuracy of $94.4\%$ ($95\%$ confidence interval $\pm0.4\%$ over three seeds) on a photo-level split ($43,383$ training, $3,869$ validation, and $2,540$ test tiled and augmented images), outperforming GoogLeNet ($89.2\%$) and AlexNet ($79.8\%$). Experimental evaluations using real UAS imagery collected by onsite visits with DJI Matrice 350 RTK drone demonstrate that the system supports rapid, repeatable, and safe roof inspections while reducing human risk, lowering operational costs, and enabling more sustainable building maintenance.

</details>


### [73] [Label-Guided Knowledge Distillation for 3D-CNNs in Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13024)
*Yanjiang Shi,Peng Zhao,Nan Qi,Guiqin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出标签引导知识蒸馏（Label-Guided Knowledge Distillation, LGKD），针对3D-CNN特征蒸馏在时间维度上的特性，利用真值标签从样本级和类别级两个层面引导学生模型学习教师模型的特征表示，在UCF101和HMDB51动作识别基准上取得有竞争力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏是模型压缩的关键技术，目的是将高容量教师模型的知识迁移到轻量学生模型。作者回顾了3D-CNN的特征蒸馏方法后发现：视频分析中大多数特征蒸馏方法只是对图像分析方法的简单迁移，忽略了视频特征在时间维度上的差异——即视频特征包含显著的时序动态信息，而单纯迁移图像蒸馏范式难以捕捉哪些特征对时序精度真正重要，且易受噪声干扰。因此需要一种能针对视频时序特性、抑制噪声并强化重要时序特征的学习机制。

Method: LGKD以真值标签作为引导信号，设计两个互补的蒸馏组件：(1) 样本级蒸馏（sample-wise distillation）：利用标签信息和教师模型的概率分布，共同指导学生模型学习对时序准确率有显著影响的特征，同时缓解噪声特征的负面影响；(2) 类别级蒸馏（class-wise distillation）：引入原型网络（prototype network）为每个类别构建特征原型，从而在同类别样本之间捕获关系型知识（relational knowledge），帮助学生模型学习更高维的语义信息并提升泛化能力。二者结合使学生在样本与类别两个层级上对齐教师的特征表示。

Result: 在两个动作识别基准数据集 UCF101 和 HMDB51 上进行了综合实验，结果表明LGKD达到了具有竞争力的性能，验证了所提方法在3D-CNN视频特征蒸馏上的有效性与优越性。（摘要未披露具体骨干网络、教师/学生配置、精度数值及对比方法细节。）

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：指出视频特征蒸馏在时间维度上的特性差异这一被忽视的问题，并提出以真值标签为引导、涵盖样本级与类别级双层次特征的LGKD蒸馏框架，原型网络的引入增强了对同类别关系型语义的建模与泛化能力。方法在UCF101/HMDB51上得到验证。局限方面，摘要未给出定量结果、消融细节及对时序模块的具体作用机理分析，且方法主要面向3D-CNN动作识别，向事件相机、轻量实时感知等场景的迁移性尚待验证。

Abstract: As a key model compression technique, knowledge distillation aims to transfer knowledge from a high-capacity teacher model to a lightweight student model for enhancing the latter's performance. In this work, we reviewed the feature knowledge distillation for 3D-CNNs and observed that most feature distillation methods in video analysis are simple adaptations of those used in image analysis, often neglecting the differences of video features in the temporal dimension. To address this issue, we proposed Label-Guided Knowledge Distillation (LGKD) to guide the distillation of student model features using ground truth labels. Our method entails two components: sample-wise distillation and class-wise distillation, enabling the student model to learn feature representation of the teacher model at two levels. Sample-wise distillation utilizes label information and the teacher's probability distribution to guide the learning of features that significantly impact temporal accuracy while mitigating noise. Meanwhile, class-wise feature distillation employs a prototype network to further capture the relational knowledge among samples within the same category, enhancing the student's ability to learn higher-dimensional semantic information and improving model generalization. To demonstrate the effectiveness and superiority of our method, we conducted comprehensive experiments on two benchmark action recognition datasets, UCF101 and HMDB51, achieving competitive results.

</details>


### [74] [Unified CT and MRI Pancreas Segmentation for Label-Efficient Cross-Modality Subregion Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13043)
*Ziliang Hong,Hongyi Pan,Halil Ertugrul Aktas,Andrea Bejar,Elif Keles,Frank H. Miller,Michael B. Wallace,Rajesh N. Keswani,Gorkem Durak,Ulas Bagci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个统一的3D胰腺分割框架，采用域对抗学习在4604例异构CT与MRI扫描上训练共享nnU-Net编码器-解码器，通过潜在域判别器对齐CT-MRI特征，实现跨模态全胰腺分割并将编码器迁移至标签高效的MRI胰腺头/体/尾亚区分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分割在不同成像模态间存在显著的外观与强度分布差异，单模态训练的模型在未见域上性能大幅下降。胰腺分割本身因器官形状多变、边界模糊而困难，CT与MRI跨模态泛化问题进一步加剧。本文动机在于学习一种跨模态的、解剖学层面的统一表示，从而在无需目标模态标注的情况下实现鲁棒的跨域分割与下游任务迁移。

Method: 核心为域对抗学习（domain-adversarial learning）：共享一个nnU-Net编码器-解码器用于3D全胰腺分割，在潜在特征空间引入域判别器，通过对抗训练使CT与MRI特征对齐，促使编码器学习模态无关的解剖表示。训练数据为4604例异构CT与MRI扫描。随后将学到的编码器迁移至胰腺头-体-尾亚区分割任务，仅使用有限的MRI亚区标注进行微调/迁移，不依赖CT亚区标注，从而实现标签高效的下游迁移。

Result: 全胰腺分割在分布内测试集上达到平均Dice 87.31%；在多个外部OOD（分布外）数据集上Dice为84.20%–88.09%，展现跨域鲁棒性。在下游亚区分割任务中，MRI Dice为80.53%、CT Dice为83.05%，且在完全未使用CT亚区标注的情况下取得该结果，验证了统一解剖表示的迁移能力与标签效率。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于证明统一解剖表示可同时支持跨模态胰腺分割与标签高效的下游亚区迁移，通过域对抗对齐在CT/MRI大规模异构数据上学习共享编码器，并在OOD数据上保持较高Dice。意义在于为跨模态医学分割与少标注迁移提供可行范式。潜在局限包括：域判别器仅做特征级对齐，未显式建模模态内差异与显著解剖变异；亚区分割CT指标依赖迁移而非直接标注，其实际临床可用性需进一步验证；此外未涉及事件相机/SNN等实时感知方向，属于离线医学影像语义分割范畴。

Abstract: Robust medical image segmentation across imaging modalities is challenging because of large differences in appearance and intensity distributions. Models trained on a single modality often show substantial performance drops when applied to unseen domains. In this work, we develop a unified 3D pancreas segmentation framework that applies domain-adversarial learning to 4,604 heterogeneous CT and MRI scans to learn anatomical representations. A shared nnU-Net encoder-decoder is trained for whole-pancreas segmentation, with a latent domain discriminator encouraging CT-MRI feature alignment. The learned encoder is subsequently transferred to pancreatic head-body-tail segmentation using limited MRI-only subregion annotations. An average Dice score of 87.31% on the in-distribution test set and Dice scores ranging from 84.20% to 88.09% across external OOD datasets were achieved in whole pancreas segmentation. Dice scores of 80.53% on MRI and 83.05% on CT were achieved for downstream subregion segmentation, without using CT subregion annotations. These results demonstrate that a unified anatomical representation can support both cross-modality pancreas segmentation and label-efficient downstream transfer.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [75] [Fundamental Dynamical Units for Physics-Informed Structural Inference from Perturbation Time-Series in Networked Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11934)
*Nima Nouri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"基元动力学单元"(Fundamental Dynamical Units, FDUs)——将符号化的三节点交互模式作为可组合基元，把网络动力学系统的交互结构假设空间转化为有限、可构造且可处理的形式，并将FDU正则化的结构推断嵌入物理信息神经常微分方程(ODE)中，实现交互结构与扰动轨迹的联合恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有向网络化动力学系统中，研究者最关心的机理参数是"带符号的交互结构"。从扰动时间序列数据中恢复该结构是一个基础性的辨识问题，但面临三个相互耦合的障碍：(1) 交互结构的组合复杂度极高；(2) 干预次数有限时因果归因存在歧义（难以区分直接影响与经由中间节点的接力影响）；(3) 状态依赖的动力学特性会混淆结构推断。作者认为这三个障碍都是结构性的，因此需要结构性解决方案，而非单纯的统计或学习方法。

Method: 采用还原论(reductionist)思路：(1) 引入FDU，即带符号的三节点交互模式，作为可组合的基元，将原本组合爆炸的交互假设空间压缩为有限、可构造、可枚举的表示；(2) 从理论上说明局部交互结构决定了区分直接影响与接力影响所需的扰动条件，从而把"干预实验设计"变成FDU表示的结构性推论（motif-prescribed intervention design）；(3) 将FDU正则化的结构推断嵌入物理信息神经常微分方程(PINN-ODE)框架，利用控制方程约束把结构假设转化为可验证的动力学预测，实现结构恢复与扰动解析轨迹的联合求解。

Result: 在具有已知真值(ground truth)的合成基准上进行了验证，评估框架能否恢复带符号的交互结构并同时拟合扰动轨迹。论文强调该框架支持"结构承诺(structural commitment)"，即通过FDU基元、motif驱动的干预设计和物理信息学习共同构成网络动力学系统中可机理阐释的推断基础。摘要未给出具体数据集规模、定量指标或与其他方法的对比数值。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：提出以FDU为基元的有限结构表示，将因果归因的干预设计问题转化为结构推论，并借助物理信息ODE实现结构假设的可验证化。其意义是为网络动力学系统的机理可解释辨识提供了原则性基础。局限在于目前仅在合成基准上验证，未展示真实系统或大规模网络的实验，也缺乏与现有结构辨识方法的定量对比，FDU在更高阶交互或噪声/部分观测条件下的可扩展性尚待检验。

Abstract: In networked dynamical systems, the parameter of primary mechanistic interest is signed interaction structure. Recovering this structure from perturbation time-series data is a fundamental identification problem, compounded by three coupled obstacles: the combinatorial complexity of interaction architectures, ambiguity of causal attribution under limited interventions, and state-dependent dynamics that confound structural inference. Each obstacle is structural in origin and calls for a structural solution. We address these challenges by adopting a reductionist approach, introducing Fundamental Dynamical Units (FDUs): signed three-node interaction patterns as composable primitives that convert the interaction hypothesis space into a finite, constructive, and tractable representation. We show that local interaction structure determines the perturbation conditions required to disentangle direct from relayed influence, making intervention design a structural consequence of the FDU representation. We embed FDU-regularized structural inference within a physics-informed neural ordinary differential equation (ODE) whose governing-equation constraint transforms structural hypotheses into verifiable dynamical predictions, enabling joint recovery of interaction structure and perturbation-resolved trajectories. Validated on synthetic benchmarks with known ground truth, the framework supports structural commitment, expressed through FDU primitives, motif-prescribed intervention design, and physics-informed learning, as a principled basis for mechanistically interpretable inference in networked dynamical systems.

</details>


### [76] [Physics-Informed Conformal Prediction: Embedding PDE Consistency into Distribution-Free Uncertainty Quantification for Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11935)
*Michael Chin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Physics-Informed Conformal Prediction (PI-CP)，将 PDE 残差嵌入 split conformal prediction 的非一致性评分（nonconformity score），为神经算子（如 FNO）求解 PDE 提供具有可证明覆盖率保证的预测区间，并揭示 FNO 平移等变性在 Dirichlet 边界条件下的近似瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子（如 Fourier Neural Operator, FNO）在逼近 PDE 解方面精度很高，但如何给出严格的不确定性量化（uncertainty quantification）仍是开放问题。可靠的预测区间对科学计算与工程决策至关重要，而现有方法（如 MC Dropout、Deep Ensembles）的覆盖率不稳定。此外，FNO 依赖平移等变性的谱卷积可能与 Dirichlet 边界条件存在内在冲突，限制了其对边界主导问题的逼近能力。

Method: 1) PI-CP 框架：在 split conformal prediction 中将 PDE 残差（physics residual）纳入非一致性分数，从而构造 (i) 分布无关（distribution-free）、具有可证明覆盖率保证的预测区间，(ii) 空间自适应区间——当 PDE 残差与预测误差相关时，物理满足良好处区间更紧、违反处更宽。2) 理论分析：证明 FNO 的平移等变性（translation equivariance）对带 Dirichlet 边界条件的 PDE 构成基本逼近障碍（approximation barrier），并提出引入坐标通道（coordinate channels）来打破该对称性，实现最高 63 倍误差降低。

Result: 在六个物理场景上验证：热传导（2D/3D）、结构力学（2D/3D）、Darcy flow 与 Navier-Stokes。四种 Conformal 方法均取得一致的 89–91% 覆盖率，而 MC Dropout 与 Deep Ensembles 覆盖率不稳定（82–100%）。FNO 相比 CNN 与 DeepONet 精度提升 10–12 倍。实验同时验证坐标通道对 Dirichlet 边界问题的误差降低效果（最高 63x）。

Conclusion: 主要贡献：将物理残差与 conformal prediction 结合，提供分布无关且可证明覆盖率的 PDE 不确定性量化；提出空间自适应区间；理论上揭示 FNO 平移等变性在 Dirichlet 边界下的近似瓶颈并给出坐标通道解决方案。意义在于提升神经算子求解 PDE 的可靠性与可信度。潜在限制：PI-CP 依赖可计算的 PDE 残差（需已知控制方程），覆盖率保证基于可交换性（exchangeability）假设，跨分布/外推场景下适用性有待考察；坐标通道对更复杂几何或非 Dirichlet 边界的泛化性需进一步验证。

Abstract: Neural operators such as the Fourier Neural Operator (FNO) achieve remarkable accuracy in approximating solutions to partial differential equations (PDEs). However, providing rigorous uncertainty estimates remains an open challenge. We propose Physics-Informed Conformal Prediction (PI-CP), a framework that embeds PDE residuals into the nonconformity score of split conformal prediction, producing prediction intervals that are (i) distribution-free with provable coverage guarantees, and (ii) spatially adaptive when the PDE residual correlates with prediction error -- tighter where physics is well-satisfied, wider where it is violated. Additionally, we prove that FNO's translation equivariance creates a fundamental approximation barrier for PDEs with Dirichlet boundary conditions, and show that coordinate channels resolve this with up to 63x error reduction. We validate PI-CP across six physics scenarios -- heat conduction (2D/3D), structural mechanics (2D/3D), Darcy flow, and Navier-Stokes -- demonstrating consistent 89-91% coverage for all four Conformal methods, while MC Dropout and Deep Ensembles are unstable (82-100%). FNO outperforms CNN and DeepONet by 10-12x.

</details>


### [77] [Fed-Equilibrium Framework for Topological Pareto Control in Robust and Fair Clinical Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11937)
*Ting Xu,Henry Leung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Fed-Equilibrium 是一个面向多中心临床联邦学习的聚合框架，提出从"几何防御"到"拓扑均衡"的范式转变。它通过两阶段梯度控制级联（余弦相似度漏斗过滤 + 帕累托膝点调控）解决高容量中心主导小样本节点的"知识支配"问题，实现全局泛化与本地临床代表性的双赢。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多中心临床联邦学习网络中，数据量大的中心（hub）会天然压制少数社区节点（spoke），使小型队列的独特临床模式被隐含地当作异常值处理，形成"知识支配"（knowledge dominance）问题。现有方法多聚焦于几何层面的安全防御（如剔除恶意更新），只能提供安全基线，无法解决效率与公平性的两难——即全局模型的泛化能力往往以牺牲少数群体本地表征为代价。这一问题在医疗场景下尤为关键，因为被忽视的少数队列可能对应真实世界中的弱势患者群体，其临床模式具有不可替代的价值。

Method: Fed-Equilibrium 采用顺序式架构协同（sequential architectural synergy），核心是两阶段梯度控制级联：Stage I（几何质量保障）通过余弦相似度漏斗（cosine similarity funnel）强制更新方向一致性，过滤恶意噪声，构建稳定的流形（stabilized manifold）；Stage II（拓扑帕累托控制）在已校验的贡献上主动进行调制，通过识别最优帕累托膝点（Pareto knee point）来平衡各节点的贡献权重，而非简单按数据量加权。整体设计将聚合器从被动防御转变为主动的拓扑均衡机制。

Result: 在两国产学研模拟环境（bi-national simulation）中验证，数据集整合了加拿大 CNODES 与美国 SyntheticMass 两个注册库。实验结果表明，该系统在抵御对抗性发散（adversarial divergence）的同时，能够容纳代表性不足的信号。尤其值得注意的是，数据量占比不足 3% 的美国少数 spoke 节点实现了与数据充足的加拿大 hub 相当的深度收敛（deep convergence），验证了框架在抑制知识支配方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献在于提出 Fed-Equilibrium 框架，将多中心联邦学习的安全与公平问题统一到"拓扑均衡"范式下，通过两阶段梯度控制级联在保障安全的同时维护少数节点的临床代表性，建立起真正意义上的"知识共享体"（knowledge commons），使全局泛化不必以牺牲本地临床表征为代价。局限性可能包括：仅在模拟注册库上验证（缺乏真实多中心部署验证）、帕累托膝点识别的计算与超参敏感性、以及两阶段级联顺序设计的鲁棒性有待进一步考验。

Abstract: The deployment of Federated Learning (FL) in multi-center clinical networks faces the challenge of "knowledge dominance," where high-volume hubs naturally overwhelm minority community nodes, implicitly treating the distinct clinical patterns of smaller cohorts as outliers. Existing geometric defenses provide a security baseline but leave this efficiency-fairness dilemma unresolved. To bridge this gap, we propose Fed-Equilibrium, a framework that advances the paradigm from simple defense to topological equilibrium. Unlike traditional aggregators, Fed-Equilibrium implements a sequential architectural synergy. It utilizes a two-stage gradient control cascade: Stage I (geometric quality assurance) enforces directional consistency via a cosine similarity funnel to filter malicious noise, creating a stabilized manifold; Stage II (topological Pareto control) then actively modulates verified contributions by identifying the optimal Pareto knee point. We validated this framework on a bi-national simulation integrating Canadian (CNODES) and U.S. (SyntheticMass) registries. Experimental results demonstrate that the system simultaneously secures the network against adversarial divergence while accommodating underrepresented signals. Notably, the minority U.S. spoke (representing less than 3% of data volume) achieved deep convergence comparable to the data-rich Canadian hub. This confirms that Fed-Equilibrium effectively counters "knowledge dominance," establishing a true "knowledge commons" where global generalizability does not come at the cost of local clinical representation.

</details>


### [78] [Dimension-Corrected Hitting Times for Heavy-Tailed Spectral Emergence in Neural Optimizer Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12994)
*Zongmin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Heavy-tailed empirical spectral densities of neural-network weight matrices are widely used as diagnostics of implicit self-regularization, but the step complexity of heavy-tail emergence remains poorly understood. We formulate spectral heavy-tail formation as a right-censored hitting-time problem: a run that does not reach a heavy-tail diagnostic within the observation horizon is treated as censored rather than discarded. In controlled full-batch teacher--student dynamics, we find that the first-step spike--bulk gap alone does not explain onset time. Instead, finite-onset regression supports a dimension-corrected spectral-gap law, (τ_{\mathrm{HT}}\approx CΔ_1^{-γ}d^ρ), with (R^2=0.683), (γ=0.626), and (ρ=0.772) across 330 completed runs. Right-censored lognormal accelerated-failure-time models further favor the dimension-corrected model over a gap-only model, improving AIC from 706.62 to 628.70. Theoretically, we prove that exact early loss dynamics in linear networks do not determine factor spectral tails, that Adam recurrences alone do not imply spectral redistribution, and that projected singular-basis spreading implies contraction of a spectral-tail potential and hence a dimension-corrected hitting-time bound. Empirically, projected-kernel profiles support the sufficient spreading mechanism, Adam and AdamW agree under tested grids, GD and signGD do not reach onset in the same regimes, and real pretrained Qwen2.5-0.5B and Pythia-70M transformer weights show non-Gaussian spectral-tail structure relative to matched Gaussian nulls. The result is a reproducible spectral hitting-time law with rigorous conditional theory, not a claim that Adam necessarily generates heavy tails from first principles.

</details>


### [79] [Efficient AI Model Deployment Using Quantization Analysis Tool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11954)
*Dwith Chenna,Kanishka Macherla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Quantization Analysis Tool（量化分析工具），一个基于 ONNX 框架构建的实用系统，用于简化和指导神经网络量化工作流。工具通过逐层敏感度分析、权重与激活分布可视化，帮助开发者识别对低精度敏感或鲁棒的层，从而在模型大小、延迟与精度之间做出有依据的权衡，提升量化后精度并支持边缘与低功耗平台的高效部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度模型日益部署于资源受限的边缘与低功耗设备，而高效部署需要在缩小模型体积、降低计算成本的同时尽量保持精度。量化是实现这一目标的关键技术，但在实际应用中，不同层对精度降低的敏感度差异显著，盲目地对整网统一量化往往导致精度大幅下降。开发者缺乏系统化的工具来判断"哪些层可以安全量化、哪些层需要保留高精度"，因而难以在模型大小、推理延迟与精度之间做出合理权衡。因此，一个能够提供逐层敏感度洞察与分布可视化的实用工具，对于推动高效、可靠的边缘 AI 部署具有重要意义。

Method: 该工具以 ONNX 作为基础框架，利用其广泛的互操作性以支持跨框架、跨平台的模型接入。核心方法包括：（1）逐层敏感度分析（layer-wise sensitivity analysis），通过评估各层在降低精度后对整体模型精度的影响，识别对量化鲁棒或敏感的层；（2）权重与激活分布的可视化，帮助开发者理解数值分布特征，为精度（位宽）选择提供依据；（3）基于上述洞察给出精度选择建议，引导开发者对敏感层保留较高精度、对鲁棒层采用较低精度，从而实现混合/分层量化的优化决策。工具定位为"实用系统"，强调简化量化工作流并支持性能高效的模型部署。

Result: 论文在多种神经网络架构上进行了实验评估，结果表明该工具能够有效提升量化后的精度，从而改善真实部署场景下的效率。此外，工具还为开发者提供了关于量化对模型及其精度影响的有价值洞察。摘要层面未披露具体的数据集、网络类型、评价指标数值或与基线方法的定量对比细节，仅报告了跨多种架构的有效性验证结论。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是提出了一个基于 ONNX 的量化分析工具，通过逐层敏感度分析、分布可视化和精度选择指导，帮助开发者在模型大小、延迟与精度之间进行有依据的权衡，实验显示其可提升量化精度并改善部署效率。其意义在于为高效 AI 模型的边缘部署提供了系统化、可操作的量化分析手段，并加深了对量化影响的理解。潜在局限包括：摘要未提供定量结果、未与现有量化方法（如 PTQ/QAT 工具链）进行明确对比，且其分析与精度提升在不同网络规模、任务类型及极端低比特场景下的泛化能力尚需更多验证。

Abstract: As deep learning models are increasingly deployed on resource constrained devices, the demand for efficient model optimization techniques continues to grow. Effective deployment of AI models on edge and low power platforms requires optimization methods that reduce model size and computational cost while maintaining high accuracy. This paper presents Quantization Analysis Tool, a practical system designed to streamline quantization workflows and support performance efficient model deployment. Built on the ONNX framework for broad interoperability, the tool provides detailed layer-wise sensitivity analysis, visualization of weight and activation distributions, and insights to guide precision selection. By identifying layers that are resilient or sensitive to reduced precision, the tool enables developers to make informed trade-offs between model size, latency, and accuracy. Experimental evaluations across multiple neural network architectures demonstrate that the tool effectively improves the quantized accuracy, leading to improved efficiency in real-world deployment scenarios. The tool also provides developers valuable insights into the effects on quantization on the model and its accuracy. This work highlights the tools capabilities, practical applications, and its role in enabling efficient AI model deployment through robust quantization analysis

</details>


### [80] [Performance, Efficiency and Collapse -- Advantages and Challenges in Offline Post-training of Code LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11956)
*Abhinav Anand,Sanjana Reddy Pachika,Shweta Verma,Mira Mezini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了代码生成大语言模型（LLM）在强化学习（RL）后训练阶段能否完全离线完成，即利用已有数据集而非在线生成新样本来进行 RL 训练。实验表明，仅需数小时训练即可显著提升 LLM 的零样本文本生成代码性能，且该方法在 0.5B 至 7B 参数规模的多类模型家族上均带来性能提升，但提升幅度因模型家族而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码生成 LLM 的 RL 后训练对于保证指令遵循和代码功能正确性至关重要。然而传统 RL 流程存在两大计算瓶颈：一是需要从基于 Transformer 的 LLM 中大量采样代码，计算开销高；二是序列验证过程涉及大量 GPU-CPU 之间的数据传输与通信。这些开销限制了 RL 后训练的实用性和可扩展性。因此，作者提出核心问题：是否可以不进行在线采样，而是完全依赖已有数据集在离线条件下完成 RL 后训练，从而规避上述计算代价。

Method: 论文的核心方法是将 RL 后训练从在线采样范式转为离线范式（offline RL）。具体而言，训练过程不生成新的代码样本，而是直接复用已有数据集中的样本作为 RL 的学习信号，从而省去 Transformer LLM 的在线代码采样开销以及随之而来的 GPU-CPU 通信。作者在不同参数规模（0.5B 至 7B）和不同模型家族上验证了该离线 RL 训练方案的通用性。文中还考察了训练时长对效果的影响，发现数小时的训练即可带来明显收益。

Result: 实验覆盖 0.5B 到 7B 参数的多个模型家族，评估指标为代码生成的零样本（zero-shot）性能。主要发现有三点：(1) 在仅数小时的训练后，LLM 的零样本代码生成性能即可获得显著提升，且无需任何在线采样；(2) 离线 RL 在不同参数规模的模型上均能带来性能增益，说明该方法对模型规模具有一定的可扩展性；(3) 性能提升的程度在不同模型家族之间存在差异，表明效果依赖于基础模型特性。

Conclusion: 该工作的主要贡献在于验证了 RL 后训练可以在完全离线、无需在线采样的条件下有效进行，从而规避了传统代码 RL 训练中昂贵的样本生成与 GPU-CPU 通信开销，为数小时级别的高效后训练提供了可行路径，并在 0.5B–7B 参数范围内展示了跨模型家族的通用增益。其潜在局限包括：提升幅度因模型家族而异，尚未给出统一的适用性保证；同时离线 RL 受限于已有数据集的质量与分布，可能难以覆盖在线采样所能探索的代码空间与奖励信号，其相对在线方法的性能上限仍有待进一步比较。

Abstract: Post-training with reinforcement learning (RL) is a critical phase in the development of code-generating large language models (LLMs), as it ensures adherence to instructions and the production of functionally correct code. This process typically requires computationally intensive code sample generation from Transformer-based LLMs and substantial GPU-CPU communication for sequence verification. To address these computational challenges, this work examines whether RL-based post-training can be performed entirely offline by leveraging existing datasets rather than generating new samples. The findings indicate that, with only a few hours of training, zero-shot code generation performance of LLMs can be substantially improved without online sampling. Additionally, offline RL produces performance gains across models ranging from 0.5B to 7B parameters, although the extent of improvement varies among model families.

</details>


### [81] [Look Before You Leap: Pre-Action Verification for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11957)
*Asaad Althoubi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出将"动作生效前的廉价确定性检查"作为一种被低估的LLM智能体监督形式，并在单一框架下研究shell命令与代码编辑两种动作模态。核心思想是在执行器运行前按构造固定动作的正确效果，从而直接度量"静默失败"（silent failure），并允许验证器在不确定时拒绝执行（refuse-when-unsure）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体通过发出动作（如执行shell命令、施加代码编辑）来影响外部世界。错误动作并不总是显式报错，而可能"静默失败"——产生看似合理但实际错误的效果且不触发任何错误信息。这类失败难以发现，却可能导致严重的下游后果。作者认为，在动作生效前进行低成本、确定性的预检查，是当前智能体监督中一种有效但未被充分利用的手段，因此需要系统性地量化与建模这一问题。

Method: 论文在统一框架下研究两类动作的预执行验证：(1) shell命令——设计静态验证器，对命令的语法、二进制可执行文件、标志（flag）进行检查；语法与二进制检查是oracle-exact的，标志检查的覆盖范围受限于help文本。(2) 代码编辑——构建隔离"apply（应用编辑）"步骤的基准，对比内容锚定格式（search/replace、diff）与位置锚定格式（行号、函数名）在失败模式上的差异。总体设计原则是"按构造固定正确效果"，使验证器可直接度量静默失败，并可在不确定时abstain而非猜测；采用refuse-when-unsure策略与anchor-and-verify应用器，将静默失败转化为可恢复失败，代价是可应用性（applicability）的可调下降。

Result: Shell命令方面：静态验证器在9930条命令、482个工具上以10.0%假阳性率捕获95.8%的无效命令。其中语法与二进制检查为oracle-exact，零假阳性并捕获一半错误；标志检查仅受help文本覆盖率限制，且贡献了全部假阳性。代码编辑方面：640次编辑、224个文件的基准暴露出明显分化——内容锚定格式（search/replace、diff）干净失败，而位置锚定格式则静默失败：一行位移导致99.1%的文件损坏，函数名编辑有12.7%命中错误函数。在两种场景中，refuse-when-unsure策略可将静默失败转化为可恢复失败：选择性grounding达到0.958召回率、7.0%假阳性；anchor-and-verify应用器在8320次试验中仅记录1次静默误应用（0.01%）。作者开源了基准、验证器与防护模块。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：形式化并实证了LLM智能体中的"静默失败"问题；提出预执行确定性检查作为监督范式，并在shell命令与代码编辑两种模态下量化其效果；揭示了内容锚定与位置锚定编辑格式在失败模式上的本质差异；给出可调权衡下的refuse-when-unsure机制与高精度anchor-and-verify应用器。意义在于为智能体安全与可靠性提供了低成本、可部署的防护手段。潜在局限包括：flag检查依赖help文本覆盖率而引入假阳性、验证器覆盖的动作模态有限（仅shell命令与代码编辑），以及refuse-when-unsure策略会牺牲部分可应用性。

Abstract: An LLM agent acts on the world by emitting actions: shell commands to run, edits to apply. A wrong action does not always fail loudly; it can fail silently, producing a plausible but incorrect effect that raises no error. We argue that a cheap deterministic check, run before an action takes effect, is an effective and underused form of agent oversight, and we study it across two action modalities in one framework. The idea is to fix an action's correct effect by construction, before any executor runs, so that silent failure is measured directly and the verifier may abstain rather than guess. For shell commands, a static verifier over 9930 commands and 482 tools catches 95.8% of invalid commands at a 10.0% false-positive rate. Its syntax and binary checks are oracle-exact, giving zero false positives while catching half of all errors; the flag check is bounded only by help-text coverage and accounts for every false positive. For code edits, a benchmark of 640 edits over 224 files isolating the apply step exposes a sharp split. Content-anchored formats such as search/replace and diff fail cleanly, whereas location-anchored formats fail silently: line numbers corrupt 99.1% of files under a one-line shift, and function-name edits hit the wrong function 12.7% of the time. In both settings a refuse-when-unsure policy turns silent failures into recoverable ones at a tunable cost in applicability: selective grounding reaches 0.958 recall at 7.0% false positives, and an anchor-and-verify applier records one silent misapplication in 8320 trials (0.01%). We release both benchmarks, the verifiers, and the guards.

</details>


### [82] [Decoding Mixture Perception through Computational Modeling of Component Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11958)
*Fei Wang,Xiaoya Xie,Junfei Liu,Huihao Wang,Yixiao Wang,Yintao Wang,Yi Li,Hao Dong,Xing Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种生物启发的深度学习框架，用于多分子混合物气味感知识别：通过构建分子-受体神经响应曲线，融合注意力加权的多受体曲线与浓度依赖的多分子曲线，并利用响应曲线模式一致性进行知识迁移，实现从化学混合到神经编码再到感知形成的完整计算路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嗅觉在危险判别、情绪体验和记忆形成中至关重要，但自然界大多数物质以多分子混合物形式存在。混合物成分复杂、存在浓度依赖的饱和效应以及受体特异性激活阈值，使得嗅觉特征识别极具挑战。现有方法难以刻画混合物中组分间的竞争性激活与协同整合，因此需要一种能模拟生物嗅觉编码机制的通用解决方案。

Method: 提出生物启发的深度学习框架：1）稳健构建分子-受体相互作用的神经响应曲线，模拟受体对分子的响应特征；2）设计融合策略，将注意力加权的多受体曲线与浓度依赖的多分子曲线相融合，以复现混合物组分的竞争性激活与协同整合；3）通过比较响应曲线模式的一致性，将语义丰富的分子关联空间中的知识迁移到混合物感知特征识别，形成从化学混合、神经编码到感知形成的完整计算通路。

Result: 研究进行了全面评估，模型取得了92.2%的识别准确率，表现出显著优越性。摘要未披露具体数据集名称、样本规模、基线方法以及除准确率外的其他评价指标。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于将生物嗅觉机制（分子-受体响应、竞争性激活与协同整合、知识迁移）形式化为深度计算框架，为长期存在的混合物感知难题提供通用可行方案，并可集成到具身认知系统中提升智能体在复杂场景下的感知与交互能力。局限性在于摘要未给出数据集细节与消融分析，对真实生物嗅觉系统的泛化性和可扩展性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Olfaction played an indispensable role throughout human evolution and civilization. Even in the contemporary era of advanced technology, olfaction remains a critical channel for person to conduct danger discrimination, emotional experience, and memory formation. However, most substances in nature exist as multi-molecule mixtures. The complexity of mixture compositions, as well as concentration dependent saturation effects and receptor specific activation thresholds, pose substantial challenges in identifying olfactory characteristics. In this study, we proposed a novel bio inspired deep learning framework for accurate odor perception recognition of mixtures. We robustly constructed neural response curves for molecule-receptor interactions, and developed a fusion strategy that integrates attention-weighted multi-receptor curves with concentration-dependent multi-molecule curves, replicating the competitive activation and synergistic integration of mixture components. Furthermore, by comparing the consistency of response curve patterns, the model can transfer knowledge from the semantically rich space of molecular associations to guide recognition of mixture perception characteristics. Therefore, we established a complete computational pathway from chemical blending, neural encoding, to perceptual formation. Finally, we conducted comprehensive evaluation, and results demonstrated exceptional superiority, achieving an accuracy of 92.2%. Consequently, our work provides a generalizable solution to the long standing mixture perception challenge. More importantly, it can be integrated into embodied cognitive systems to enhance the agents perceptual and interactive capabilities in complex scenarios.

</details>


### [83] [Space as an Interventional Invariant: Cross-Modal Predictive Geometry for Stratified Cities and Em-Spaced Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11959)
*Tao Yang,Xuhui Lin,Kunyao Li,Haijiang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将"空间"重新定义为一种干预不变性（interventional invariant），即在中允许动作下保持局部相容性与未来观测条件律的最小关系结构，并提出跨模态预测几何框架（整合局部状态空间、模态特异观测映射、动作群胚与规范预测态商），证明在联合点分离、等变性、干预忠实性下隐空间可识别至干预群的中心化子，并扩展至层化城市系统的层束（sheaf）表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学、物理、空间认知、城市科学与具身智能等领域通常将空间结构视为共享几何容器或一组互不相连的表征，难以解释异构感知与城市过程如何在度量与表征不一致的情况下共同揭示共同的潜在空间结构。论文针对此缺口，试图给出一个统一、可证伪的空间基础，特别解决不同模态无法共享同一度量或表征时的跨模态对齐与因果识别问题。
