Advis satisfies three desirable criteria not jointly achieved by prior work: it requires no additional environment interactions, no auxiliary networks, and captures long-term robustness. Second, we introduce advrl, a modular PyTorch library that provides clean, single-file implementations of existing robustness methods and adversarial attacks, facilitating rapid prototyping and enabling reproducible and traceable evaluations. Third, we revisit evaluation under learned adversaries and show that optimal adversarial hyperparameters do not transfer across agents, which can lead to an overestimation of robustness when using a limited set of attacker configurations. Accordingly, we evaluate policies against a large and diverse set of attackers, using 6-14x more configurations than prior work. Finally, we evaluate our approach on continuous control environments, demonstrating its effectiveness relative to existing baselines. The code is available at: https://github.com/AmineAndam04/advrl

</details>


### [169] [MCRL2: Multi-resource Cross-attention-based Representation Learning-augmented Reinforcement Learning for Cloud Microservice Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13048)
*Tiangang Li,Shi Ying,Xiangbo Tian,Chuan Shi,Ding Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MCRL2：一种将"多资源交叉注意力表示学习（MCRL）"与强化学习相结合的数据中心微服务调度方法，通过增强的 actor-critic 架构与最大熵目标，学习节点-资源-微服务之间的结构化交互表示，从而在负载均衡、调度成功率和平均完成时间上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心微服务调度的核心目标是维持跨节点的负载均衡并保障服务质量，但实践中面临三大挑战：（1）工作负载波动导致的动态资源失衡；（2）多维资源之间的非线性耦合关系；（3）微服务资源需求的高度异构性。现有基于强化学习的方法虽有潜力，但难以刻画异构资源间复杂的相互依赖关系，并且忽视了学习"信息量充足的系统表示"这一关键环节。因此，如何在调度决策中有效建模多资源、多节点与多微服务之间的结构化交互，成为提升调度性能的关键问题。

Method: 论文提出 MCRL2，其核心是先用 MCRL（Multi-resource Cross-attention Representation Learning）构建系统表示：通过多资源交叉注意力机制（cross-attention），建模节点（nodes）、资源（resources）与微服务（microservices）三者之间的结构化、信息量丰富的交互关系，从而替代传统方法中较弱的状态编码方式。随后，MCRL2 将 MCRL 增强的表示嵌入 actor-critic 强化学习框架中，并引入最大熵（maximum entropy）目标函数，以提升系统状态表达的丰富度、增强探索能力，进而得到更稳定且更有效的调度决策。

Result: 论文在真实生产集群的 trace 数据上进行了大量实验，覆盖多种工作负载模式。评估指标包括负载均衡、调度成功率与平均完成时间。结果显示 MCRL2 在这些指标上均显著优于现有基线方法，表明该表示学习增强的强化学习框架在不同负载形态下具备较好的泛化性与稳定性。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献在于：（1）提出 MCRL，利用多资源交叉注意力显式建模节点、资源与微服务间的结构化交互，改善系统状态表示；（2）提出 MCRL2，将其与最大熵 actor-critic 强化学习融合，实现更稳定的调度决策；（3）在真实生产 trace 上验证了其在负载均衡、调度成功率与完成时间方面的优越性。潜在局限在于：摘要未披露具体数据集规模、基线构成及消融细节，且 MCRL2 在更大规模集群或在线动态环境下的可扩展性与计算开销仍有待验证。注：该论文属于数据中心资源调度与强化学习方向，与事件相机、脉冲神经网络、UAV 感知等领域无直接关联。

Abstract: Efficient microservice scheduling is crucial for maintaining load balance across nodes in data centers and ensuring high quality of service. However, achieving this in practice remains challenging due to dynamic resource imbalance under fluctuating workloads, nonlinear coupling across multiple resource dimensions, and the heterogeneity of microservice resource demands. While reinforcement learning-based approaches have shown promise, they struggle to capture the complex interdependencies among heterogeneous resources and neglect the importance of learning informative system representations. To address these limitations, we propose MCRL2, a novel reinforcement learning approach augmented with multi-resource cross-attention-based representation learning for microservice scheduling. Specifically, we first propose MCRL, a novel representation learning approach that captures structured and informative interactions among nodes, resources, and microservices via a multi-resource cross-attention mechanism. Then, MCRL2 augments reinforcement learning through MCRL-enhanced actor-critic architecture combined with a maximum entropy objective, improving system state expressiveness and leading to more stable and effective scheduling decisions. Extensive experiments on real production cluster traces demonstrate that MCRL2 significantly outperforms existing baselines in load balancing, scheduling success rate and average completion time across diverse workload patterns.

</details>


### [170] [A Unified and Constrained View of Regularization-Based Robust Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13050)
*Amine Andam,Jamal Bentahar,Mustapha Hedabou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文为深度强化学习（DRL）中基于正则化的对抗鲁棒性训练方法提供了统一的理论框架：通过推导名义策略与最坏情况策略之间性能差距的新上界，将现有正则化目标统一为“现有正则化项 + 名义/最坏策略间的KL散度惩罚”，并将其重构为带约束的优化问题，用可自动调整的Lagrange乘子替代固定权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习策略对输入对抗扰动高度敏感，在连续控制等任务中，微小的观测扰动即可导致策略性能急剧下降，带来安全性和可靠性隐患。近年来，加正则项（尤其是加入KL惩罚）成为提升鲁棒性的主流经验做法，但缺乏理论解释：为何加KL惩罚有效、不同正则化方法之间有何联系、正则化权重应如何选取，均未被系统阐明。论文旨在填补这一理论与实践之间的鸿沟，给出统一的理论解释并据此改进训练算法。

Method: 核心是从理论上刻画名义策略与最坏情况（对抗）策略之间的性能差距上界。作者证明该上界可分解为“某一现有正则化目标 + 名义策略与最坏策略之间的KL散度惩罚”两项之和，从而说明各类正则化鲁棒训练方法本质上是同一上界的不同实例，也从理论上解释了加入KL惩罚为何能提升鲁棒性。基于这些上界，作者将鲁棒训练表述为带约束的优化问题，指出既有方法等价于使用固定Lagrange乘子的特例；论文则提出将乘子与策略联合更新，实现对正则化权重的自适应自动调节，无需人工设定固定权重。

Result: 论文在多个连续控制任务上进行了广泛的对抗评估，用以验证理论分析结论。评估围绕名义性能与对抗（最坏情况）性能的对比展开，检验KL惩罚项与自适应乘子机制对鲁棒性的作用。结果支持了所推导上界的有效性，表明将正则化权重作为可优化变量联合更新相比固定乘子能更好地平衡性能与鲁棒性。摘要未给出具体任务名称、扰动范式、指标数值或基线对比细节。

Conclusion: 主要贡献包括：统一了DRL对抗鲁棒训练中的正则化方法，给出性能差距的新上界并揭示KL惩罚的作用机理；将鲁棒训练形式化为约束优化问题，用自适应Lagrange乘子自动调节正则化强度，统一并推广了现有固定权重的做法；在连续控制任务上验证了理论分析。意义在于为鲁棒DRL提供了可解释的理论基础与可实现的算法改进。局限在于：摘要仅提及连续控制任务的对抗评估，未展示在离散动作空间、高维视觉输入或真实物理系统上的验证，理论界与实际鲁棒增益之间的紧致性也有待进一步分析。

Abstract: Regularization-based methods have become a standard approach for training Deep Reinforcement Learning policies against adversarial input perturbations. In this paper, we unify these methods by deriving new upper bounds on the performance gap between the nominal and worst-case policies. Each upper bound is expressed as an existing regularization objective plus a KL-divergence penalty between the nominal and worst-case policies, which further explains why adding a KL penalty improves robustness in practice. Building on these bounds, we formulate robust training as a constrained optimization problem, showing that existing methods correspond to the special case of a fixed Lagrange multiplier. We instead update the multiplier jointly with the policy to automatically tune the regularization weight. Finally, we conduct extensive adversarial evaluations across several continuous control tasks to validate our theoretical analysis.

</details>


### [171] [Benign Loss Landscapes Can Coexist with Worst-Case Hardness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13057)
*Zach Furman,Stephan Wäldchen,Yangda Bei,Liam Hodgkinson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究树张量网络（TTN）作为分析深度网络可学习性的替代模型，证明其能嵌入任意read-once布尔公式（因此包含多项式规模却无法被梯度下降在多项式时间内学习的目标），但同时证明其损失景观对任意可实现目标都是"条件良性"的——最小范数的局部极小即全局极小；学习困难实际来源于由秩亏引起的高阶退化鞍点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络虽然表达能力强，却存在"最坏情况目标"：这些目标可在多项式时间内求值，却无法被梯度下降在多项式时间内学得。现实中训练却能成功，因此核心问题是：真实世界目标具备何种非一般性结构使其可学习？已有替代模型无法提出这一问题：深度线性网络不含难以学习的目标；核方法与无限宽度极限则无法高效求值这类目标。作者需要一个既包含难学习目标、又能高效求值、且便于分析损失景观几何的新模型类别。

Method: 作者采用树张量网络（TTN）作为研究对象，它是一种可高效求值的模型类，泛化了深度线性网络与Tucker分解。技术上：(1) 证明TTN可嵌入任意read-once布尔公式，从而构造出多项式规模、但在与神经网络相同的机制下无法被梯度下降多项式时间学习的目标；(2) 对任意可实现目标证明其损失景观是"条件良性"的——每一个最小范数（minimum-norm）局部极小点都是全局极小点；(3) 通过奇偶函数（parity）案例研究，将学习困难归因于由秩亏（rank-deficiency）导致的高阶退化鞍点，探讨将景观几何与计算硬度相联系的潜力。

Result: 实验以理论推导与案例研究为主，未报告标准视觉/检测数据集上的数值基准。主要发现：(1) TTN确实包含多项式规模但梯度下降难学的最坏情况目标；(2) 尽管存在此类目标，其损失景观对每个可实现目标都是条件良性的，即坏局部极小并非区分典型与最坏情况问题的根源；(3) 学习困难源于高阶退化鞍点，且这些鞍点由秩亏造成；(4) 通过奇偶函数的案例研究，展示了TTN将损失景观几何与计算硬度联系起来的能力。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于引入TTN这一可高效求值且泛化深度线性网络的模型类，将深度网络的表达性-可学习性张力形式化；证明坏局部极小并非难学习的本质来源，而退化鞍点（源于秩亏）才是关键，为理解深度学习为何在真实任务上成功提供了新的理论视角。局限：分析具有较强理论性，主要针对read-once布尔公式与特定机制，尚未直接验证于实际神经网络的训练动态或大规模现实任务，工程/应用指导性有待进一步建立。

Abstract: Deep neural networks are expressive enough to contain worst-case targets that can be evaluated in polynomial time but cannot be learned in polynomial time by gradient descent. For practical tasks they nonetheless learn well, raising the question of what non-generic structure of real-world targets enables this. Existing surrogate models cannot pose this question because they either lack hard-to-learn targets entirely (deep linear networks) or cannot evaluate such targets efficiently (kernel methods, infinite-width limits). We study tree tensor networks (TTNs), a model class that generalizes deep linear networks and Tucker decompositions. We show they embed arbitrary read-once Boolean formulas, and thus contain polynomial-size targets that cannot be learned by gradient descent in polynomial time under the same mechanism as neural networks. Despite this, we prove that their loss landscapes are conditionally benign for every realizable target: every local minimum that is minimum-norm is global. Thus, surprisingly, bad local minima are not what distinguishes between typical and worst-case problems in TTNs. Instead, learning difficulty in TTNs can arise from high-order degenerate saddle points, which we show are caused by rank-deficiency. This is explored through a case study of the parity function, illustrating the potential for TTNs to relate landscape geometry to computational hardness.

</details>


### [172] [CanvasAnneal: Curriculum Reinforcement Learning for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13060)
*Blake Olson,Yuhang Song,Emmett McQuinn,Yuan Shangguan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 CanvasAnneal——一种课程引导的扩散语言模型强化学习框架，通过在 RL 初期将更强的教师模型生成的推理轨迹注入扩散画布（diffusion canvas）进行"热启动"探索，并随训练逐步退火移除引导，从而缓解扩散语言模型 RL 的探索瓶颈，在数学推理与工具使用任务上加速奖励提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLMs）具备并行生成能力，但在复杂推理与工具使用任务上落后于自回归模型。近期虽将强化学习（RL）用于增强 DLM，但标准 RL 方法（如 diffu-GRPO）面临探索瓶颈：扩散采样空间巨大且并行去噪过程中缺乏有效探索信号，导致在困难任务上奖励提升缓慢、收敛困难。作者认为需要引入结构化先验来引导探索。

Method: 核心思路是从更强的教师模型注入推理先验来引导 RL 探索。具体而言，CanvasAnneal 采用课程学习（curriculum）策略：在 RL 初始阶段，将教师模型生成的推理轨迹作为初始内容注入到扩散画布（initial diffusion canvas）中，实现"热启动"（warm-start）探索；随着训练推进，逐步移除该引导（annealing），迫使模型自主生成更多推理轨迹。该方法建立在 diffu-GRPO 等扩散 RL 基线之上，将训练时引导（training-time guidance）作为可调度退火的课程信号，而非固定约束。

Result: 在数学推理与工具使用基准上评估，包括 MATH500、Countdown 与 Tau2。相比标准 diffu-GRPO，CanvasAnneal 在 MATH500、Countdown、Tau2 上均取得改进，并在多项任务上显著加速奖励提升；但增益具有任务依赖性（task-dependent），并非所有场景一致。结果表明结构化训练时引导可减轻扩散 RL 的探索瓶颈并加快困难任务的收敛。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出课程引导的扩散 RL 框架 CanvasAnneal，用教师推理先验热启动并退火移除，缓解 DLM 强化学习的探索瓶颈、加速收敛。意义在于为扩散语言模型的后训练提供了一种结构化引导范式。局限包括：效果具有任务依赖性，依赖更强的教师模型（带来额外成本与能力上限约束），且主要在推理与工具使用基准上验证，泛化性尚待更广泛确认。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) offer promising parallel generation capabilities but lag behind autoregressive models in complex reasoning and tool-use tasks. While Reinforcement Learning (RL) has recently been applied to enhance DLMs, standard RL approaches suffer from an exploration bottleneck. To address this, we inject reasoning priors from a stronger teacher model to guide RL exploration. In this paper, we introduce CanvasAnneal, a curriculum-guided diffusion RL framework. During the initial RL phase, we warm-start exploration by injecting teacher-generated reasoning traces into the initial diffusion canvas. As training progresses, we gradually remove this guidance and require the model to generate more of the reasoning trajectory independently. Across mathematical reasoning and tool-use benchmarks, CanvasAnneal improves over standard diffu-GRPO on MATH500, Countdown, and Tau2 and substantially accelerates reward improvement on several tasks, while gains are task-dependent. Our results suggest that structured training-time guidance can alleviate exploration bottlenecks in diffusion RL and speed up convergence on harder tasks.

</details>


### [173] [MAxBench: A Multinomial Concept Recovery Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13072)
*Divya Appapogu,Freya Behrens,Yonatan Belinkov,Aaron Mueller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 MAxBench——一个与几何结构无关的多项式概念表示评测框架，通过"从恢复的概念表示中采样"来量化评估其质量；并据此系统比较了 10 种定位方法（覆盖 5 类几何结构）、6 个概念与 4 个模型，考察语言模型激活空间中多类别概念（如 Animals、Countries）的可控性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性研究的一个实用成果是对语言模型行为进行细粒度控制（如 steering/引导）。对于二值概念（如 refusal），激活空间中的单一方向往往就足以完成引导。但许多概念并非二值：如 Animals 和 Countries 包含众多子类别，每个子类别又有多个实例。这类多类别（multinomial）概念的可表示几何结构的搜索空间远大于二值概念，因此哪些几何结构最合适、哪些方法最擅长恢复它们，此前并不清楚。该问题关乎将可解释性与模型控制从二值场景推广到结构更丰富的真实概念。

Method: 核心贡献是 MAxBench，一个"与几何无关（geometry-agnostic）"的多类别概念表示评测框架，其评测思想是从恢复出的概念表示中进行采样，以此度量表示质量，从而避免评测标准与方法所偏好的几何结构绑定。论文基于该框架，比较了 10 种定位（localization）方法，覆盖 5 类几何结构，并在 6 个概念与 4 个模型上进行大规模对照，考察包括 rank-one、linear subspace、affine subspace 及 manifold 等不同几何假设下表示的有效性。

Result: 实验结论包括：(i) 仿射子空间（affine subspaces）相比 rank-one 或线性子空间，引导更可靠且召回率更高；(ii) 这一优势很大程度来自更好的非零偏移（non-zero offsets），而非基（bases）的选择；(iii) 在适用场景下，流形引导（manifold steering）与最佳方法具有竞争力；(iv) 没有任何方法能稳定优于 prompting，这与先前关于二值概念的发现一致。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是提出了几何无关的多类别概念表示评测框架 MAxBench，并据此揭示：仿射子空间是更合适的几何选择，其收益主要源于偏移项而非基选择；manifold 方法在可用时具备竞争力，而所有方法都难以稳定超越 prompting。研究意义在于强调应将可解释性研究与元评测（meta-evaluation）扩展到结构更丰富的概念上。局限在于：结论基于 6 个概念与 4 个模型的设定，且未发现可稳定超越 prompting 的方法，说明当前概念表示恢复方法在实用性上仍有待提升。

Abstract: Fine-grained control of language model behaviors (e.g., steering) is among the more actionable outcomes of interpretability research. For binary concepts such as refusal, a single direction in activation space often suffices for steering. However, many concepts are not binary: Animals and Countries contain many subcategories, each with multiple instances. For these concepts, the search space over possible representation geometries is far larger than for binary concepts; it is thus not clear what geometries are most appropriate, nor what methods are most effective at recovering them. In this work, we introduce MAxBench, a geometry-agnostic evaluation framework for multinomial concept representations based on sampling from the recovered concept representation. We use MAxBench to compare 10 localization methods (covering 5 geometry types) across 6 concepts and 4 models. Using this framework, we find that (i) affine subspaces steer more reliably and have greater recall than rank-one or linear subspaces; (ii) much of this advantage is due to better non-zero offsets rather than the choice of bases; (iii) manifold steering is competitive with the best methods when applicable; and (iv) no method consistently outperforms prompting, in alignment with prior findings on binary concepts. These findings underscore the importance of expanding the scope of interpretability research and meta-evaluation to concepts with more varied structure.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [174] [Signed Sensitivity of Expected Hitting Time to Mutation Rate in the (1+1) EA: Per-State Sign Theorems and Verifiable Certificates for Non-Lumpable Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12510)
*RenKai Wang*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文研究标准位变异(1+1)进化算法中期望命中时间 \(H_p=\mathbb{E}_x T\) 对变异率的敏感性，指出"改进事件对变异掩码非单调"这一易被忽视的形式化陷阱，并基于运行时间导数 \(H'_p=(I-Q_p)^{-1}Q'_pH_p\) 构造可计算的双残差符号证书，给出(1+1)EA在OneMax及非可集(positive linear)适应度族上的精确符号结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: (1+1)EA是最基础的进化算法模型，\(p=c/n\) 的变异率选择直接决定运行时间。既有直觉常通过"单步成功率"(one-step success rate)来推断期望命中时间的行为；但作者指出：改进事件并非变异掩码的单调事件，因此Margulis–Russo公式的无符号(总影响)形式不适用，正确的对象应是带符号的端点差分。论文要回答的核心问题是：单步成功率曲线是否足以决定期望命中时间？以及能否严格证明 \(H\) 对变异率 \(c\) 的单调(负导数)性？这关系到变异率调优的理论根据。

Method: 1) 形式化纠偏：证明改进事件对掩码不单调，故不能用无符号MR公式，须用带符号端点差分。2) 精确三维分离构造：给出两个适应度函数，其一维单步成功率曲线完全相同，但期望命中时间为两个不同的精确有理数，从而证明单步成功率不决定 \(H_p\)。3) 以运行时间导数 \(H'_p=(I-Q_p)^{-1}Q'_pH_p\) (\(Q_p\) 为非吸收跃迁矩阵) 为基础，构造可计算的双残差(double-residual)符号证书。4) 在OneMax上证明逐初始状态的符号定理：对每个非最优初态，在 \(0<c<1\) 上 \(\partial_c H<0\)(即 \(H\) 随变异率上升而下降)，\(c=1\) 时仅有距离为1的状态是平稳点。5) 推广到不可集(non-lumpable)正线性族：给出显式不可集性见证、覆盖所有状态而无需枚举的块区间双残差证书、以及均匀符号界 \(\partial_c\mathbb{E}T\le -9n/16\) 在整个区间 \(c\in[1/4,1/2]\) 对所有偶数规模 \(n\ge 8\) 成立，并对 \(c=1\) 的异构实例在63个状态中的57个上证明 \(H'_x\le -1/6\)。所有有限验证使用精确有理数算术。

Result: 实验/验证为精确有理算术下的形式化推导与证书计算：(a) 构造出单步成功率一致但期望命中时间不同的精确有理数反例(三维分离)；(b) 在OneMax上得到逐初态负导数定理及 \(c=1\) 的平稳性刻画；(c) 在显式正线性族上给出均匀界 \(\partial_c\mathbb{E}T\le -9n/16\)(\(c\in[1/4,1/2]\)，所有偶数 \(n\ge 8\))，以及 \(c=1\) 时 57/63 状态的异构证书。作者同时进行了有界的系统性文献检索，未发现该精确组合，但强调所用工具均已成熟，故不对超出该组合的新颖性作声明。

Conclusion: 贡献在于：澄清期望命中时间对变异率敏感性的形式化框架(带符号端点差分而非无符号总影响)，给出单步成功率无法决定期望命中时间的精确反例，并建立可计算的符号证书以证明 \(H\) 对 \(c\) 的单调性，扩展至非可集正线性适应度族。意义在于为(1+1)EA变异率选择提供更严谨的理论依据。局限：作者自述不主张超出所述组合的新颖性，所用工具成熟；结果主要针对(1+1)EA、OneMax及特定正线性族，未覆盖种群型算法或一般非线性适应度，部分状态(如 \(c=1\) 的6/63状态)未获证书。

Abstract: For the (1+1) evolutionary algorithm with standard bit mutation, we study the sensitivity of the expected hitting time $H_p=\mathbb{E}_x T$ to the mutation rate. We first point out an easily overlooked formalization pitfall: the improvement event is not monotone in the mutation mask, so the unsigned (total-influence) form of the Margulis-Russo formula does not apply; the correct object is the signed endpoint difference. Second, we give an exact three-dimensional separation: two fitness functions share the entire one-step success-rate curve, yet their expected hitting times are two different exact rational numbers; hence one-step success-rate quantities do not determine the expected hitting time. Building on the runtime derivative $H'_p=(I-Q_p)^{-1}Q'_p H_p$, we construct computable double-residual sign certificates, prove a per-initial-state sign theorem on OneMax (for every non-optimal initial state, $\partial_c H<0$ on $0<c<1$, where $p=c/n$; at $c=1$ only the distance-one state is stationary), and extend the framework to non-lumpable positive linear families: an explicit non-lumpability witness, a block-interval double-residual certificate that covers all states without enumerating them, a uniform sign bound $\partial_c\mathbb{E}T\le -9n/16$ over the whole interval $c\in[1/4,1/2]$ for an explicit family at all even scales $n\ge 8$, and a heterogeneous instance certificate $H'_x\le -1/6$ on 57 of 63 states across $c=1$. All finite verifications use exact rational arithmetic. A bounded systematic literature search did not uncover this exact combination, although the underlying tools are well established; we therefore make no novelty claim beyond the stated combination.

</details>


### [175] [MAAPO:an innovative membrane algorithm based on artificial protozoa optimizer for multilevel threshold image segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12756)
*Xiaopeng Wang,Vaclav Snasel,Seyedali Mirjalili,Jeng-Shyang Pan*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出一种基于人工原生动物优化器（APO）的膜算法 MAAPO，融合膜计算并行分布式框架与基于轮盘赌的适应度-距离平衡（RFDB）机制的自养模型增强，用于全局优化与多阈值图像分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 元启发式算法在复杂全局优化问题中常面临种群多样性不足、探索与开发失衡等挑战，已有方法易早熟收敛。APO 作为一种较新且具竞争力的元启发式算法，仍有改进空间。作者希望借助膜计算的并行分布式计算范式提升搜索动态与种群多样性，并改善参考点选择策略，从而增强全局搜索能力，同时将该改进算法应用于多阈值图像分割这一典型复杂优化任务中，验证其实用价值。

Method: MAAPO 以 APO 为基础算法，集成两项核心创新：(1) 膜计算（MC）框架，引入并行分布式计算范式，将种群组织于膜结构中，提升种群多样性与搜索动态；(2) 增强的自养模型，在 APO 中引入基于轮盘赌的适应度-距离平衡（RFDB）机制，实现自适应参考点选择。两项策略协同作用，改善探索-开发平衡与全局搜索能力。多阈值分割以 Otsu 与 Kapur 熵为目标函数。

Result: 在 CEC2017 测试套件上，MAAPO 与 12 种先进算法进行对比；进一步应用于基于 Otsu 和 Kapur 熵的多阈值图像分割问题。分割图像质量采用 PSNR、SSIM 与 FSIM 三项指标评估。实验结果表明 MAAPO 优于对比算法，取得更优的分割质量。

Conclusion: 该研究为元启发式算法提供了一种有效的增强策略，并将膜计算与 APO 相结合，为复杂图像分割任务引入了新颖且具应用价值的求解方法。局限在于论文未涉及事件相机、时序建模或小目标检测等感知任务，其贡献主要集中于优化算法本身与静态图像分割；此外结论主要基于 CEC2017 与图像分割实验，跨任务泛化性有待进一步验证。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel membrane algorithm based on artificial protozoa optimizer (MAAPO) for global optimization problems. The artificial protozoa optimizer (APO) is adopted as the base meta-heuristic algorithm due to its novelty and competitive performance. MAAPO integrates two key innovations:(1) a membrane computing (MC) framework that introduces a parallel distributed paradigm to improve population diversity and search dynamics, and (2) an enhanced autotrophic model within APO that uses a roulette-based fitness-distance balance (RFDB) mechanism for adaptive reference point selection. These strategies collectively enhance the algorithm's exploration-exploitation balance and global search capabilities. To validate its performance, MAAPO is tested against 12 advanced algorithms on the CEC2017 test suite, and further applied to the multilevel thresholding image segmentation problem using Otsu and Kapur entropy as objective functions. The quality of segmented images is assessed using peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR), structural similarity index (SSIM), and feature similarity index (FSIM) metrics. Experimental results demonstrate that MAAPO outperforms its counterparts, delivering superior segmentation quality. This research on MAAPO contributes an effective enhancement strategy to meta-heuristic algorithms and introduces a novel, highly applicable approach for complex image segmentation tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [Scenario-Independent Criticality Assessment and Prediction for Vulnerable Road Users in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11947)
*Jörg Gamerdinger,Victor Schwarzenberger,Philipp Schmid,Sven Teufel,Oliver Bringmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向弱势道路使用者（VRU）的新型临界度（criticality）度量，并进一步构建与场景无关的临界度预测框架，适用于所有交通参与者类别，在DeepAccident数据集上显著提升临界度分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶的首要目标是提升安全性，而可靠的安全性评估依赖于能够纳入对象类型、速度、临界程度等安全相关因素的度量指标。其中一个核心能力是区分关键对象与非关键对象，这通过临界度或相关性估计来实现。然而，现有临界度度量通常针对特定场景设计，且主要面向车对车（V2V）交互，忽视了VRU（行人、骑行者等）因运动行为更不可预测而需要特殊处理的特点。因此，需要一种针对VRU的临界度度量，以及一种摆脱场景依赖、可跨对象类别通用的临界度预测框架。

Method: 论文提出两项核心贡献：一是专门面向VRU的临界度度量（VRU-centric criticality metric），充分考虑到VRU运动行为的低可预测性；二是与场景无关的临界度预测框架（scenario-independent criticality prediction framework），可用于所有交通参与者类别，从而避免为不同场景设计专属度量所带来的复杂性。该框架实现了跨对象类别的统一临界度评估。

Result: 实验在DeepAccident数据集上进行，该数据集包含多样化且安全性关键的交通场景。结果显示：所提出的VRU导向临界度度量将行人临界度分类性能提升最高达50%；所提出的临界度预测框架相比现有最先进度量方法性能提升275%，取得F1-score达0.96，并实现了跨所有对象类别的场景无关临界度评估。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献在于提出针对VRU的临界度度量以及与场景无关的临界度预测框架，验证了其在自动驾驶安全评估中的潜力，显著优于现有方法并实现跨类别适用。潜在局限在于实验主要基于DeepAccident单一数据集，泛化性与在真实多场景条件下的有效性仍有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Increasing safety is the primary objective of automated vehicles. Achieving this goal requires reliable safety metrics that incorporate safety-relevant factors such as object type, velocity, and criticality. A key capability of such metrics is the distinction between critical and non-critical objects, which is addressed through criticality or relevance estimation. Existing criticality metrics are typically designed for specific scenarios and primarily focus on vehicle-to-vehicle interactions. In this paper, we therefore propose a novel criticality metric tailored to vulnerable road users (VRUs), which require special consideration due to their less predictable motion behavior. Furthermore, to avoid the complexity introduced by scenario-specific metrics, we introduce a scenario-independent criticality prediction framework applicable to all traffic participant classes. The effectiveness of both the proposed VRU-centric criticality metric and the criticality prediction framework is evaluated using the DeepAccident dataset, which contains a diverse set of safety-critical traffic scenarios. The proposed VRU-centric criticality metric improves pedestrian criticality classification performance by up to 50 %. In addition, the proposed criticality prediction framework outperforms state-of-the-art metrics by 275 %, achieving an F1-score of 0.96 and enabling scenario-independent criticality assessment across all object classes. These results demonstrate the strong potential of the proposed approaches to enhance criticality assessment for safety evaluation in automated driving systems.

</details>


### [177] [Pelican-Sim 1.0: A General World Model Simulator for Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12036)
*Shilong Zou,Shilin Zhang,Yingji Zhang,Yuhang Huang,Yi Zhang,Zeyuan Ding,Han Dong,Junwei Liao,Yong Dai,Jian Tang,Xiaozhu Ju*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Pelican-Sim 1.0，一个面向具身智能的通用世界模型模拟器：以视觉上下文与机器人动作作为输入，预测未来观测，从而支撑下游策略学习与决策。其四大设计为统一动作表示（28 维）、动作-视觉注入、稀疏 MoE 以及高效 rollout 生成（四步蒸馏仿真器）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能缺乏可扩展、可控的交互式环境：真实数据采集成本高，现有仿真器与真实世界存在差距，且难以跨异构本体迁移。构建一个能根据动作预测未来视觉观测的世界模型，可为策略学习、评估与数据增强提供低成本、可泛化的“神经仿真器”，因此对通用性和动作可控性提出迫切需求。

Method: 核心是一个动作条件视频预测世界模型，包含四项设计：(1) 统一动作表示——28 维动作空间覆盖主流本体，使单一模型跨异构设备有效；(2) 动作-视觉注入——通过 URDF 渲染与相机渲染生成动作视频，将动作与像素对齐，提升跨本体、场景、任务的可控性；(3) 稀疏 MoE 层——为异构动力学增加容量并吸收动作模态，缓解模态冲突；(4) 高效 rollout——采用因果适配与少步蒸馏，得到四步自回归仿真器，替代 35 步模型实现 5.67 倍加速。

Result: 在约一百万条真实与仿真轨迹上训练。视频质量（PSNR）在 AgiBotWorld Beta、RoboMIND、RoboTwin 上分别超过最强基线 4.636、2.080、10.343；动作-视觉注入较其他融合基线 PSNR +0.904；稀疏 MoE 较稠密骨干 FVD -6.530；适配的 EWMBench DYN 在 RoboTwin 上提升 0.426。下游任务均成功：每任务 50 条演示加入 500 条生成轨迹将策略成功率从 70% 提升至 93%；策略评估在五个检查点上达到 0.994 的 Pearson 相关；动作选择与策略改进的相对成功率增益分别为 47.7% 和 20.3%。

Conclusion: 贡献在于提出可跨本体、动作可控的通用世界模型仿真器，并通过统一动作空间、动作-视觉注入、稀疏 MoE 与少步蒸馏兼顾泛化性、可控性和推理效率，验证了在策略提升、策略评估与动作选择等下游应用中的价值。局限可能在于评测仍以仿真基准（尤其 RoboTwin）为主，真实世界长期一致性与物理准确性、以及大规模真实轨迹下的泛化边界尚待进一步验证。

Abstract: In this technical report, we propose Pelican-Sim 1.0, a general world model simulator for embodied intelligence that predicts future observations from visual context and robot actions to support downstream learning and decision making. The model incorporates four key design features: (1) Unified action representation: a 28-dimensional action value space covering most mainstream embodiments, keeping one model valid across heterogeneous devices. (2) Action-visual injection: URDF- and camera-rendered action videos bridge actions and pixels, giving markedly better controllability across embodiments, scenes, and tasks (PSNR +0.904 over alternative fusion baselines). (3) Sparse mixture-of-experts (MoE): sparse MoE layers add capacity for heterogeneous dynamics and absorb the action modality while reducing inter-modality conflict (FVD -6.530 vs. the dense backbone). (4) Efficient rollout generation: causal adaptation and few-step distillation yield a four-step autoregressive simulator, achieving a 5.67-fold speedup over the 35-step model. Benefiting from these designs, we train on approximately one million real-world and simulated trajectories and obtain large gains in action controllability and video quality: PSNR improves over the strongest evaluated baselines by 4.636 on AgiBotWorld Beta, 2.080 on RoboMIND, and 10.343 on RoboTwin, with the adapted EWMBench DYN score up 0.426 on RoboTwin. Relying on this, four downstream applications on RoboTwin succeed: 500 generated trajectories added to 50 demonstrations per task raise policy success from 70% to 93%; policy evaluation reaches a Pearson correlation of 0.994 across five checkpoints; and relative success gains reach 47.7% for action selection and 20.3% for policy improvement. Qualitative generalization across trajectory, scene, object, embodiment, and viewpoint shifts highlights its potential as a general-purpose world model simulator.

</details>


### [178] [MoPA: Coordinated Mobile Manipulation via Subsystem-Specific Perception Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12081)
*Guangyu Chen,Qiwei Liang,Shaolong Zhu,Tianxing Chen,Zikuan Xiao,Yifan Xie,Lingfeng Zhang,Ping Luo,Renjing Xu,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MoPA 提出一个面向移动操作（mobile manipulation）的策略框架，通过双感知流（Dual Perceptual Streams）为移动与操作分别提取子系统特定的感知表征，并在结构化 Mixture-of-Transformers 解码器每一层联合适配感知与动作，最终用耦合一阶条件流匹配（Coupled Conditional Flow Matching）生成协调的动作块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作需要同时为底盘移动与机械臂控制提供不同空间尺度的感知证据，而两种动作模态在运动学上是耦合的。现有策略虽为不同子系统设计了专门的动作生成分支，却让这些异构动作分支共享同一种感知表征，使得"子系统特定的感知—动作对应关系"被隐式化，难以充分支撑各子系统所需的感知需求。论文的动机正是显式地让感知条件与移动、操作两类动作对齐，同时在动作层面保持协调。

Method: MoPA 由三部分构成：(1) Dual Perceptual Streams：使用两个互相掩码（mutually masked）的 query bank，从共享的 vision-language context 中分别抽取移动与操作两套独立的感知表征；(2) Perception2Action Adaptation：在一个结构化 Mixture-of-Transformers 解码器中，于每一层联合更新每个 query bank 及其对应的动作流，同时允许两条动作流之间进行信息交换；(3) Coupled Conditional Flow Matching：学习一个联合向量场，用于协调地生成两类动作块（action chunks）。整体设计在解码器层级上实现感知条件与动作分支的逐层对齐，而非仅在共享表征上做分支。

Result: 在 ManiSkill-HAB 基准上，MoPA 在全部三个任务套件上取得 state-of-the-art 性能。在四个真实世界任务中，MoPA 取得 76.3% 的平均全任务成功率，超过最佳基线 12.5 个百分点。论文还通过消融实验与进一步分析验证了所提设计的有效性；项目网站为 https://mopa-policy.github.io/。

Conclusion: MoPA 的主要贡献是提出以双感知流对齐移动与操作的感知—动作对应关系，并通过感知到动作的逐层联合适配与耦合条件流匹配实现协调的动作生成，在仿真与真实任务上均取得领先结果。其潜在局限在于：方法依赖 vision-language context 与结构化 Transformer 解码器，计算开销相对较大；且针对移动操作这一特定形态设计，向其他机器人形态或任务的泛化能力尚待进一步验证（论文摘要未展开说明）。

Abstract: Mobile manipulation requires perceptual evidence at different spatial scales for base motion and arm control, while the two action modalities remain kinematically coupled. Existing policies often employ specialized action generation for different subsystems but condition heterogeneous action branches on a shared perceptual representation, leaving subsystem-specific perception-action correspondence implicit. We present MoPA, a framework that aligns perceptual conditioning with mobility and manipulation while preserving coordination at the action level. Dual Perceptual Streams employ two mutually masked query banks to extract separate perceptual representations from a shared vision-language context. Perception2Action Adaptation jointly updates each query bank and its corresponding action stream at every layer of a structured Mixture-of-Transformers decoder, while enabling information exchange between the two action streams. Coupled conditional flow matching learns a joint vector field for coordinated generation of both action chunks. On the ManiSkill-HAB benchmark, MoPA achieves state-of-the-art performance across all three task suites. Across four real-world tasks, MoPA achieves a mean full-task success rate of 76.3%, outperforming the best baseline by 12.5 percentage points. Ablation studies and further analyses validate the effectiveness of the proposed design. Website is available at: https://mopa-policy.github.io/.

</details>


### [179] [Uncertainty-Aware Conflict Detection Against Operator-Conditioned Weather Hazards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12095)
*Balram Kandoria,Seulki Kim,Aryaman Singh Samyal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向先进空中交通（AAM）预飞行阶段的不确定性条件轨迹预测与冲突检测框架：通过 NURBS 曲线拟合 + 卡尔曼滤波的闭式不确定性估计，结合将气象等栅格环境数据转化为带强度分层与安全缓冲的三维多面体危险体，利用网格求交与时间重叠判定实现飞行计划与动态空域危险的概率性冲突检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AAM 环境下的战略飞行计划验证需要在飞行前预测航空器状态不确定性，并检测与动态空域危险（尤其是天气现象）的潜在冲突。随着环境条件在预飞行规划时间范围内不断变化，传统方法难以同时刻画轨迹的时空不确定性范围与危险区域的不规则几何形状，因此需要一种能在整个规划周期内持续、稳健地验证飞行计划的方法。该问题关系到 AAM 飞行安全与运行可行性判断。

Method: 方法包含两个核心部分。（1）不确定性条件轨迹预测：采用非均匀有理 B 样条（NURBS）曲线拟合生成运动学轨迹，并用卡尔曼滤波传播状态协方差；借鉴光传播算法（LPA）范式，提出 sigmoid 混合的测量噪声模型，用于刻画飞行管理系统在接近航路点所需到达时间（RTA）时不确定性下降的行为；通过速度到时间的方差变换导出时间不确定性边界，从而对沿航路的到达时间偏差进行概率评估。（2）危险体表示：提出算子条件分类方案，将栅格化环境数据（气象现象）转化为三维多面体体积，并进行基于强度的分层；危险多面体融入由航空器性能特征计算得到的机型专属安全缓冲。冲突检测在均值轨迹周围的空间不确定性管（uncertainty tube）与危险多面体之间，通过网格求交算法结合时间重叠进行判定。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验数据集、基线对比或定量指标，主要贡献为方法框架本身：实现了不确定性条件轨迹预测、三维多面体危险表示及基于网格求交与时间重叠的冲突检测，并支持在预飞行规划时间范围内随环境条件演化的连续战略验证。

Conclusion: 本文贡献在于将闭式不确定性估计（NURBS + 卡尔曼滤波 + sigmoid 混合噪声模型 + 速度到时间方差变换）与算子条件的三维多面体危险表示相结合，形成面向 AAM 预飞行阶段的战略冲突检测框架，并引入机型安全缓冲与强度分层。潜在局限在于摘要未提供实验验证与定量评估、计算效率分析，以及方法对真实复杂气象数据与大规模空域场景的适用性验证。

Abstract: Strategic flight plan validation in Advanced Air Mobility (AAM) environments requires robust methods for predicting aircraft state uncertainty and detecting potential conflicts with dynamic airspace hazards. This paper presents a novel framework for uncertainty-conditioned trajectory prediction combined with polyhedra hazard representation for pre-flight conflict detection. We introduce a closed-form uncertainty estimation method that couples non-uniform rational B-spline (NURBS) curve fitting for kinematic trajectory generation with a Kalman Filter for state covariance propagation. Drawing from the Light Propagation Algorithm (LPA) paradigm, we employ a sigmoid-blended measurement noise model that captures the uncertainty reduction behavior of flight management systems approaching the required time of arrival (RTA) for waypoints. The resulting temporal uncertainty bounds are derived through a velocity-to-time variance transformation, enabling probabilistic assessment of arrival time deviations along the flight path. For hazard representation, we develop an operator-conditioned classification scheme that transforms gridded environmental data, specifically weather phenomena, into three-dimensional polyhedra volumes with intensity-based stratification. These hazard polyhedra incorporate aircraft-specific safety buffers computed from vehicle performance characteristics. Conflict detection is performed through mesh intersection algorithms operating on the spatial uncertainty tube surrounding the mean trajectory against the hazard polyhedra and temporal overlap. The framework enables the continuous strategic validation of flight plans throughout the pre-flight planning time horizon as environmental conditions evolve.

</details>


### [180] [RodForesight: A World Model Enhanced Diffusion Policy for Slender and Material Agnostic Rod Insertion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12103)
*Chuanbo Yu,Mingyu Yue,Yan Lyu,Chuhan Song,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RodForesight 是一个面向毫米级细长杆件插入任务的学习框架，将任务分解为"粗接近"与"预测式插入"两个阶段，并通过扩散策略结合动作条件世界模型，在动作执行前预测候选动作对杆-孔对齐的影响，从而选择最优动作块，将成功率从 88.9% 提升至 96.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细长杆件插入广泛存在于精密制造中，其毫米级直径与紧密配合间隙对感知与控制精度要求极高。传统 peg-in-hole（轴孔装配）方法假设被操作物体为刚性、且末端位姿相对夹爪固定不变。但对于高长径比的细杆，操作过程中会发生弯曲变形，其末端运动依赖于杆的构型、抓取方式、材料属性以及接触状态，导致该刚性假设失效，末端位姿难以精确建模与控制。因此需要一种能显式建模细杆形变及其对对齐误差影响的方法。

Method: RodForesight 将任务因子化为两个阶段：(1) 粗接近阶段，采用视觉伺服（visual servoing）将多样化的初始构型收敛到孔洞附近的一个紧凑的交接区域（hand-off region）；(2) 预测式插入阶段，进行精细对齐并完成插入。两个阶段也可封装为端到端设计。插入阶段的核心是：由扩散策略（diffusion policy）生成多个候选动作块（action chunks），再由一个以动作为条件的"世界模型"（action-conditioned world model）预测每个候选动作对杆-孔对齐状态的影响。基于预执行评估所预测的倾斜（tilt）与径向误差（radial errors），在动作真正执行前选出最优动作块。该"先预测、后执行"机制是区别于纯反应式扩散策略的关键创新。

Result: 论文通过实验分别考察了粗接近阶段、预测式插入阶段以及端到端一体化设置的性能。主要结果表明，RodForesight 在端到端设置下将任务成功率从 88.9% 提升至 96.7%，优于扩散策略等基线方法。论文主要采用成功率作为评价指标；摘要未给出具体数据集、杆件几何参数与训练数据规模的细节。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：针对高长径比细杆因弯曲导致刚性末端假设失效的问题，提出两阶段框架，并用"扩散策略生成候选 + 世界模型预测动作效果 + 执行前评估择优"的机制提升精细对齐与插入的鲁棒性和成功率，同时支持端到端整合。其意义在于将预测式世界模型引入精密装配的细杆操作中。局限性方面：摘要中实验规模有限，仅报告成功率，未涵盖跨杆件材质/直径/间隙泛化、世界模型预测精度、预测开销对实时性的影响，以及真实工业场景下的长时可靠性等评估。

Abstract: Slender rod insertion arises in precision manufacturing, where millimetre scale diameter and tight clearances demand accurate perception and control. Conventional peg-in-hole methods assume a rigid object whose tip pose is fixed relative to the gripper. This assumption breaks down for a high aspect ratio rod, which can bend during manipulation, making its tip motion dependent on the rod configuration, grasp, material properties, and contact. We present RodForesight, a learning framework that factorises the task into two stages: 1) coarse approaching, which uses visual servoing to map diverse initial configurations into a compact near hole hand-off region; and 2) predictive insertion, which performs fine alignment and completes the insertion. It is worth noting that the two stages can be wrapped into an end-to-end design. During insertion, a diffusion policy generates candidate action chunks, while an action conditioned world model predicts their effects on rod-hole alignment. This pre-execution evaluation enables RodForesight to select the best action chunk based on predicted tilt and radial errors before execution. Experiments investigate the performance of different stages and the end-to-end setting, where RodForesight improves the success rate from 88.9% to 96.7%, compared to baseline methods such as diffusion policy.

</details>


### [181] [Multi-Objective Agent-Based Model Predictive Controller for Plug-and-Play Vehicle Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12108)
*Jiaming Zhong,Ladan Khoshnevisan,Shucheng Huang,Mohammad Pirani,Yash Vardhan Pant,Amir Khajepour*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种多目标智能体模型预测控制（multi-objective AMPC）分布式控制方案，将交替方向乘子法（ADMM）推广为通用控制策略，在解耦各目标的同时逼近全局最优，并首次系统性地应用于复杂车辆控制系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆控制中的功能集成趋势要求多个控制器协同实现多种目标，而"即插即用"的设计理念对传统集成式模型预测控制（MPC）构成挑战。已有的智能体模型预测控制（AMPC）虽以分布式方式将控制器视为智能体并建立协作框架，但当智能体之间存在耦合或相互依赖时，难以处理分布式的冲突目标。因此需要一个既能保持灵活性、又能处理耦合与冲突目标的实用分布式控制方案。

Method: 核心是将ADMM（交替方向乘子法）适配为通用的分布式控制策略，把多目标优化问题分解为各智能体子问题，在迭代中通过协调变量实现解耦，同时近似全局最优。论文系统性地推导了三种公式化形式，在保证收敛性的前提下分别处理控制正则化与不等式约束。这是该类方法首次应用于复杂车辆控制系统。

Result: 在两个具有多目标拓扑的车辆控制场景中进行测试，通过仿真对三种公式化形式进行比较，并将计算效率最高的形式部署到一台电动汽车上进行真实世界评估。结果表明，所提出的多目标AMPC能够近似收敛到与集成式MPC相同的全局最优解，同时具备更高的灵活性，并具有降低计算成本的潜力。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出并验证了一种基于ADMM的多目标分布式控制框架，实现了多控制器解耦与近全局最优的平衡，并首次在复杂车辆控制系统上完成仿真与实车验证。局限在于全局最优仅为近似逼近，且三种公式化的对比主要依赖仿真，实车评估仅采用单一的计算效率最高形式，尚未完整验证各形式在真实系统中的表现。

Abstract: Functional integration is a growing trend in vehicle control, often involving the coordination of multiple controllers to achieve various objectives simultaneously. The need for flexibility and reliability has led to a "plug-and-play" approach in control system design, which presents challenges for traditional integrated model predictive control (MPC). Agent-based model predictive control (AMPC) has recently emerged as a distributed solution that treats controllers as agents, creating a collaborative framework among them to reach a common goal. However, this approach struggles to manage distributed conflicting objectives when agents are coupled or interdependent. To address this, we propose a novel, practical distributed control scheme called multi-objective AMPC, which adapts the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) into a general control strategy that approximates global optimization while decoupling objectives. We systematically develop three formulations that maintain convergence while addressing control regularization and inequality constraints, applying them to complex vehicle control systems for the first time. The proposed method has been tested on two vehicle control scenarios with a multi-objective topology. Different formulations are compared through simulations, and the most computationally efficient one was implemented on an electric vehicle for real-world evaluations. The results demonstrate that the proposed multi-objective AMPC can converge approximately to the same global optimum as integrated MPC with greater flexibility and the potential to reduce computational costs.

</details>


### [182] [A Data-Driven Distributed Control Scheme: Learning Multi-Objective Agent-Based MPC for Path-Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12142)
*Jiaming Zhong,Reza Valiollahi Mehrizi,Yash Vardhan Pant,Amir Khajepour*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将分布式代理式模型预测控制（AMPC）与交替方向乘子法（ADMM）结合，提出一种学习型多目标 AMPC，通过 ADMM 实现多代理多目标的解耦与集成式 MPC 等价的迭代性能，并引入学习式迭代初始化加速收敛，配合数据管理与认证模块，在自动驾驶路径跟踪任务中将计算时间减少 43.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆系统中，AMPC 将差动制动、扭矩矢量分配等控制器视为分布式代理，共同服务同一目标。但当多个目标存在冲突且代理间相互耦合时，AMPC 难以处理；常用的替代方案是集成式 MPC，将全部目标与代理堆叠进单一优化问题。随着代理和目标数量增加，集成式 MPC 面临计算负担重、实际部署与维护困难的问题。因此需要一种既能保持集成方案控制性能、又具备设计灵活性和计算效率的多目标控制框架。

Method: 1）在信息可交换的假设下，提出从 ADMM 推导而来的多目标 AMPC，将耦合系统解耦为各代理子问题，通过迭代求解逼近集成式方案的控制性能。2）提出基于学习的迭代初始化方法，为 ADMM 迭代提供更好的初值以加速收敛。3）设计数据管理方法以提升实时效率，并引入认证模块保证学习的可靠性。整体形成"ADMM 解耦 + 学习加速 + 数据管理 + 认证"的学习型多目标 AMPC 架构。

Result: 在自动驾驶车辆的组合路径跟踪仿真中，将所提方案与集成式 MPC 方案进行对比，包含多种控制器（如差动制动、扭矩矢量分配）。结果显示：所提方案达到与集成式方案相同的控制性能，同时计算时间减少 43.5%；在此基础上，学习式初始化方法相比无学习方法进一步节省 88.6% 的计算时间，表明其适合实时实现。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：将 ADMM 引入 AMPC 实现多目标多代理的解耦求解并保持集成式性能，通过学习式迭代初始化显著加速收敛，并配套数据管理与认证模块以兼顾实时性与可靠性，为自动驾驶车辆实时多目标控制提供了可行方案。潜在局限包括：方法依赖代理间信息可交换的假设；验证仅基于路径跟踪仿真，缺乏实车或更复杂场景验证；学习式初始化的泛化性、认证模块的适用范围及在更多冲突目标下的可扩展性仍有待检验。

Abstract: Agent-based model predictive control (AMPC) has recently been proposed for vehicle systems with various controllers, such as differential braking and torque vectoring, where controllers are regarded as distributed agents contributing to the same objective. However, this scheme is challenging in handling multiple conflicting objectives with coupled agents. A common approach for such tasks is the integrated MPC, where all objectives and agents are stacked together in one optimization. Nevertheless, as more agents and objectives are involved, the integrated MPC will face challenges like computational burdens and maintenance difficulties in practice. To this end, this paper proposes a learning multi-objective AMPC that can improve design flexibility and computing efficiency. First, under the assumption of information exchange, a multi-objective AMPC tailored from the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) is proposed to decouple the system and achieve the same performance as the integrated scheme iteratively. Second, a learning-based method for initializing iterations is proposed to accelerate convergence. In addition, a data management method is proposed for real-time efficiency, and an authentication module is designed for learning reliability. We compare the proposed scheme against the integrated scheme via a combined path-tracking simulation for autonomous vehicles with various controllers. The proposed scheme achieves the same control performance as the integrated one while reducing the computational time by 43.5%. Furthermore, the learning-based method saves 88.6% more computational time than without learning, making it suitable for real-time implementation.

</details>


### [183] [A Physics-Based Closed-Loop Robotic Bioprinting Framework Towards Volumetric Muscle Loss Treatment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12159)
*Omid Rezayof,Jerin T. Andrews,Ehsan Zobeidi,Ali Ghasemkhani,Meenakshi Kamaraj,Maryam Tilton,Johnson V. John,Farshid Alambeigi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于物理模型的闭环机器人生物打印（DIW）系统，利用3D点云相机与全自主视觉算法在线量化评估打印构件的线宽（bead thickness），并反馈给控制器近实时自适应调节打印参数，从而无需耗时耗材的DOE或大规模数据集即可实现目标线宽的稳定跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究面向体积性肌肉损伤（VML）治疗的机器人生物打印与直写成型（DIW）。已有研究表明打印参数选择对成型质量至关重要，但现有方法多依赖时间与材料消耗巨大的实验设计（Design of Experiments, DOE），或需要大规模且精心标注的数据集来训练机器学习模型。这类方法成本高、泛化性受限，难以适应打印过程中的参数漂移，因此需要一种无需大量先验数据、能在线自适应调节的闭环方案。

Method: 提出基于物理的闭环机器人生物打印系统，实现近实时的参数自适应。硬件层面集成3D点云相机作为在线感知模块；算法层面采用全自主的基于视觉的方法，对打印出的构件进行定量评估（如线宽/线宽几何）。评估结果输入控制器，由控制器调节打印参数（如挤出压力），使线宽收敛至期望值。核心创新在于以物理模型驱动的闭环反馈替代数据驱动的DOE或大规模学习范式，实现从任意初始参数出发的参数自整定。

Result: 实验设置四种配置，每种重复三次。每次打印均从任意初始参数值开始，由控制器调节参数以达到目标线宽。所有试验均收敛，在打印开始后平均5.2秒内实现低于0.5 mm的跟踪误差。不同试验间收敛压力的标准差很低（平均0.04 bar），表明系统具备鲁棒性与可重复性。此外还进行了关闭控制器的对照实验，直接与开环DIW生物打印对比，进一步验证闭环框架在实现目标线宽几何方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是构建并验证了一套集3D点云视觉感知与物理模型闭环控制的机器人生物打印系统，可在无需大规模数据集的情况下实现近实时线宽控制，收敛快（平均5.2秒）、误差小（<0.5 mm）、重复性好（压力标准差约0.04 bar）。意义在于降低参数整定的材料与时间成本，推动VML治疗相关的功能性组织打印。可能的局限包括：评估指标较集中于线宽/压力等单一几何量，眼于实验规模较小（四种配置各三次）与特定材料/设备，向更复杂结构、多材料及长期打印稳定性的泛化尚需进一步验证。

Abstract: Robotic bioprinting and Direct Ink Writing (DIW) are being explored towards the treatment of Volumetric Muscle Loss (VML). While previous studies have shown the importance of proper parameter selection on the print outcome, existing approaches often rely on time- and material-intensive design of experiments methods, or require large, well-curated datasets for training machine learning models. In this paper, we propose a physics-based closed-loop robotic bioprinting system capable of near real-time parameter adaptation. The system integrates a 3D point cloud camera and fully autonomous vision-based algorithms to provide quantitative evaluation of printed constructs. This evaluation is fed into a controller that adjusts printing parameters to achieve a desired bead thickness. To assess the framework's performance, four experimental configurations were tested, each repeated three times. In these tests, printing began from an arbitrary initial parameter value, and the controller was tasked with adjusting the parameters to reach the desired thickness. The system converged in all trials, achieving a tracking error below 0.5 mm within an average of 5.2 seconds from the start of printing. The low standard deviation of the converged pressure over different tests (0.04 bar on average) demonstrates robustness and repeatability. Additional experiments were conducted with the controller turned off, enabling direct comparison with open-loop DIW bioprinting, further confirming the effectiveness of the proposed closed-loop framework in achieving the desired bead geometry.

</details>


### [184] [Battery-Aware Predictive Trajectory Planning and Control for Multirotors Under Disturbances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12188)
*Krishna Bhavithavya Kidambi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向多旋翼飞行器的电池感知预测轨迹规划与控制框架:通过在规划环路中嵌入闭环的"飞行器—电机—电池"联合传播模型,使轨迹评估同时考虑扰动引起的控制需求、电能消耗、电池状态演化以及端电压相关的执行器能力。在含三个扰动区域的150 s、640 m任务中,相比扰动感知的固定参考基线,电能消耗降低7.46%,位置跟踪RMSE降低约72%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多旋翼在存在空间局部化扰动(如阵风、局部气流)的环境中执行任务时,现有扰动感知规划方法通常只关注跟踪性能或控制代价,而把电能与电池状态视为事后约束或常数。然而扰动会同时抬高控制需求、加速电量消耗、并拉低电池端电压,进而削弱执行器可用的推力/力矩能力,形成"扰动—控制—能量—执行能力"的耦合退化。若在规划阶段忽略该耦合,可能选出在标称电量下可行、但在电量耗尽时实际不可行或高度保守/低效的轨迹。因此有必要把预测的闭环能量学与电池—执行器后果纳入轨迹选择过程。

Method: 核心方法是"闭环飞行器—电机—电池传播"的候选轨迹评估:对每条候选轨迹,不采用开环代价近似,而是通过闭环仿真传播扰动作用下的状态、控制器响应、电机功率与电池动态,从而提取(1)扰动引起的控制需求、(2)电气能量消耗、(3)电池状态(如SOC)演化、(4)端电压依赖的执行器能力作为规划判据。电池侧采用降阶(reduced-order)电池模型;为验证其保真度,与独立实现的Simscape等效电路参考模型进行数值基准对比。框架支持与多种反馈控制器组合执行,以考察控制器选择对跟踪精度、能耗与执行器利用率之间权衡的影响。另进行了规划器消融实验,分离电池相关代价项的作用。

Result: 模型验证:降阶电池模型相对Simscape等效电路参考的功率NRMSE为0.64%,累计能量偏差低于0.7%。任务实验:150 s、640 m、含三个扰动区域的飞行任务中,所选轨迹相比"扰动感知的固定参考"基线,电能消耗下降7.46%,位置跟踪RMSE降低约72%。消融实验:电池相关代价项在标称SOC条件下不具约束力(nonbinding),但在电量耗尽的压力工况下会改变所选轨迹。多控制器执行实验表明,控制器选择会改变跟踪精度、能耗与执行器利用率之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于将预测的闭环能量学与电池—执行器耦合后果前移到轨迹选择阶段,并证明其在含局部扰动的多旋翼任务中可同时改善能耗与跟踪精度;同时通过消融揭示了电池相关项的作用条件是电量状态,而非始终有效。意义在于为续航受限、执行能力随电压退化的机器人/无人系统规划提供了能量-控制一体化的评估范式。局限方面:文中所报告结论基于单一仿真任务场景(150 s、640 m、三个扰动区域)与降阶电池模型,与Simscape参考相比仍存在非零偏差;规划器与控制器耦合的结论依赖具体控制器集合;是否可迁移到真实飞行或更一般扰动分布尚未在本摘要中给出证据。

Abstract: This paper presents a battery-aware predictive trajectory-planning and control framework for multirotors operating under spatially localized disturbances. Candidate trajectories are evaluated through closed-loop vehicle--motor--battery propagation, allowing disturbance-induced control demand, electrical energy, battery evolution, and terminal-voltage-dependent actuator capability to enter the planning process. % A reduced-order battery model is numerically benchmarked against an independently implemented Simscape equivalent-circuit reference, with a power NRMSE of $0.64\%$ and a cumulative-energy discrepancy below $0.7\%$. % In a $150$-s, $640$-m mission containing three disturbance regions, the selected trajectory reduces electrical energy consumption by $7.46\%$ and position-tracking RMSE by approximately $72\%$ relative to the disturbance-aware fixed-reference baseline. % Planner ablations show that battery-dependent terms are nonbinding at nominal SOC but alter the selected trajectory under a depleted-battery stress condition. % Execution with multiple feedback controllers further demonstrates that controller selection changes the tradeoff among tracking accuracy, energy consumption, and actuator utilization. % The results demonstrate the benefit of accounting for predicted closed-loop energetic and battery--actuator consequences during trajectory selection.

</details>


### [185] [An Automated Thickness Evaluation Procedure Using an Integrated Structured Light 3D Camera in a Robotic Bioprinting Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12206)
*Ehsan Zobeidi,Omid Rezayof,Farshid Alambeigi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: tldr: 提出一种全自动视觉测量方法，结合结构光3D相机的点云与RGB图像，对复杂几何形状的生物打印构造体（BPCs）进行厚度量化。方法包括基于RGB图像的分割以分离BPCs，以及在点云上执行的几何厚度测量算法。仿真中平均绝对误差达0.025–0.057 mm，并成功部署于机器人生物打印平台进行实测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: motivation: 生物打印正成为替代大面积损伤常规疗法的组织工程手段，而BPCs的厚度直接影响细胞的成熟与整合。然而现有文献缺乏稳健、自动化且定量的厚度测量方法，尤其对于几何形状复杂的构造体，人工测量难以保证精度与一致性。因此需要一种可自动化、定量、高精度地测量复杂形状BPCs厚度的技术方案。

Method: method: 系统基于结构光3D相机同时采集点云与RGB图像。首先对RGB图像执行基于图像的分割，将BPCs从背景中分离并生成分割掩膜；随后提出新颖的基于几何的厚度测量算法，直接作用于点云扫描数据。这些算法将分割掩膜与机器人正运动学（forward kinematics）数据、3D点云扫描相融合，从而对复杂形状的BPCs实现精确的厚度度量。整体流程完全自动化，输出为空间可分辨的厚度指标。

Result: result: 通过仿真与实验两类研究进行验证。仿真中使用已知真实厚度的虚拟BPCs进行测试，测得厚度与真实厚度的对比显示方法具有高精度，在0.1 mm × 0.1 mm/pixel的空间分辨率下，平均绝对误差（MAE）介于0.025 mm至0.057 mm之间。此外，将算法部署于实际机器人生物打印装置，配用结构光3D相机，打印复杂图案并对其厚度进行准确测量，验证了方法的实际可行性。

Conclusion: conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出首个面向复杂几何BPCs的全自动、定量且高精度的视觉厚度测量方法，融合RGB分割、点云几何分析与机器人运动学信息，并在仿真与真实机器人生物打印平台上均验证有效。其意义在于为生物打印中的厚度质量控制提供了自动化与可量化的手段。潜在局限包括：精度依赖结构光相机的空间分辨率与点云质量，对分割掩膜的准确性较为敏感，且当前评估主要在受控实验环境下进行，泛化至更多样化材料与复杂光照条件仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Bioprinting is emerging as a tissue engineering technique to replace common treatment methods for large scale injuries. While thickness of the BioPrinted Constructs (BPCs) have shown to be of importance in the cell maturation and integration, the literature lacks a robust, automated, and quantitative method for measuring these metrics. In this paper, we propose a fully automated vision-based method for measuring the thickness of the BPCs with complex geometries. Leveraging the point cloud and RGB images of a structured light 3D camera, our proposed method performs an image-based segmentation for delineating the BPCs from the RGB images, accompanied by novel geometry-based thickness measurement algorithms performed on the point cloud scans. These algorithms combine the segmentation mask with the robot's forward kinematics data and a 3D point cloud scan to precisely measure the aforementioned metrics for complex-shaped BPCs. The proposed method was evaluated in simulation and experimental studies. In simulation studies, the algorithms were used to measure the thickness of some virtually created BPCs with known thickness. The comparison between the measured and true thicknesses demonstrates the high accuracy of the proposed method, achieving mean absolute errors between 0.025 mm and 0.057 mm in simulation at a spatial resolution of 0.1 mm x 0.1 mm per pixel. Furthermore, we successfully deployed the algorithms on our robotic bioprinting setup utilizing a structure light 3D camera, where complex patterns were printed and the developed methods utilized to accurately measure the thickness of printed BPCs.

</details>


### [186] [Guardrailed Meta-Agent Loops: Stress-Testing Policy Pinning, Budget Bounds, and Crash Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12216)
*Qinzhen Ma,Jialin Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GuardrailLoop 是一个基于仿真的测试床，用于联合验证自改进智能体工作流的三项"操作契约"：人类策略保持、每个执行前缀的算力记账、以及崩溃后科学状态的恢复。其核心贡献是一套可执行的边界（hash-pinned 策略 + 受限的机器导向演化）与评估协议，能区分"有用自适应"、"状态恢复"和"重复执行"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自改进智能体工作流存在审计难题：同一控制器既能改变自身行为，又能改变评判该行为的条件，导致行为与评判标准被同时操纵，难以界定何为合法自适应。作者强调，成功的结果恢复并不足以证明"恰好一次"（exactly-once）执行，因此需要一个可控、可复现的测试环境来分离上述问题，并检验安全/合规约束是否被满足。

Method: 提出 GuardrailLoop 仿真测试床，使三类操作契约可联合测试：(1) 人类定义策略的保持；(2) 在每个已记录执行前缀上进行算力记账；(3) 崩溃后恢复指定科学状态。采用 hash-pinned 策略固定目标、范围、评估身份、预算与发布条件；机器导向的演化被限制在一个代码拥有的特征目录和受限旋钮（bounded knobs）内。整体设计为可执行边界 + 评估协议，专门分离"有用自适应""状态恢复"和"重复执行"三种现象。

Result: 在 50 个随机种子的 2×2 配对研究中，round-stage growth 使目标达成度提升 +1.00，并将到达目标的受限平均算力减少 56.97 模拟 GPU-hours（95% 配对 bootstrap 区间 [-58.91, -54.70]）；idle growth 的效用影响测得为零。在 240 次枚举式崩溃注入中，所有运行都恢复了已定义结果，但只有 210 次保持了归一化 trace；30 次 pre-commit 崩溃会重复一次 planner 调用。资源漂移、kill-switch、完整性和输出护栏矩阵均通过其指定检查。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提供一个可执行的合规边界与评估协议，证明"成功的结果恢复不足以作为 exactly-once 执行的证据"，并揭示 pre-commit 阶段崩溃会导致重复 planner 调用这一具体失效模式。意义在于将自改进智能体的安全性/合规性问题转化为可测量的契约验证。局限在于：结论仅成立于单一经过校准的确定性测试床内的合规性，不构成对一般安全性或现实世界自改进的保证。

Abstract: Self-improving agent workflows create an audit problem when the same controller can change both its behavior and the conditions under which that behavior is judged. We present GuardrailLoop, a simulation-based testbed that makes three operational contracts jointly testable: preservation of human-defined policy, compute accounting at every recorded execution prefix, and recovery of a specified scientific state after crashes. A hash-pinned policy fixes goals, scope, evaluation identity, budget, and release conditions; machine-directed evolution is restricted to a code-owned feature catalog and bounded knobs. The contribution is an executable boundary and an evaluation protocol that separates useful adaptation, state recovery, and repeated execution. In a paired 50-seed 2 x 2 study, round-stage growth changes target attainment by +1.00 and restricted mean compute to target by -56.97 simulated GPU-hours (95% paired-bootstrap interval [-58.91,-54.70]); idle growth has zero measured utility effect. Across 240 enumerated crash injections, all runs recover the defined outcome, but only 210 preserve the normalized trace: 30 pre-commit crashes repeat a planner call. Resource-drift, kill-switch, integrity, and output-guard matrices satisfy their specified checks. These findings show why successful outcome recovery is insufficient evidence of exactly-once execution. They establish conformance within one calibrated deterministic testbed, rather than general safety or real-world self-improvement.

</details>


### [187] [Chain-SLAM: Globally Consistent Backend for Multi-Session LiDAR SLAM via Chained Loop Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12221)
*Zhiheng Li,Xinhao Liu,Juexiao Zhang,Yongqing Liang,Chen Feng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Chain-SLAM 是一种面向大规模多会话 LiDAR SLAM 的在线后端系统，通过"链式回环（chained loop closure）"机制在会话间的关键帧邻接图上传播几何约束，从而实现跨多次采集地图的全局一致对齐与复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模 LiDAR SLAM 中，跨越长时间、长空间范围的全局一致性是核心难题，尤其在整合多次会话（multi-session）采集的地图时更为突出。多会话建图需要将新采集的轨迹与已有地图对齐并复用，但不同会话间存在累积漂移、视角与平台差异等问题。传统方法往往依赖动态物体去除、精细超参调优或离线处理，难以满足在线、大尺度、跨平台的鲁棒需求。因此，如何在无须动态目标移除、且超参调优成本极低的前提下，保持会话内与会话间几何一致性，具有重要实用价值，适用于自动驾驶、机器人巡检与长期地图维护等场景。

Method: Chain-SLAM 是一个构建于因子图优化之上的 LiDAR SLAM 后端，主要包含三个技术要点：
1) 链式回环机制：构建会话间关键帧的邻接图（adjacency graph），当检测到可靠的短时距（short-horizon）回环后，将几何约束沿邻接图向长时距（long-horizon）传播，从而以低成本实现长程一致性触发。
2) 会话间对齐初始化：采用基于 GNSS 邻近性的位置识别（GNSS-proximity place recognition）为跨会话对齐提供初始位姿。
3) 在线联合优化：在统一因子图中对新采集轨迹与已加载地图进行实时回环检测与联合优化，同时维护会话间（inter-session）与会话内（intra-session）几何一致性。系统设计不依赖动态物体移除，且具备跨平台鲁棒性、所需超参调优极少。

Result: 论文给出的大规模数据集实验结果表明：Chain-SLAM 提升了轨迹精度，并实现了稳健的多会话地图整合。系统支持在线多会话地图对齐与复用，在保持全局一致性的同时无需动态目标移除与大规模超参调优。作者开源了源码以支持可复现研究，并提供项目主页 https://ai4ce.github.io/Chain-SLAM/。摘要未披露具体数据集名称、评价指标数值与消融细节。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出链式回环机制以高效传播跨会话几何约束，结合 GNSS 邻近位置识别与统一因子图的在线联合优化，实现了大规模、跨平台、低超参依赖的多会话 LiDAR SLAM 后端一致性，并开源代码促进复现。潜在局限在于：摘要未量化精度提升幅度、未说明在 GNSS 失效或退化环境下的表现、未讨论长时距链式传播可能带来的误差累积与约束一致性风险，以及计算开销与实际在线实时性指标未给出。

Abstract: Maintaining consistency over long spatial and temporal horizons remains a fundamental challenge in large-scale LiDAR SLAM, particularly when integrating maps collected across multiple sessions. We present Chain-SLAM, a LiDAR SLAM backend enabling online multi-session map alignment and reuse with global consistency at large scale. We implement a chained loop closure mechanism that efficiently propagates geometric constraints across inter-session keyframes through an adjacency graph, enabling robust long-horizon consistency triggered by reliable short-horizon loop closures. The system initializes inter-session alignment with GNSS-proximity place recognition, then performs on-the-fly loop closure detections and joint optimization of loaded maps and newly acquired trajectories within a unified factor graph, maintaining both inter- and intra-session geometric consistency without dynamic object removal, and cross-platform robustness with minimal hyperparameter tuning. Experimental results show improved trajectory accuracy and robust multi-session integration on large-scale datasets. We release our source code to support reproducible research in large-scale multi-session LiDAR SLAM. Project site: https://ai4ce.github.io/Chain-SLAM/

</details>


### [188] [DIA: Denoising Intermediate Advantage for Diffusion Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12245)
*Arjun Sohal,Yuchi Zhao,Miroslav Bogdanovic,Alan Aspuru-Guzik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DIA（Denoising Intermediate Advantage）是一种用于扩散（diffusion）机器人策略的策略梯度微调方法，它在去噪过程中引入"去噪层级优势"，即对部分去噪动作学习价值函数，为每一步去噪提供状态相关的信用分配，并与环境级 PPO 优势结合，从而显著提升策略微调性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略通常通过行为克隆（behavior cloning）从演示数据训练，其性能受限于数据质量与覆盖范围。强化学习可通过交互进一步提升预训练策略。常用做法将扩散策略微调建模为外层环境 MDP + 内层去噪 MDP。然而，现有方法把环境级信用统一分配给构成动作序列（action chunk）的所有去噪步骤，无法区分哪些中间决策对最终回报贡献最大，导致信用分配粗粒度、策略改进受限。

Method: DIA 的核心创新在于细粒度信用分配。它对部分去噪动作（partially denoised actions）学习一个价值函数，并据此为生成过程的每一步构建去噪层级优势（denoising-level advantage）。该内层信用信号与标准环境级 PPO 优势相结合，从而在整个去噪链上提供状态相关的信用。这样，不同去噪步依据其对最终回报的实际贡献获得差异化奖励信号，而非共享同一环境信用。

Result: 在 Robomimic、FurnitureBench、Franka Kitchen 和 D3IL 四个基准上评估，DIA 在最终性能上一致优于现有扩散策略微调方法。除最终奖励外，DIA 能更高效地到达成功状态，并可使策略更远离预训练行为分布，发现基线方法无法达到的更有效、更高效的任务级策略与子任务序列。

Conclusion: 主要贡献：提出去噪层级优势机制，将扩散策略微调中的信用分配从粗粒度环境级细化到去噪步级，提升了微调性能、样本效率与策略探索能力。意义：为扩散策略的 RL 微调提供了更合理的信用分配框架，在机器人操作任务中验证有效。局限：方法针对扩散策略微调，价值函数学习与优势估计可能引入额外计算开销；对训练稳定性与超参数敏感性未在摘要中说明。

Abstract: Diffusion-based robot policies have become widely used in robotic manipulation, where they are typically trained with behavior cloning. However, policies trained purely from demonstrations are limited by the quality and coverage of the available data. Reinforcement learning can further improve the performance of these pretrained policies through interaction. A common approach is to use policy-gradient methods that formulate diffusion-policy fine-tuning as an outer environment MDP together with an inner denoising MDP. However, existing methods typically assign the same environment-level credit to all denoising steps used to construct an action chunk, without distinguishing which intermediate decisions contributed most to the final return. We introduce Denoising Intermediate Advantage (DIA), a policy-gradient method that learns a value function over partially denoised actions and uses it to construct a denoising- level advantage for each step of the generative process. DIA com- bines this inner credit signal with the standard environment-level PPO advantage, providing state-dependent credit throughout the denoising chain. Across Robomimic, FurnitureBench, Franka Kitchen, and D3IL, DIA consistently improves final performance over existing diffusion-policy fine-tuning methods. Beyond final reward, DIA reaches successful states more efficiently and can shift farther from the pretrained behavior distribution, enabling it to discover more effective and efficient task-level strategies and subtask sequences that baseline methods fail to reach.

</details>


### [189] [Trajectory Bundle Method in SE(3) for Black-Box Fixed-Wing Aircraft Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12248)
*Matthew D. Osburn,Cameron K. Peterson,John L. Salmon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Trajectory Bundle Method (TBM)，将轨迹束方法隐式地建立在 SE(3) 李群上，通过李代数中的 bundle 构造与指数/对数映射传播，实现无需梯度的黑箱刚体动力学轨迹优化，并给出了插值误差的二次界及其 SE(3) 推广，成功用于固定翼飞行器穿越旋转开口的机动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚体系统的动力学可行轨迹优化天然应定义在特殊欧几里得群 SE(3) 上，但现有基于梯度的方法依赖动力学的解析导数。在许多实际场景中，动力学、气动模型或碰撞模型仅以黑箱形式提供（如高保真仿真器或实验数据），无法获得导数，且直接对 SE(3) 这类非欧空间做无导数优化会破坏其流形结构，导致插值失真与收敛困难。因此，需要一种既尊重 SE(3) 几何结构、又对导数无依赖的运动规划方法。

Method: 方法核心是将轨迹束方法从欧氏空间推广到 SE(3)。具体而言，bundle（一族邻近轨迹）在李代数 so(3) 与李群 SE(3) 之间构造，并通过指数映射（Exp）与对数映射（Log）在流形与切空间之间往返传播，从而利用非线性刚体动力学对 bundle 进行前向传播。这一设计使无导数规划可作用于非欧氏轨迹，同时保持群结构一致性。作者还分析了插值误差：证明欧氏 TBM 的插值误差由 bundle 直径二次界定，并将该结果推广到 SE(3)，此时误差界额外依赖 Log 映射的局部 Lipschitz 常数。整个优化过程无需动力学、气动或碰撞模型的解析表达或导数，仅在黑箱前向计算即可评估轨迹代价。

Result: 数值实验验证了所推导的插值误差界（欧氏与 SE(3) 两种情形）。进一步地，作者以 SE(3) TBM 求解了一个固定翼飞行器的高难度特技机动：在不提供车辆动力学、气动或碰撞模型解析形式与导数的条件下，规划出穿越旋转开口的无碰撞轨迹。实验表明该方法能在纯黑箱动力学设定下生成动力学可行的非欧氏机动轨迹。

Conclusion: 贡献在于：(1) 将 TBM 隐式地扩展到 SE(3)，实现非欧氏轨迹的无导数优化；(2) 给出欧氏 TBM 插值误差的二次界，并推广到 SE(3)，引入 Log 映射局部 Lipschitz 常数的影响；(3) 展示在固定翼特技机动规划中的可行性，无需模型与导数。意义在于为黑箱动力学下的几何一致的轨迹优化提供了理论保证与实用算法。局限可能包括：误差界依赖 Log 映射的 Lipschitz 常数，在奇异点附近（如旋转角接近 π）该常数可能增大，影响界紧致性与优化稳定性；实验主要集中在单例固定翼机动，缺乏更广泛任务与真实硬件验证。

Abstract: Dynamically feasible trajectory optimization for rigid-body systems is naturally formulated on the special Euclidean group SE(3) but is challenging when dynamics are available only as black-box computations without derivatives. This paper formulates the Trajectory Bundle Method (TBM) for motion planning implicitly on SE(3). Bundles are constructed in the Lie algebra and propagated through nonlinear rigid-body dynamics using exponential and logarithmic maps, enabling derivative-free planning of non-Euclidean trajectories. We show that Euclidean TBM interpolation error is bounded quadratically by bundle diameter and extend this result to SE(3), where the bound additionally depends on a local Lipschitz constant of the Log map. Numerical experiments corroborate these bounds. Finally, we demonstrate SE(3) TBM by optimizing an acrobatic, collision-free fixed-wing maneuver through a rotated aperture without explicit models or derivatives of the vehicle dynamics, aerodynamics, or collision model.

</details>


### [190] [Pneumatic neurons for soft robots enable inflate-and-fire networks for rhythmic motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12258)
*Dongting Li,Michael Tolley,Nick Gravish*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"Pneu-ron"——一种受漏电积分-放电(LIF)神经元模型启发的软体气动神经元模块，将低沸点流体、加热器与机械开关集成为可自激发的单元，通过"充气-放电"(inflate-and-fire)机制驱动相邻模块的兴奋与抑制；由兴奋-抑制环互连可产生稳定的顺序振荡，无需集中式电子控制器即可驱动软体机器人节律运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物通过分布式神经回路协调运动，而现有软体机器人仍普遍依赖外部集中式电子器件进行控制，限制了自主性与环境适应性。如何在无集中电子控制器的前提下，使软体机器人具备对环境(机械负载、温度变化)的响应能力，是软体机器人领域的前沿挑战。该工作试图将逻辑与驱动功能下放到材料层面。

Method: 核心是 Pneu-ron 模块：将低沸点流体(LBF)、加热器与机械开关集成于单一软体驱动器，统一实现能量转换、逻辑与驱动。加热使 LBF 沸腾并膨胀，模块充气形成自激发，进而触发相邻模块的兴奋与抑制，作者称之为"inflate-and-fire"充气-放电机制。将多个 Pneu-ron 以兴奋-抑制环(excitatory-inhibitory ring)互连，可产生稳定、顺序性的振荡，其振荡频率由材料动力学与环境条件共同涌现，而非由计算得出。同时建立动力学模型，给出一个无量纲分岔图(bifurcation diagram)以刻画网络的振荡行为。

Result: Pneu-ron 网络在机械负载与热扰动条件下仍能维持振荡，并通过材料物理特性而非计算实现自适应。作者利用 Pneu-ron 的膨胀驱动软体机器人实现节律性运动。动力学建模给出了决定网络振荡模式的无量纲分岔图。摘要未给出具体数据集、定量指标或对比实验的数值结果。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出将逻辑与驱动编码到材料级模块的新型软体机器人控制架构，实现无电子控制器的自适应节律运动，为分布式、材料驱动的软体机器人提供了新路径。与神经形态/脉冲计算中 LIF 的类比具有概念启发意义，但需注意其并非事件视觉或 SNN 类电子实现，而是物理气动类比。局限在于摘要未提供定量性能评估、可扩展性与模块间耦合精度等方面的验证。

Abstract: Animals coordinate their movements through distributed neural circuits, but soft robots still typically depend on external, centralized electronics for control. Building soft robots that operate without centralized electronic controllers while remaining responsive to their environment remains a frontier challenge in soft robotics. In this work we introduce a soft-robot control architecture inspired by leaky integrate-and-fire models of biological neural circuits. The Pneumatic neuron (Pneu-ron) is a soft actuator that unifies energy conversion, logic, and actuation in one component. Each module combines a low-boiling-point fluid (LBF), a heater, and a mechanical switch into a self-excitable unit. Boiling the LBF inflates the module and triggers excitation and inhibition of adjacent modules in a process we call "inflate-and-fire". When interconnected into excitatory-inhibitory rings, Pneu-rons generate stable, sequential oscillations whose frequency emerges from the material dynamics and environmental conditions. By harnessing the inflation of Pneu-rons for actuation these networks can drive oscillatory locomotion of soft robots. Pneu-ron networks sustain oscillation under mechanical load and thermal variations, adapting through material physics rather than computation. Dynamical modeling of these networks reveals a dimensionless bifurcation diagram that dictates the network's oscillatory behavior. Encoding logic and actuation into material-level modules presents a new avenue for adaptive, electronics controller-free, soft robots.

</details>


### [191] [AnchorVLN: Geometry-Anchored Vision-Language Grounding Reasoning for Open-Vocabulary Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12285)
*Long Giang Vu,Chengkai Yao,Yuxin Liu,FNU Aryan,Rajath Chandrashekar Aralikatti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AnchorVLN 是一个开放词汇的视觉语言导航（VLN）系统，遵循"VLM 提供语义、几何决定度量"的分离原则，并以 EMBODIED-NAV-MCP（一个由 VLM 智能体驱动的 MCP 服务器）形式实现，在 CMU VLN Challenge 2026 的两个任务上进行了基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未见过的室内环境中，智能体需要遵循自然语言指令、定位物体并回答空间问题，且无预建地图或固定物体词表。多模态视觉语言模型（VLM）虽具备强大的开放词汇 grounding 与零样本推理能力，但难以从图像中直接输出可靠的度量量（如距离、方位角、比较性空间关系）。现有方法要么将几何信息硬编码进手工设计流程，要么让模型输出航点（waypoints），这导致针对不同机器人、任务或词表都需要修改控制栈，缺乏通用性与可移植性。

Method: 提出 AnchorVLN，其核心设计规则为：VLM 负责提出语义，几何负责决定度量（the VLM proposes semantics; geometry decides metrics）。系统实现为 EMBODIED-NAV-MCP——一个 Model Context Protocol（MCP）服务器，由 VLM 智能体通过一组紧凑的可调用工具（callable tools）驱动。关键的架构性约束在于：没有任何工具接口以米为单位接受距离或以弧度接受方位角，从而在 schema 层面强制施加"语义—几何"边界，使得下游自主控制栈无需任何修改即可复用。此外引入"控制器建模"（controller modeling）用于指令跟随任务，以及"几何锚定"（geometric anchoring）用于物体参考任务。

Result: 在 CMU Vision-Language Navigation Challenge 2026 的两个任务上进行基准测试：(1) 指令跟随任务，包含 15 个场景上的 30 个问题；(2) 冻结的物体参考集，包含 45 个问题。完整系统在指令跟随任务上达到 64.4%，在移除控制器建模后下降 13.3 个百分点（t = 2.77），表明控制器建模具有统计显著性贡献。在物体参考任务上，几何锚定在 45 个问题中有 10 个达到挑战赛的重叠阈值，而直接坐标估计为 0/45；中位中心误差从 3.37 m 降至 2.48 m。评估指标包括指令跟随准确率、重叠阈值达成数以及中心误差（米）。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出了一种解耦语义与几何的开放词汇 VLN 框架 AnchorVLN，并通过 MCP 工具 schema 强制执行语义—几何边界，从而在不修改下游自主控制栈的前提下提升可移植性；同时验证了几何锚定相较于直接坐标估计在物体参考定位上的显著优势。系统整体性能仍有限（指令跟随 64.4%，物体参考仅 10/45 达标），几何锚定虽改善中位误差但从 3.37 m 到 2.48 m 的绝对精度仍不足以支撑高精度导航，且实验规模较小（30 与 45 个问题），跨机器人、跨环境的泛化能力有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) in unseen indoor environments is useful in real-world robotics, where an agent must follow natural-language instructions, locate objects, and answer spatial questions without a pre-built map or fixed object vocabulary. Multimodal vision-language models (VLMs) provide strong open-vocabulary grounding and zero-shot reasoning, but struggle to emit reliable metric quantities such as range, bearing, and comparative spatial relations directly from images. Existing approaches address this by folding geometry into hand-engineered pipelines or asking models to output waypoints, requiring changes to the control stack for different robots, tasks, or vocabularies. We introduce AnchorVLN, an open-vocabulary VLN system built on a simple rule: the VLM proposes semantics; geometry decides metrics. It is realised as EMBODIED-NAV-MCP, a Model Context Protocol (MCP) server driven by a VLM agent through a compact set of callable tools. Since no tool accepts distance in metres or bearing in radians, the schema enforces the semantic-geometry boundary without modifying the downstream autonomy stack. We benchmark both tasks of the CMU Vision-Language Navigation Challenge 2026: 30 instruction-following questions over 15 scenes and a frozen 45-question object-reference set. The full system achieves 64.4 percent on instruction following, dropping by 13.3 percentage points without controller modeling (t = 2.77). On object reference, geometric anchoring clears the challenge overlap threshold on 10 of 45 questions, versus 0 of 45 for direct coordinate estimation, reducing median center error from 3.37 m to 2.48 m.

</details>


### [192] [Mission Performance: Automatic and Adaptive Race Pace Progression for Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12292)
*Giovanni Lambertini,Matteo Pini,Nicola Musiu,Ayoub Raji,Francesco Iacovacci,Marko Bertogna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种应用于全自动驾驶赛车的"Mission Performance"性能管理模块，通过分赛段自适应调节纵向、横向及综合性能水平，在保证安全的前提下加速圈速收敛，并在A2RL赛事中得到实车验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶赛车需要逼近车辆物理极限以缩短圈速，但这要求对轮胎抓地力有实时准确的估计。实时抓地力估计存在难度大、风险高的问题，若直接将其耦合进运动规划与控制器等关键模块，可能会带来不稳定性与安全隐患。因此，论文的动机是：不通过修改车辆模型参数（如抓地力系数）来影响规划与控制，而是通过"目标性能水平"这一间接、可管理的接口来引导这些模块，从而在提升圈速的同时保证安全。这一思路对高动态、极限工况下的自主系统具有实际意义。

Method: Mission Performance模块的核心机制包括两部分：（1）预设的性能爬升曲线（pre-defined progressions），用于在单次运行开始时对轮胎进行暖胎，使轮胎进入合适的工作温度区间；（2）基于分赛段（per-sector）的在线监测机制，持续跟踪安全指标与车辆动力学指标，并据此对每个赛段自适应地执行"降低/维持/提升"性能水平的决策，逐步收敛到允许的最大性能值。关键设计在于将"性能水平"作为规划器和控制器的目标输入进行调节，而非改动底层车辆模型参数，从而避免了对实时抓地力估计的强依赖。

Result: 该方案在EAV-25（一辆全自动驾驶的Dallara Superformula赛车）上于Yas Marina赛道进行了验证，测试场景为阿布扎比自动驾驶赛车联赛（A2RL）第二赛季。论文报告了该模块在加快圈速进步（laptime progression）方面的有效性与安全性表现。需要说明的是，摘要未给出具体的量化指标（如圈速提升百分比、收敛所需圈数、安全指标阈值等），因此本文的具体数值结论在摘要层面尚不明确。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出并实车验证了一种面向自动驾驶赛车的性能管理层：以安全约束下的自适应性能调节替代对实时抓地力的直接依赖，解耦了关键模块对高风险估计任务的耦合，并在真实赛事环境中证明了有效性。其意义在于为极限驾驶场景下的自主系统提供了一种更稳健的性能与安全平衡策略。潜在局限包括：方法高度依赖预定义的暖胎曲线与分赛段的经验化调节策略，泛化到不同赛道、天气或轮胎条件的能力有待验证；摘要中缺乏定量结果与消融分析，方法对其他车辆平台的迁移性也未充分讨论。

Abstract: In this paper, we describe the Mission Performance module implemented for a fully autonomous racing car to automatically manage the longitudinal, lateral, and combined performances, aiming to speedup the laptime progression while assuring safety. Motivated by the difficulty and risks of applying the real-time estimation of the grip to critical modules like the motion planner and controller, the Mission Performance guides these modules adapting their target performance instead of changing the vehicle model parameters. The module is formed by pre-defined progressions to warm up the tires at the beginning of a run. Then, the system continuously monitors safety and vehicle dynamics metrics on a per-sector basis to adaptively reduce, maintain, or increase the performance levels for each sector, progressively converging toward the maximum allowed value. The solution's effectiveness is demonstrated on the EAV-25, a fully autonomous Dallara Superformula, at the Yas Marina Circuit during the Abu Dhabi Autonomous Racing League (A2RL) Season 2.

</details>


### [193] [DATAFARM: Distribution-Aligned Task and Motion Planning for Fine-Tuning Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12316)
*Samrat Sahoo,Yixuan Huang,Tom Silver*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DATAFARM 通过在任务与运动规划（TAMP）生成的演示轨迹中直接嵌入预训练分布约束，使规划器轨迹与 VLA 模型预训练数据在关节配置、运动风格和时间执行剖面上对齐，从而让 TAMP 成为机器人基础模型微调的有效数据来源。平均成功率达 56.7%，远高于原始 TAMP 的 8.3%，接近人类遥操作的 61.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型（VLA）训练面临高质量数据稀缺的核心瓶颈。TAMP 理论上可规模化生成演示数据，但作者实验发现：原始 TAMP 轨迹虽然能成功完成目标任务，用于微调预训练 VLA 时收益极小。作者将其归因于规划器生成轨迹与 VLA 预训练数据之间存在"行为分布失配"（behavioral distribution mismatch）——即轨迹在动作空间中的统计特性与预训练数据不一致，导致微调无法有效迁移。这一问题的重要性在于：若不能弥合该失配，TAMP 这一可扩展数据源将无法用于机器人基础模型训练。

Method: DATAFARM（Distribution-Aligned Task And motion planning for Fine-tuning A Robot foundation Model）的核心思想是将预训练数据的分布约束直接纳入 TAMP 的轨迹生成过程，而非在生成后再做后处理。具体对齐维度包括三方面：(1) 机器人关节配置（joint configurations）；(2) 运动风格（motion style）；(3) 时间执行剖面（temporal execution profiles）。通过在规划阶段引入这些分布对齐约束，使生成的演示在行为统计特性上与 VLA 预训练数据保持一致，从而降低微调时的分布偏移。论文未在摘要中披露具体的约束建模与优化细节。

Result: 评估涵盖四类任务：三个 TAMP 可执行的桌面操作任务，以及一个超出 TAMP 能力的衣物折叠任务；另在 Deformable Object Manipulation 上测试分布外泛化。主要结果：(1) DATAFARM 平均成功率 56.7%，显著优于原始 TAMP 的 8.3%，并接近人类遥操作的 61.7%；(2) 在超出微调分布的 Deformable Object Manipulation 上，微调后模型保留 85% 成功率，而预训练模型为 90%，说明对齐后的微调未显著损害原有能力。指标为任务成功率。代码与网站已公开。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于揭示并量化了"TAMP 生成数据用于 VLA 微调时因行为分布失配而失效"这一现象，并提出分布对齐的 TAMP 轨迹生成范式，将 TAMP 转变为可扩展的 VLA 微调数据源，性能接近人类遥操作水平，同时在分布外任务上保持较好的能力保留。意义在于为机器人基础模型的数据规模化提供了一条基于规划器的路径。潜在局限：实验任务规模较小（桌面操作与折叠任务），对齐维度是否足以推广到更复杂的全身运动或长时程任务尚不明确；微调后在分布外任务上仍有 5 个百分点的性能下降；摘要未给出对齐方法对规划器求解效率与可行性的影响。

Abstract: Collecting high-quality robot data remains a fundamental challenge for training robot foundation models. Task and motion planning (TAMP) offers a scalable way to generate demonstrations, but our experiments show that raw TAMP trajectories provide surprisingly little benefit when used to fine-tune pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) models, despite successfully solving the target tasks. We hypothesize that this failure arises from a behavioral distribution mismatch between planner-generated trajectories and the data used to pretrain the VLA. To address this mismatch, we introduce DATAFARM: Distribution-Aligned Task And motion planning for Fine-tuning A Robot foundation Model, an approach that incorporates the pretraining distribution directly into TAMP trajectory generation. DATAFARM aligns generated trajectories with the pretraining data in robot joint configurations, motion style, and temporal execution profiles. We evaluate DATAFARM on three tabletop manipulation tasks that TAMP can perform and a cloth-folding task beyond the capability of TAMP. DATAFARM achieves an average success rate of 56.7%, substantially outperforming raw TAMP (8.3%) while approaching human teleoperation (61.7%). On Deformable Object Manipulation, which is outside the fine-tuning distribution, the fine-tuned model retains 85% success, compared with 90% for the pretrained model. These results show that aligning planner-generated demonstrations with the pretraining distribution can make TAMP an effective source of data for VLA fine-tuning. Website and code: https://prpl-group.com/datafarm/

</details>


### [194] [DWMP: Leveraging Dual World Models for Humanoid Obstacle Traversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12347)
*Rongjun Jin,Jianming Ma,Yue Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DWMP（Dual World Model Policy）为类人机器人越障任务提出一种双世界模型策略框架，将本体感知观测与自我中心视觉观测分别用不同的世界模型建模：Koopman 动力学模型将本体状态提升到近似线性的潜空间，RSSM 视觉世界模型将深度观测压缩为紧凑随机隐状态，二者融合后供 student policy 生成动作，并在仿真与 Unitree G1 真机上验证了越障性能与真实部署能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类人机器人在杂乱障碍场中行走需要依赖机载本体感知与视觉观测，但这两类模态特性差异显著：本体感知维度低却由高度非线性的机器人动力学支配，视觉观测维度高、含噪且冗余。现有方法通常将多模态观测混合处理，未显式区分其特性，导致 actor 难以从非线性动力学和高冗余视觉中高效学习可用于控制的有效表征。因此，如何为不同类型观测设计匹配的世界模型表征，成为提升类人机器人越障与真实部署能力的关键问题。

Method: DWMP 为 actor 提供两路互补的世界模型表征。其一为基于 Koopman 的动力学世界模型，将本体感知观测提升（lift）到时间演化近似线性的潜空间，使动力学特征更易于策略学习。其二为基于 RSSM 的视觉世界模型，将自我中心深度观测压缩为紧凑的随机状态，同时保留与障碍相关的几何信息。student policy 接收两路潜表征的融合结果用于动作生成，从而结合线性化本体动力学与压缩视觉感知。整体框架在概念上属于 model-based RL / world model 与多模态表征学习结合的 student-teacher 式策略学习，但摘要未给出具体的损失函数、融合机制与训练流程细节。

Result: 论文在仿真环境以及 Unitree G1 类人机器人真机上开展实验。结果显示 DWMP 在越障任务上的表现优于基线方法，并能在随机化障碍布局条件下支持真实世界部署。摘要未披露具体数据集规模、评价指标（如成功率、通过时间、碰撞率等）、对比基线细节及定量数值，故无法在摘要层面给出更细的性能结论。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：针对类人机器人越障中本体感知与视觉观测特性迥异的问题，提出双世界模型（Koopman 动力学 + RSSM 视觉）表征框架，并通过融合潜状态驱动 student policy，在仿真和真实 Unitree G1 上验证了有效性。其意义在于将不同模态按其内在结构（非线性动力学 vs. 高维冗余视觉）分别建模，提升表征可学习性与真机泛化能力。潜在局限包括：摘要未说明对深度视觉传感器的依赖程度、遮挡/光照等视觉退化场景下的鲁棒性、Koopman 潜空间线性近似的误差上界、真实世界随机化布局的覆盖范围，以及计算开销与实时性表现。

Abstract: Humanoid robots must traverse cluttered obstacle fields using onboard proprioceptive and visual observations, yet existing methods usually process multimodal observations without explicitly considering their different characteristics: proprioceptive observations are low-dimensional but governed by highly nonlinear robot dynamics, while egocentric visual observations are high-dimensional, noisy, and redundant. We propose DWMP (Dual World Model Policy), a framework that provides the actor with separate but complementary world-model representations for humanoid obstacle traversal. A Koopman-based dynamics world model lifts proprioceptive observations into a latent space where their temporal evolution is approximately linear, making the dynamics features easier for the actor to learn from. An RSSM-based visual world model compresses egocentric depth observations into compact stochastic states while preserving obstacle-related geometry. The student policy receives the fused latent representation for action generation, combining linearized proprioceptive dynamics with compressed visual perception. Experiments in simulation and on a Unitree G1 humanoid robot show that DWMP improves obstacle traversal performance over baselines and supports real-world deployment under randomized obstacle layouts.

</details>


### [195] [A Deployable Architecture for Robot-Mediated Tasks (DART): Evaluation in Socially Assistive Robot-Guided Cognitive Behavioral Therapy Exercises](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12349)
*Mina Kian,Lydia Ignatova,Jiong Wang,Ji Min Lee,Jiancheng Li,Qianwei Guo,Emily Weiss,Amy O'Connell,Kaitlin Zareno,Jiani Li,Reyna Patel,Leyaa George,Minyu Huang,Justin Yang,Maja J. Matarić*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DART（Deployable Architecture for Robot-Mediated Tasks）是一种面向低成本社交辅助机器人（SAR）的可部署系统架构，通过Web应用与云基础设施扩展机器人的视觉内容展示、用户输入、远程计算与持久化数据存储能力，并与机器人的物理载体、语音和运动协同工作。作者将其实例化为一个全栈HRI系统，辅助高焦虑水平大学生完成认知行为疗法（CBT）家庭作业，并在103人实验室研究和6周居家部署中验证了其有效性与可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交辅助机器人可支持结构化的健康与福祉干预，但受限于硬件能力与成本，交互复杂度有限，且难以支撑长期真实场景（longitudinal real-world）部署。现有低成本SAR平台（如Blossom）计算、感知与内容呈现能力弱，无法独立完成需要多媒体呈现、语音输入、远程计算与数据持久化的复杂干预任务。因此，需要一种能在不显著提升硬件成本的前提下扩展SAR能力的架构，以支持可长期运行的机器人中介任务。

Method: 论文提出DART架构，将SAR与Web应用及云基础设施耦合，使视觉内容呈现、用户输入采集、远程计算与持久化数据存储与机器人本体的语音和运动协同。作者以参与式设计（participatory design）方式迭代开发了一个面向大学生广泛性焦虑的CBT家庭作业辅助全栈HRI系统，硬件采用低成本开源Blossom机器人平台。系统通过Web前端承载交互内容与输入，云端负责计算与数据存储，机器人端负责语音和动作表达，形成Web-机器人同步的闭环交互；架构设计强调可部署性与任务中介（robot-mediated tasks）的可扩展性。

Result: 评估分两阶段：一是103名参与者的实验室单次会话研究，二是在4名参与者家中进行的六周居家部署。实验室研究显示参与者在会话内压力、状态焦虑（state anxiety）和负性情绪（negative affect）显著下降，系统可用性量表（SUS）平均分为78.89。居家部署中SUS平均分达87.5，定性反馈对可用性评价积极。两组参与者均指出语音输入、视觉呈现以及Web-机器人同步是后续需优先改进的方面。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出并验证了一种将低成本SAR与Web/云基础设施结合的架构，证明其可在实验室单次会话和真实世界长期部署中有效延长机器人中介健康干预的能力，同时保持较高的可用性评分。意义在于为低成本SAR支持结构化、长周期的心理健康干预提供了可复用的架构范式与实证依据。局限包括：居家部署样本量很小（仅4人），缺乏对照组或长期临床效果（如焦虑量表随访）的严格验证，且语音输入、视觉呈现与Web-机器人同步被明确列为待改进项，架构在更广泛SAR平台与任务上的泛化性尚待验证。此外，该工作与事件相机、事件流表示、时序建模、小目标检测、UAV感知或脉冲神经网络等方向无直接关联。

Abstract: Socially assistive robots (SARs) can support structured health and well-being interventions, but hardware and cost constraints limit interaction complexity and longitudinal real-world deployments. We present DART: Deployable Architecture for Robot-Mediated Tasks, an architecture that extends SARs through a web application and cloud infrastructure, enabling visual content, user input, remote computation, and persistent data storage synergistically with the robot's physical embodiment, speech, and movement. We evaluated DART by instantiating it in an interatively-developed full-stack HRI system for helping university students with elevated generalized anxiety to complete cognitive behavioral therapy (CBT) homework exercises. The resulting system, which used the low-cost open-source Blossom robot platform, was refined and evaluated through a participatory design process and multiple user studies, and finally evaluated in an in-lab study with 103 participants, and then a six-week in-home deployment with four participants. In the in-lab evaluation, participants showed significant within-session reductions in stress, state anxiety, and negative affect, and gave the platform a mean System Usability Scale score of 78.89. In the home deployment, the mean System Usability Scale score was 87.5, with positive qualitative feedback on usability. Participants across both groups identified speech input, visual presentation, and web-robot synchronization as priorities for improvement. These findings validate DART as an effective architecture for extending the capabilities of a low-cost SAR in both in-lab single-session and in real-world longitudinal deployments.

</details>


### [196] [READ: Learning Risk-Informed Fields for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12371)
*Zhiyuan Liu,Yuanxin Tian,Zehong Ke,Jinhao Li,Hao Cheng,Zhenhua Xu,Wenhao Yu,Jianqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: README 提出 READ 框架，从几何约束与行为约束中学习一种显式、面向规划的可微风险表征，并将其实例化为连续时空场（spatiotemporal field），使候选轨迹可沿路径进行可微风险查询。该表征可与端到端规划器及 VLA 模型集成，在 NAVSIM 上取得稳定增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶规划不仅需要识别场景中的元素，还需要判断道路结构、周围智能体及其运动状态如何影响未来的驾驶操作。现有基于学习的规划器虽能通过隐式潜特征与轨迹解码器捕捉这些影响，但环境因素与候选动作之间的关系通常是隐式的，难以检查、诊断或细化。经典安全场（safety field）虽然提供显式空间风险表示，但其风险形状与相对权重是预先设定的，无法针对每个场景自适应调整。因此需要一种既能显式表达、又能从数据中自适应学习的规划对齐风险表征。

Method: READ 从互补的几何约束（如道路结构）与行为约束（如周围智能体运动）中学习显式、面向规划的风险表征。该表征被实例化为一个连续时空场，支持沿候选轨迹进行可微查询。学习到的场通过鼓励预测轨迹对齐低风险区域，将场景理解与动作选择连接起来，同时保留用于轨迹评估与细化的可微接口。READ 可同时集成到端到端规划器以及 Vision-Language-Action（VLA）模型中。

Result: 在 NAVSIM 基准上，READ 在匹配的端到端骨干网络上取得一致增益，并在 VLA 设定下表现强劲；在 NAVSIM v2 上也取得有竞争力的结果。实验表明所学的空间风险作为一种显式、可适应的表示，能够提升安全规划的可行性与可解释性。

Conclusion: READ 的主要贡献在于将风险从隐式潜特征或预设安全场，转化为一种可学习、可微、时空连续的显式规划对齐表示，从而提升规划安全性、可解释性与可细化能力，并具备与端到端及 VLA 规划器兼容的良好通用性。潜在局限包括对风险场监督信号与训练数据分布的依赖，以及在不同数据集与场景泛化性、计算开销方面尚需进一步验证（摘要未作详细说明）。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires more than recognizing what is present in a scene: a planner must determine how road structure, surrounding agents, and their motion states should influence a future maneuver. Existing learning-based planners can capture these influences through latent scene features and trajectory decoders, but the relationship between environmental factors and candidate actions often remains implicit. This limits the ability to inspect, diagnose, or refine how scene context affects the safety of a predicted trajectory. Classical safety fields provide an explicit spatial representation of this relationship, but their risk shapes and relative weights are prescribed in advance and do not adapt to each scene. We introduce READ, a framework that learns an explicit, planning-aligned risk representation from complementary geometric and behavioral constraints. READ instantiates this representation as a continuous spatiotemporal field, enabling differentiable queries along candidate trajectories.
